<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

$basePath = __DIR__;
require_once $basePath . '/vendor/autoload.php';

use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;

Config::load($basePath);

$host = Config::get('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1');
$port = (int) Config::get('DB_PORT', 3306);
$database = Config::get('DB_DATABASE', 'kich_ban_sat_v3');
$username = Config::get('DB_USERNAME', 'root');
$password = Config::get('DB_PASSWORD', '');
$charset = Config::get('DB_CHARSET', 'utf8mb4');

return [
    'paths' => [
        'migrations' => '%%PHINX_CONFIG_DIR%%/database/migrations',
        'seeds' => '%%PHINX_CONFIG_DIR%%/database/seeders',
    ],
    'environments' => [
        'default_migration_table' => 'phinxlog',
        'default_environment' => 'development',
        'development' => [
            'adapter' => 'mysql',
            'host' => $host,
            'name' => $database,
            'user' => $username,
            'pass' => $password,
            'port' => $port,
            'charset' => $charset,
            'collation' => 'utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci',

            'adapter' => 'mysql',
            'host' => $host,
            'name' => Config::get('DB_TEST_DATABASE', 'kich_ban_sat_v3_test'),
            'user' => $username,
            'pass' => $password,
            'port' => $port,
            'charset' => $charset,
            'collation' => 'utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci',
        ],
    ],
    'version_order' => 'creation',
];

FILE: backend\app\Core\Application.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core;

use App\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler;
use App\Core\Middleware\MiddlewareInterface;
use Throwable;

class Application
{
    protected Router $router;
    /** @var MiddlewareInterface[] */
    protected array $middleware = [];
    protected string $basePath;

    public function __construct(string $basePath)
    {
        $this->basePath = rtrim($basePath, '/\\');
        $this->router = new Router();
    }

    public function getRouter(): Router
    {
        return $this->router;
    }

    public function addMiddleware(MiddlewareInterface $middleware): self
    {
        $this->middleware[] = $middleware;
        return $this;
    }

    public function getBasePath(): string
    {
        return $this->basePath;
    }

    /**
     * Dispatch an incoming Request through middleware pipeline and Router.
     */
    public function dispatch(Request $request): Response
    {
        try {
            return $this->runPipeline($request);
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            return ExceptionHandler::handle($e, $request);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Execute the middleware pipeline and resolve the route handler.
     */
    protected function runPipeline(Request $request): Response
    {
        $middlewareStack = $this->middleware;

        $corePipeline = function (Request $req): Response {
            $match = $this->router->match($req);
            $handler = $match['handler'];
            $params = $match['params'];

            return $this->invokeHandler($handler, $req, $params);
        };

        $pipeline = array_reduce(
            array_reverse($middlewareStack),
            function (callable $next, MiddlewareInterface $middleware) {
                return function (Request $req) use ($middleware, $next): Response {
                    return $middleware->handle($req, $next);
                };
            },
            $corePipeline
        );

        return $pipeline($request);
    }

    /**
     * Invoke the matched route handler.
     *
     * @param callable|array{0: class-string, 1: string} $handler
     * @param array<string, string> $params
     */
    protected function invokeHandler(callable|array $handler, Request $request, array $params): Response
    {
        if (is_array($handler) && is_string($handler[0])) {
            $class = $handler[0];
            $method = $handler[1];
            $instance = new $class();
            $result = $instance->$method($request, ...array_values($params));
        } else {
            $result = call_user_func($handler, $request, ...array_values($params));
        }

        if ($result instanceof Response) {
            return $result;
        }

        if (is_array($result)) {
            return Response::json($result);
        }

        if (is_string($result)) {
            return new Response($result);
        }

        return Response::json($result);
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Core\Request.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core;

use App\Core\Exceptions\BadRequestException;

class Request
{
    protected string $method;
    protected string $path;
    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    protected array $queryParams;
    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    protected array $bodyParams;
    /** @var array<string, string> */
    protected array $routeParams = [];
    /** @var array<string, string> */
    protected array $headers;
    protected string $rawBody;

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $queryParams
     * @param array<string, mixed> $bodyParams
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     */
    public function __construct(
        string $method = 'GET',
        string $path = '/',
        array $queryParams = [],
        array $bodyParams = [],
        array $headers = [],
        string $rawBody = ''
    ) {
        $this->method = strtoupper(trim($method));
        $this->path = self::normalizePath($path);
        $this->queryParams = $queryParams;
        $this->bodyParams = $bodyParams;
        $this->headers = self::normalizeHeaders($headers);
        $this->rawBody = $rawBody;
    }

    /**
     * Parse raw JSON payload into an array of parameters.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     * @throws BadRequestException
     */
    public static function parseJsonPayload(string $rawBody): array
    {
        if (trim($rawBody) === '') {
            return [];
        }

        $decoded = json_decode($rawBody, true);
        if (json_last_error() !== JSON_ERROR_NONE) {
            throw new BadRequestException('Malformed JSON in request payload: ' . json_last_error_msg());
        }

        if (!is_array($decoded)) {
            throw new BadRequestException('Malformed JSON in request payload: Payload must be a JSON object or array');
        }

        return $decoded;
    }

    /**
     * Capture the current HTTP request from superglobals and input stream.
     */
    public static function capture(?string $rawBody = null): self
    {
        $server = $_SERVER;
        $method = $server['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET';
        $uri = $server['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/';

        $parsedUrl = parse_url($uri, PHP_URL_PATH);
        $path = is_string($parsedUrl) ? $parsedUrl : '/';

        $headers = [];
        if (function_exists('getallheaders')) {
            $rawHeaders = getallheaders();
            if (is_array($rawHeaders)) {
                $headers = $rawHeaders;
            }
        } else {
            foreach ($server as $key => $value) {
                if (str_starts_with($key, 'HTTP_')) {
                    $headerName = str_replace('_', '-', substr($key, 5));
                    $headers[$headerName] = (string) $value;
                } elseif (in_array($key, ['CONTENT_TYPE', 'CONTENT_LENGTH', 'CONTENT_MD5'], true)) {
                    $headerName = str_replace('_', '-', $key);
                    $headers[$headerName] = (string) $value;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($rawBody === null) {
            $input = file_get_contents('php://input');
            $rawBody = $input !== false ? $input : '';
        }

        // Case-insensitive Content-Type detection across server vars and headers
        $contentType = $server['CONTENT_TYPE'] ?? $server['HTTP_CONTENT_TYPE'] ?? '';
        if ($contentType === '') {
            foreach ($headers as $k => $v) {
                if (strcasecmp($k, 'Content-Type') === 0) {
                    $contentType = (string) $v;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        $bodyParams = [];

        if (str_contains(strtolower($contentType), 'application/json')) {
            $bodyParams = self::parseJsonPayload($rawBody);
        } elseif (!empty($_POST)) {
            $bodyParams = $_POST;
        }

        return new self($method, $path, $_GET, $bodyParams, $headers, $rawBody);
    }

    public static function normalizePath(string $path): string
    {
        $trimmed = '/' . trim($path, '/');
        return $trimmed === '' ? '/' : $trimmed;
    }

    public function getMethod(): string
    {
        return $this->method;
    }

    public function getPath(): string
    {
        return $this->path;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getQueryParams(): array
    {
        return $this->queryParams;
    }

    public function query(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->queryParams[$key] ?? $default;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getBodyParams(): array
    {
        return $this->bodyParams;
    }

    public function input(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->bodyParams[$key] ?? $this->queryParams[$key] ?? $default;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function all(): array
    {
        return array_merge($this->queryParams, $this->bodyParams);
    }

    public function getRawBody(): string
    {
        return $this->rawBody;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getHeaders(): array
    {
        return $this->headers;
    }

    public function getHeader(string $name, ?string $default = null): ?string
    {
        $lower = strtolower($name);
        return $this->headers[$lower] ?? $default;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, string> $params
     */
    public function setRouteParams(array $params): void
    {
        $this->routeParams = $params;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getRouteParams(): array
    {
        return $this->routeParams;
    }

    public function param(string $key, ?string $default = null): ?string
    {
        return $this->routeParams[$key] ?? $default;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    private static function normalizeHeaders(array $headers): array
    {
        $normalized = [];
        foreach ($headers as $key => $value) {
            $normalized[strtolower((string) $key)] = (string) $value;
        }
        return $normalized;
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Response.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core;

class Response
{
    protected int $statusCode;
    /** @var array<string, string> */
    protected array $headers;
    protected string $content;

    /**
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     */
    public function __construct(string $content = '', int $statusCode = 200, array $headers = [])
    {
        $this->content = $content;
        $this->statusCode = $statusCode;
        $this->headers = array_merge([
            'Content-Type' => 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
            'X-Content-Type-Options' => 'nosniff',
            'X-Frame-Options' => 'DENY',
            'X-XSS-Protection' => '1; mode=block',
            'Referrer-Policy' => 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin',
        ], $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Create a standard JSON response.
     *
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     */
    public static function json(mixed $data, int $statusCode = 200, array $headers = []): self
    {
        $encoded = json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);
        if ($encoded === false) {
            $encoded = json_encode([
                'success' => false,
                'message' => 'Failed to encode JSON response',
                'data' => null,
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'JSON_ENCODE_ERROR',
                    'message' => 'Failed to encode JSON response',
                    'details' => null,
                ],
                'meta' => null,
            ]);
            $statusCode = 500;
        }

        return new self($encoded, $statusCode, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Standard Success Response Envelope (Kịch Bản Sát V16 Convention).
     */
    public static function success(
        mixed $data = null,
        string $message = 'Request completed',
        mixed $meta = null,
        int $statusCode = 200,
        array $headers = []
    ): self {
        return self::json([
            'success' => true,
            'message' => $message,
            'data' => $data,
            'error' => null,
            'meta' => $meta,
        ], $statusCode, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Standard Paginated Response Envelope (Kịch Bản Sát V16 Convention).
     */
    public static function paginated(
        array $items,
        int $page,
        int $perPage,
        int $total,
        string $message = 'Retrieved successfully',
        array $headers = []
    ): self {
        $totalPages = $perPage > 0 ? (int) ceil($total / $perPage) : 1;

        return self::json([
            'success' => true,
            'message' => $message,
            'data' => $items,
            'error' => null,
            'meta' => [
                'pagination' => [
                    'page' => $page,
                    'per_page' => $perPage,
                    'total' => $total,
                    'total_pages' => $totalPages,
                ],
            ],
        ], 200, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Standard Error Response Envelope (Kịch Bản Sát V16 Convention).
     */
    public static function fail(
        string $message,
        string $code = 'BAD_REQUEST',
        int $statusCode = 400,
        ?array $details = null,
        mixed $meta = null,
        array $headers = []
    ): self {
        $errorObj = [
            'code' => $code,
            'message' => $message,
        ];
        if ($details !== null) {
            $errorObj['details'] = $details;
        }

        return self::json([
            'success' => false,
            'message' => $message,
            'data' => null,
            'error' => $errorObj,
            'meta' => $meta,
        ], $statusCode, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Backward-compatible structured error response.
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $errors
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     */
    public static function error(
        string $message,
        int $statusCode = 400,
        array $errors = [],
        ?string $code = null,
        array $headers = []
    ): self {
        $errCode = $code ?? self::getDefaultErrorCode($statusCode);
        $details = !empty($errors) ? $errors : null;

        return self::fail($message, $errCode, $statusCode, $details, null, $headers);
    }

    public function getStatusCode(): int
    {
        return $this->statusCode;
    }

    public function getContent(): string
    {
        return $this->content;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getHeaders(): array
    {
        return $this->headers;
    }

    public function withHeader(string $name, string $value): self
    {
        $this->headers[$name] = $value;
        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Send headers and body to HTTP client.
     */
    public function send(): void
    {
        if (!headers_sent()) {
            http_response_code($this->statusCode);
            foreach ($this->headers as $name => $value) {
                header("{$name}: {$value}");
            }
        }

        echo $this->content;
    }

    private static function getDefaultErrorCode(int $statusCode): string
    {
        return match ($statusCode) {
            400 => 'BAD_REQUEST',
            401 => 'UNAUTHORIZED',
            403 => 'FORBIDDEN',
            404 => 'NOT_FOUND',
            405 => 'METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED',
            409 => 'CONFLICT',
            422 => 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
            429 => 'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS',
            default => 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR',
        };
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Router.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core;

use App\Core\Exceptions\MethodNotAllowedException;
use App\Core\Exceptions\NotFoundException;

class Router
{
    /**
     * @var array<int, array{
     *     method: string,
     *     path: string,
     *     regex: string,
     *     paramNames: string[],
     *     handler: callable|array{0: class-string, 1: string}
     * }>
     */
    protected array $routes = [];

    public function addRoute(string $method, string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        $normalizedPath = Request::normalizePath($path);
        [$regex, $paramNames] = self::compileRoute($normalizedPath);

        $this->routes[] = [
            'method' => strtoupper($method),
            'path' => $normalizedPath,
            'regex' => $regex,
            'paramNames' => $paramNames,
            'handler' => $handler,
        ];

        return $this;
    }

    public function get(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('GET', $path, $handler);
    }

    public function post(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('POST', $path, $handler);
    }

    public function put(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('PUT', $path, $handler);
    }

    public function patch(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('PATCH', $path, $handler);
    }

    public function delete(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('DELETE', $path, $handler);
    }

    public function options(string $path, callable|array $handler): self
    {
        return $this->addRoute('OPTIONS', $path, $handler);
    }

    /**
     * Match a request against registered routes.
     *
     * @throws NotFoundException
     * @throws MethodNotAllowedException
     * @return array{handler: callable|array, params: array<string, string>}
     */
    public function match(Request $request): array
    {
        $requestMethod = $request->getMethod();
        $requestPath = $request->getPath();

        $matchingPathFound = false;
        $allowedMethods = [];

        foreach ($this->routes as $route) {
            if (preg_match($route['regex'], $requestPath, $matches)) {
                $matchingPathFound = true;
                $allowedMethods[] = $route['method'];

                if ($route['method'] === $requestMethod) {
                    $params = [];
                    foreach ($route['paramNames'] as $paramName) {
                        if (isset($matches[$paramName])) {
                            $params[$paramName] = $matches[$paramName];
                        }
                    }

                    $request->setRouteParams($params);

                    return [
                        'handler' => $route['handler'],
                        'params' => $params,
                    ];
                }
            }
        }

        if ($matchingPathFound) {
            $uniqueMethods = array_values(array_unique($allowedMethods));
            throw new MethodNotAllowedException(
                $uniqueMethods,
                "HTTP method {$requestMethod} is not supported for path {$requestPath}"
            );
        }

        throw new NotFoundException("Route not found: {$requestMethod} {$requestPath}");
    }

    /**
     * Compile a path with parameters (e.g. /games/{id}) into a regex.
     *
     * @return array{0: string, 1: string[]}
     */
    protected static function compileRoute(string $path): array
    {
        $paramNames = [];
        $pattern = preg_replace_callback('/\{([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\}/', function ($matches) use (&$paramNames) {
            $paramNames[] = $matches[1];
            return '(?P<' . $matches[1] . '>[^/]+)';
        }, $path);

        $regex = '#^' . $pattern . '$#u';

        return [$regex, $paramNames];
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Core\Exceptions\BadRequestException.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Exceptions;

class BadRequestException extends HttpException
{
    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    protected array $errors;

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $errors
     */
    public function __construct(string $message = 'Bad request', array $errors = [], ?\Throwable $previous = null)
    {
        parent::__construct($message, 400, [], $previous);
        $this->errors = $errors;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getErrors(): array
    {
        return $this->errors;
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Exceptions;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use Throwable;

class ExceptionHandler
{
    /** @var (callable(string): void)|null */
    protected static $logger = null;

    /**
     * Set a custom logger callback (useful for testing or custom log channels).
     *
     * @param (callable(string): void)|null $logger
     */
    public static function setLogger(?callable $logger): void
    {
        self::$logger = $logger;
    }

    /**
     * Convert an uncaught Throwable into a clean JSON Response.
     */
    public static function handle(Throwable $e, ?Request $request = null): Response
    {
        $statusCode = 500;
        $headers = [];
        $errors = [];
        $errorCode = 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR';

        if ($e instanceof HttpException) {
            $statusCode = $e->getStatusCode();
            $headers = $e->getHeaders();
            $message = $e->getMessage();
            $errorCode = null;

            if ($e instanceof BadRequestException) {
                $errorCode = 'BAD_REQUEST';
                $errors = $e->getErrors();
            } elseif ($e instanceof NotFoundException) {
                $errorCode = 'NOT_FOUND';
            } elseif ($e instanceof MethodNotAllowedException) {
                $errorCode = 'METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED';
            }
        } else {
            $isDebug = (bool) Config::get('APP_DEBUG', false);
            if ($isDebug) {
                $message = self::sanitize($e->getMessage());
                $errors = [
                    'exception' => get_class($e),
                    'file' => $e->getFile(),
                    'line' => $e->getLine(),
                ];
            } else {
                $message = 'Internal Server Error';
            }

            self::logError($e);
        }

        return Response::error($message, $statusCode, $errors, $errorCode, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Format a safe log message with structured metadata and sanitized secrets across the entire exception chain.
     */
    public static function formatLogMessage(Throwable $e): string
    {
        $frames = [];
        $current = $e;

        while ($current !== null) {
            $safeMessage = self::sanitize($current->getMessage());
            $safeTrace = self::sanitize($current->getTraceAsString());

            $frames[] = sprintf(
                "[%s] %s: %s in %s:%d\nTrace:\n%s",
                date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
                get_class($current),
                $safeMessage,
                $current->getFile(),
                $current->getLine(),
                $safeTrace
            );

            $current = $current->getPrevious();
        }

        return implode("\nCaused by: ", $frames) . "\n";
    }

    /**
     * Sanitize sensitive keywords and patterns from string output.
     */
    public static function sanitize(string $input): string
    {
        // 1. Redact URI/DSN passwords: scheme://user:password@host
        $sanitized = preg_replace(
            '#://([^:]+):([^@]+)@#',
            '://$1:***@',
            $input
        );
        if (!is_string($sanitized)) {
            $sanitized = $input;
        }

        // 2. Redact Authorization headers: Authorization: Bearer <token> or Basic <token>
        $sanitized = preg_replace(
            '/((?:authorization|proxy-authorization)\s*:\s*(?:bearer|basic|token)\s+)[^\s,;\r\n]+/i',
            '$1***',
            $sanitized
        );
        if (!is_string($sanitized)) {
            $sanitized = $input;
        }

        // 3. Redact URL query parameters: ?token=xyz or &password=xyz or &key=xyz
        $sanitized = preg_replace(
            '/([?&](?:password|pwd|token|client_secret|secret|api_?key|key|access_token|refresh_token|auth)=)[^&\s#]+/i',
            '$1***',
            $sanitized
        );
        if (!is_string($sanitized)) {
            $sanitized = $input;
        }

        // 4. Redact key-value pairs (JSON quoted, array dumps, key=val, key: val, or key val)
        // Matches:
        // - Prefix: optional [ or [' or [" then keyword then optional ] or '] or "]
        // - Separator: => or : or is or = or whitespace
        // - Value: single-quoted string, double-quoted string, or unquoted value up to delimiter/whitespace
        $pattern = '/(?P<prefix>\[?(?:\'|")?\b(?:password|pwd|token|client_secret|secret|api_?key|access_token|refresh_token|auth|credential)\b(?:\'|")?\]?)(?P<sep>\s*(?:=>|:|is|=|\s+)\s*)(?P<val>\'[^\']*\'|"[^"]*"|(?!(?:password|pwd|token|client_secret|secret|api_?key|access_token|refresh_token|auth|credential)\s*(?:=|:|=>))[^\s,\r\n]+)/i';
        $sanitized = preg_replace_callback($pattern, function (array $m): string {
            $val = $m['val'];
            // If already redacted, avoid altering
            if ($val === '***' || $val === '"***"' || $val === "'***'" || str_starts_with($val, '***')) {
                return $m[0];
            }

            if (str_starts_with($val, '"') && str_ends_with($val, '"')) {
                $replacementVal = '"***"';
            } elseif (str_starts_with($val, "'") && str_ends_with($val, "'")) {
                $replacementVal = "'***'";
            } else {
                $replacementVal = '***';
            }

            return $m['prefix'] . $m['sep'] . $replacementVal;
        }, $sanitized);

        if (!is_string($sanitized)) {
            $sanitized = $input;
        }

        return $sanitized;
    }

    /**
     * Log unexpected server errors safely.
     */
    protected static function logError(Throwable $e): void
    {
        $logMessage = self::formatLogMessage($e);

        if (self::$logger !== null) {
            (self::$logger)($logMessage);
        } else {
            error_log($logMessage);
        }
    }
}

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Exceptions;

class HttpException extends \Exception
{
    protected int $statusCode;
    /** @var array<string, string> */
    protected array $headers;

    /**
     * @param array<string, string> $headers
     */
    public function __construct(string $message, int $statusCode = 500, array $headers = [], ?\Throwable $previous = null)
    {
        parent::__construct($message, $statusCode, $previous);
        $this->statusCode = $statusCode;
        $this->headers = $headers;
    }

    public function getStatusCode(): int
    {
        return $this->statusCode;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getHeaders(): array
    {
        return $this->headers;
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Exceptions;

class MethodNotAllowedException extends HttpException
{
    /**
     * @param string[] $allowedMethods
     */
    public function __construct(array $allowedMethods = [], string $message = 'Method not allowed', ?\Throwable $previous = null)
    {
        $headers = [];
        if (!empty($allowedMethods)) {
            $headers['Allow'] = implode(', ', $allowedMethods);
        }
        parent::__construct($message, 405, $headers, $previous);
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Middleware;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Identity\Repositories\UserRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;

class AuthMiddleware implements MiddlewareInterface
{
    /** @var string[] */
    protected array $allowedRoles;
    protected UserRepository $userRepository;

    /**
     * @param string[] $allowedRoles
     */
    public function __construct(array $allowedRoles = [], ?UserRepository $userRepository = null)
    {
        $this->allowedRoles = $allowedRoles;
        $this->userRepository = $userRepository ?? new UserRepository(DatabaseConnection::getInstance());
    }

    /**
     * Authenticate request and check role permissions.
     * Returns the user array on success, or a Response (401/403) on failure.
     *
     * @param string[] $allowedRoles
     * @return array<string, mixed>|Response
     */
    public static function authenticate(Request $request, array $allowedRoles = []): array|Response
    {
        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }

        $userId = null;
        if (!empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
            $userId = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
        }

        // Support Bearer token header in API/Testing environment (e.g. "Bearer {user_id}")
        if ($userId === null && Config::get('APP_ENV') === 'testing') {
            $authHeader = $request->getHeader('authorization');
            if ($authHeader !== null && preg_match('/Bearer\s+(\d+)/i', $authHeader, $matches)) {
                $userId = (int) $matches[1];
            }
        }

        if ($userId === null || $userId <= 0) {
            return Response::fail(
                'Unauthorized - Please log in to continue',
                'UNAUTHORIZED',
                401
            );
        }

        $userRepo = new UserRepository(DatabaseConnection::getInstance());
        $user = $userRepo->findById($userId);

        if ($user === null) {
            return Response::fail(
                'User not found or account deactivated',
                'UNAUTHORIZED',
                401
            );
        }

        if (($user['status'] ?? 'ACTIVE') !== 'ACTIVE') {
            return Response::fail(
                'Account is suspended or inactive',
                'FORBIDDEN',
                403
            );
        }

        // RBAC check
        if (!empty($allowedRoles)) {
            $userRoleCode = strtoupper((string) ($user['role_code'] ?? 'PLAYER'));
            $allowedUpper = array_map('strtoupper', $allowedRoles);

            if (!in_array($userRoleCode, $allowedUpper, true)) {
                return Response::fail(
                    'Forbidden - You do not have permission to access this resource',
                    'FORBIDDEN',
                    403
                );
            }
        }

        return $user;
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, callable $next): Response
    {
        $result = self::authenticate($request, $this->allowedRoles);
        if ($result instanceof Response) {
            return $result;
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Middleware\CorsMiddleware.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Middleware;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;

class CorsMiddleware implements MiddlewareInterface
{
    /** @var string[] */
    protected array $allowedOrigins;
    protected bool $supportsCredentials;

    /**
     * @param string[]|null $allowedOrigins
     * @param bool|null $supportsCredentials
     */
    public function __construct(?array $allowedOrigins = null, ?bool $supportsCredentials = null)
    {
        if ($allowedOrigins !== null) {
            $this->allowedOrigins = array_values(array_filter(array_map('trim', $allowedOrigins)));
        } else {
            $rawOrigins = Config::get('ALLOWED_ORIGINS', 'http://127.0.0.1:3000,http://localhost:3000');
            $this->allowedOrigins = array_values(array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', (string) $rawOrigins))));
        }

        if ($supportsCredentials !== null) {
            $this->supportsCredentials = $supportsCredentials;
        } else {
            $this->supportsCredentials = (bool) Config::get('CORS_SUPPORTS_CREDENTIALS', true);
        }
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, callable $next): Response
    {
        $origin = $request->getHeader('origin');
        $isWildcard = in_array('*', $this->allowedOrigins, true);

        // Security requirement: If wildcard is present, credentials MUST NOT be enabled
        $allowCredentials = $this->supportsCredentials && !$isWildcard;

        $isAllowed = false;
        $matchedOrigin = '';

        if ($origin !== null && $origin !== '') {
            if ($isWildcard) {
                // Literal wildcard: never reflect dynamic origin with credentials
                $isAllowed = true;
                $matchedOrigin = '*';
            } else {
                foreach ($this->allowedOrigins as $allowed) {
                    if (strcasecmp($allowed, $origin) === 0) {
                        $isAllowed = true;
                        $matchedOrigin = $origin;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Handle preflight OPTIONS request
        if ($request->getMethod() === 'OPTIONS') {
            $headers = [
                'Access-Control-Allow-Methods' => 'GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS',
                'Access-Control-Allow-Headers' => 'Content-Type, Authorization, X-Requested-With, Accept, X-CSRF-Token, X-Idempotency-Key, Idempotency-Key',
                'Access-Control-Max-Age' => '86400',
            ];

            if ($isAllowed) {
                $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin'] = $matchedOrigin;
                if ($allowCredentials) {
                    $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Credentials'] = 'true';
                }
            }

            $response = new Response('', 204, $headers);
            // RFC 7234: If the response representation varies by Origin allowlist,
            // Vary: Origin must be set consistently on all responses to protect HTTP caches.
            if (!$isWildcard) {
                $response = $this->appendVaryHeader($response, 'Origin');
            }

            return $response;
        }

        /** @var Response $response */
        $response = $next($request);

        // For non-wildcard configurations, response headers depend on the Origin header.
        // Therefore, Vary: Origin MUST be set on all responses (including requests without Origin,
        // or with disallowed origins) so intermediate caches do not serve an origin-specific response
        // to an origin-less or different-origin request.
        if (!$isWildcard) {
            $response = $this->appendVaryHeader($response, 'Origin');
        }

        if ($isAllowed) {
            $response->withHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', $matchedOrigin);
            if ($allowCredentials) {
                $response->withHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', 'true');
            }
        }

        return $response;
    }

    /**
     * Safely append a field to the Vary header, preserving any existing values.
     */
    protected function appendVaryHeader(Response $response, string $field): Response
    {
        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $existingVary = null;

        foreach ($headers as $name => $value) {
            if (strcasecmp($name, 'Vary') === 0) {
                $existingVary = $value;
                break;
            }
        }

        if ($existingVary === null || trim($existingVary) === '') {
            return $response->withHeader('Vary', $field);
        }

        $parts = array_map('trim', explode(',', $existingVary));
        foreach ($parts as $part) {
            if (strcasecmp($part, $field) === 0) {
                return $response;
            }
        }

        return $response->withHeader('Vary', $existingVary . ', ' . $field);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Middleware\CsrfMiddleware.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Middleware;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;

class CsrfMiddleware implements MiddlewareInterface
{
    /** @var string[] */
    protected array $excludedPaths = [
        '/api/v1/auth/csrf-token',
        '/api/auth/csrf-token',
        '/api/health',
    ];

    /**
     * Get or initialize CSRF token for the active session.
     */
    public static function getToken(): string
    {
        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            session_start();
        }

        if (empty($_SESSION['_csrf_token'])) {
            $_SESSION['_csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
        }

        return (string) $_SESSION['_csrf_token'];
    }

    /**
     * Verify whether a submitted token matches session CSRF token.
     */
    public static function verifyToken(?string $submittedToken): bool
    {
        if ($submittedToken === null || $submittedToken === '') {
            return false;
        }

        $sessionToken = self::getToken();
        return hash_equals($sessionToken, $submittedToken);
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, callable $next): Response
    {
        $method = $request->getMethod();

        // Safe methods (GET, HEAD, OPTIONS) do not require CSRF validation
        if (in_array($method, ['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'], true)) {
            return $next($request);
        }

        // Skip in testing environment or test header
        $isTesting = Config::get('APP_ENV') === 'testing';
        if ($isTesting) {
            return $next($request);
        }

        $path = $request->getPath();
        foreach ($this->excludedPaths as $excluded) {
            if (str_starts_with($path, $excluded)) {
                return $next($request);
            }
        }

        $token = $request->getHeader('x-csrf-token') ?? $request->input('_csrf_token');
        if (!is_string($token) || !self::verifyToken($token)) {
            return Response::fail(
                'Invalid or missing CSRF token',
                'CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID',
                403
            );
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Middleware;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;

interface MiddlewareInterface
{
    /**
     * Process an incoming request.
     *
     * @param callable(Request): Response $next
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, callable $next): Response;
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Core\Middleware\RateLimitMiddleware.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Core\Middleware;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;

class RateLimitMiddleware implements MiddlewareInterface
{
    /** @var string[] */
    protected array $limitedPaths = [
        '/api/v1/auth/login',
        '/api/auth/login',
        '/api/v1/auth/register',
        '/api/auth/register',
    ];

    public function __construct(
        protected int $maxAttempts = 10,
        protected int $decaySeconds = 60
    ) {
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, callable $next): Response
    {
        if (Config::get('APP_ENV') === 'testing' || $request->getMethod() !== 'POST') {
            return $next($request);
        }

        $path = $request->getPath();
        if (!in_array($path, $this->limitedPaths, true)) {
            return $next($request);
        }

        $ip = $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '127.0.0.1';
        $key = hash('sha256', $ip . '|' . $path);
        $dir = dirname(__DIR__, 3) . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR . 'storage' . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR . 'rate_limits';
        if (!is_dir($dir)) {
            @mkdir($dir, 0775, true);
        }

        $file = $dir . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR . $key . '.json';
        $now = time();
        $timestamps = [];

        if (is_file($file)) {
            $raw = @file_get_contents($file);
            $decoded = is_string($raw) ? json_decode($raw, true) : null;
            if (is_array($decoded)) {
                $timestamps = array_values(array_filter(
                    $decoded,
                    fn ($ts) => is_int($ts) && ($now - $ts) < $this->decaySeconds
                ));
            }
        }

        if (count($timestamps) >= $this->maxAttempts) {
            return Response::fail(
                'Bạn thao tác quá nhanh. Vui lòng thử lại sau ít phút.',
                'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS',
                429,
                ['retry_after_seconds' => $this->decaySeconds]
            );
        }

        $timestamps[] = $now;
        @file_put_contents($file, json_encode($timestamps), LOCK_EX);

        return $next($request);
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Booking\Repositories\BookingRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Booking\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class BookingRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find session by primary key ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findSessionById(int $sessionId): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($sessionId): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`,
                        `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, CONCAT(\'SESS_\', `id`) AS `session_code`,
                        DATE(`start_at`) AS `scheduled_date`, TIME(`start_at`) AS `start_time`, TIME(`end_at`) AS `end_time`,
                        `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `started_at` AS `actual_start_time`,
                        `completed_at` AS `actual_end_time`, `cancel_reason` AS `cancellation_reason`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `sessions`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $sessionId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find active session for a game room.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findActiveSessionByGameRoomId(int $gameRoomId): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`,
                        `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, CONCAT(\'SESS_\', `id`) AS `session_code`,
                        DATE(`start_at`) AS `scheduled_date`, TIME(`start_at`) AS `start_time`, TIME(`end_at`) AS `end_time`,
                        `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `cancel_reason` AS `cancellation_reason`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `sessions`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :game_room_id
                   AND `status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\')
                 ORDER BY `id` DESC
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Check if a physical room is already booked in an overlapping time window.
     */
    public function isPhysicalRoomBooked(
        int $physicalRoomId,
        string $reservationDate,
        string $startTime,
        string $endTime,
        ?int $excludeReservationId = null
    ): bool {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($physicalRoomId, $reservationDate, $startTime, $endTime, $excludeReservationId): bool {
            $startAt = $reservationDate . ' ' . $startTime;
            $endAt = $reservationDate . ' ' . $endTime;

            $sql = 'SELECT COUNT(1)
                    FROM `sessions`
                    WHERE `physical_room_id` = :physical_room_id
                      AND `status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\')
                      AND (`start_at` < :end_at AND `end_at` > :start_at)';

            if ($excludeReservationId !== null) {
                $sql .= ' AND `id` != :exclude_id';
            }

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bindValue(':physical_room_id', $physicalRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);

            if ($excludeReservationId !== null) {
                $stmt->bindValue(':exclude_id', $excludeReservationId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            }

            $stmt->execute();
            return (int) $stmt->fetchColumn() > 0;
        });
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\DMManagement\Controllers\DMAssignmentController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\DMManagement\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\DMManagement\Repositories\DMRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class DMAssignmentController
{
    protected DMRepository $dmRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?DMRepository $dmRepo = null, ?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->dmRepo = $dmRepo ?? new DMRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/dm/assignments
     * Query: status, dm_id (admin only)
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['DM', 'ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));
        $targetDmId = (int) $auth['id'];

        if ($userRole === 'ADMIN') {
            $queryDmId = $request->query('dm_id');
            $targetDmId = $queryDmId !== null && (int) $queryDmId > 0 ? (int) $queryDmId : null;
        }

        $status = $request->query('status') !== null ? (string) $request->query('status') : null;

        $assignments = $this->dmRepo->listAssignments($targetDmId, $status);

        return Response::success([
            'assignments' => $assignments,
            'count' => count($assignments),
        ], 'Danh sách phân công quản trò');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/dm/assignments/{id}/accept
     */
    public function accept(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['DM', 'ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $assignmentId = (int) $id;
        $assignment = $this->dmRepo->findAssignmentById($assignmentId);

        if ($assignment === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy ca làm việc yêu cầu', 'ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));

        // Chống IDOR: Chỉ DM được gán cho ca này hoặc Admin mới có quyền accept
        if ($userRole !== 'ADMIN' && (int) $assignment['dm_user_id'] !== (int) $auth['id']) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không có quyền phản hồi ca làm việc của người khác', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        if ($assignment['status'] !== 'PENDING') {
            return Response::fail('Ca làm việc này đã được xử lý trước đó (Trạng thái: ' . $assignment['status'] . ')', 'INVALID_STATUS', 400);
        }

        $this->dmRepo->respondAssignment($assignmentId, 'ACCEPTED');

        $updated = $this->dmRepo->findAssignmentById($assignmentId);

        return Response::success([
            'assignment' => $updated,
        ], 'Đã chấp nhận ca làm việc thành công');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/dm/assignments/{id}/reject
     * Body: reason (string, required)
     */
    public function reject(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['DM', 'ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $assignmentId = (int) $id;
        $assignment = $this->dmRepo->findAssignmentById($assignmentId);

        if ($assignment === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy ca làm việc yêu cầu', 'ASSIGNMENT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));

        // Chống IDOR: Chỉ DM được gán hoặc Admin mới có quyền reject
        if ($userRole !== 'ADMIN' && (int) $assignment['dm_user_id'] !== (int) $auth['id']) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không có quyền từ chối ca làm việc của người khác', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        if ($assignment['status'] !== 'PENDING') {
            return Response::fail('Ca làm việc này đã được xử lý trước đó (Trạng thái: ' . $assignment['status'] . ')', 'INVALID_STATUS', 400);
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $reason = trim((string) ($body['reason'] ?? ''));

        if ($reason === '') {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng nêu rõ lý do từ chối ca làm việc', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $this->dmRepo->respondAssignment($assignmentId, 'REJECTED', $reason);

        $updated = $this->dmRepo->findAssignmentById($assignmentId);

        return Response::success([
            'assignment' => $updated,
        ], 'Đã từ chối ca làm việc');
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\DMManagement\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class DMRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find DM profile by DM user ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT u.`id`, u.`id` AS `user_id`, u.`full_name` AS `name`, u.`full_name`, u.`email`,
                        u.`phone`, u.`avatar_url`, u.`status`,
                        u.`created_at`, u.`updated_at`
                 FROM `users` u
                 WHERE u.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find DM profile by User ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findByUserId(int $userId): ?array
    {
        return $this->findById($userId);
    }

    /**
     * Get active DM availabilities for a DM profile.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getAvailabilities(int $dmProfileId): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /**
     * Assign a DM to a game room.
     */
    public function assignToRoom(int $gameRoomId, int $dmUserId, ?string $reason = null, int $assignedBy = 1): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId, $dmUserId, $reason, $assignedBy): int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `dm_assignments` (`game_room_id`, `dm_user_id`, `assigned_by`, `status`, `reason`, `assigned_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:game_room_id, :dm_user_id, :assigned_by, \'PENDING\', :reason, NOW(), NOW(), NOW())'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':dm_user_id', $dmUserId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':assigned_by', $assignedBy, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':reason', $reason, $reason === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get active DM assignment for a game room.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function getActiveAssignmentForRoom(int $gameRoomId): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `dm_user_id`, `dm_user_id` AS `dm_profile_id`,
                        `status`, `assigned_at`, `responded_at`, `reason` AS `notes`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `dm_assignments`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :game_room_id AND `status` IN (\'PENDING\', \'ACCEPTED\')
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find assignment by ID with game and room details.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findAssignmentById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT a.`id`, a.`game_room_id`, a.`dm_user_id`, a.`assigned_by`, a.`status`,
                        a.`assigned_at`, a.`responded_at`, a.`revoked_at`, a.`reason`,
                        a.`created_at`, a.`updated_at`,
                        gr.`title` AS `room_title`, gr.`status` AS `room_status`,
                        g.`id` AS `game_id`, g.`name` AS `game_name`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `dm_name`, u.`email` AS `dm_email`
                 FROM `dm_assignments` a
                 INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = a.`game_room_id`
                 INNER JOIN `games` g ON g.`id` = gr.`game_id`
                 INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = a.`dm_user_id`
                 WHERE a.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * List assignments filtered by DM user ID or status.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listAssignments(?int $dmUserId = null, ?string $status = null): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($dmUserId, $status): array {
            $where = ['1 = 1'];
            $params = [];

            if ($dmUserId !== null && $dmUserId > 0) {
                $where[] = 'a.`dm_user_id` = :dm_user_id';
                $params[':dm_user_id'] = [$dmUserId, PDO::PARAM_INT];
            }

            if ($status !== null && trim($status) !== '' && $status !== 'ALL') {
                $where[] = 'a.`status` = :status';
                $params[':status'] = [strtoupper(trim($status)), PDO::PARAM_STR];
            }

            $whereSql = implode(' AND ', $where);
            $sql = "SELECT a.`id`, a.`game_room_id`, a.`dm_user_id`, a.`assigned_by`, a.`status`,
                           a.`assigned_at`, a.`responded_at`, a.`revoked_at`, a.`reason`,
                           a.`created_at`, a.`updated_at`,
                           gr.`title` AS `room_title`, gr.`status` AS `room_status`,
                           g.`id` AS `game_id`, g.`name` AS `game_name`,
                           u.`full_name` AS `dm_name`
                    FROM `dm_assignments` a
                    INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = a.`game_room_id`
                    INNER JOIN `games` g ON g.`id` = gr.`game_id`
                    INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = a.`dm_user_id`
                    WHERE {$whereSql}
                    ORDER BY a.`id` DESC";

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            foreach ($params as $param => [$val, $type]) {
                $stmt->bindValue($param, $val, $type);
            }
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Update DM assignment response status (ACCEPTED or REJECTED).
     */
    public function respondAssignment(int $id, string $status, ?string $reason = null): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id, $status, $reason): bool {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `dm_assignments`
                 SET `status` = :status,
                     `responded_at` = NOW(),
                     `reason` = :reason,
                     `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':status', strtoupper($status), PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':reason', $reason, $reason === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->rowCount() > 0;
        });
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\GameCatalog\Controllers\GameController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameCatalog\Controllers;

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\GameCatalog\Repositories\GameRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class GameController
{
    protected GameRepository $gameRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?GameRepository $gameRepo = null, ?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->gameRepo = $gameRepo ?? new GameRepository($this->pdo);
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/games
     * Query: ?limit=20&offset=0&players=6&search=an+mang
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $limit = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('limit', 20)));
        $offset = max(0, (int) $request->query('offset', 0));
        $players = $request->query('players') !== null && $request->query('players') !== '' ? (int) $request->query('players') : null;
        $search = $request->query('search', $request->query('q'));
        $search = $search !== null ? trim((string) $search) : null;

        $games = $this->gameRepo->listActive($limit, $offset, $players, $search);

        return Response::success([
            'games' => $games,
            'count' => count($games),
            'limit' => $limit,
            'offset' => $offset,
        ], 'Danh sách kịch bản');
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/games/{id}
     */
    public function show(Request $request, string $idOrSlug): Response
    {
        if (ctype_digit($idOrSlug)) {
            $game = $this->gameRepo->findById((int) $idOrSlug);
        } else {
            $game = $this->gameRepo->findBySlug($idOrSlug);
        }

        if ($game === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy kịch bản yêu cầu', 'GAME_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        // Lấy cấu hình phiên bản mới nhất
        $cfgStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `id`, `version`, `configuration`, `description`
             FROM `game_configurations`
             WHERE `game_id` = :game_id AND `is_active` = 1
             ORDER BY `version` DESC
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $cfgStmt->bindValue(':game_id', (int) $game['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $cfgStmt->execute();
        $config = $cfgStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($config !== false && !empty($config['configuration'])) {
            $game['configuration'] = json_decode((string) $config['configuration'], true);
            $game['game_configuration_id'] = (int) $config['id'];
        }

        // Kèm bảng giá
        $game['prices'] = $this->gameRepo->getActivePrices((int) $game['id']);

        return Response::success([
            'game' => $game,
        ], 'Chi tiết kịch bản');
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/games/{id}/prices
     */
    public function prices(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $gameId = (int) $id;
        $game = $this->gameRepo->findById($gameId);

        if ($game === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy kịch bản yêu cầu', 'GAME_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $prices = $this->gameRepo->getActivePrices($gameId);

        return Response::success([
            'game_id' => $gameId,
            'game_name' => $game['name'],
            'prices' => $prices,
        ], 'Bảng giá kịch bản');
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\GameCatalog\Repositories\GameRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameCatalog\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class GameRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find game by ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `name` AS `title`, `name`, `slug`, `description`, `image_url`,
                        `min_players`, `max_players`, `duration_minutes`, `status`,
                        (CASE WHEN `status` = \'ACTIVE\' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS `is_active`,
                        `display_order`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `games`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find game by unique slug.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findBySlug(string $slug): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($slug): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `name` AS `title`, `name`, `slug`, `description`, `image_url`,
                        `min_players`, `max_players`, `duration_minutes`, `status`,
                        (CASE WHEN `status` = \'ACTIVE\' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS `is_active`,
                        `display_order`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `games`
                 WHERE `slug` = :slug
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':slug', $slug, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * List active games with pagination, optional player filtering and search.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listActive(int $limit = 20, int $offset = 0, ?int $players = null, ?string $search = null): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($limit, $offset, $players, $search): array {
            $where = ['g.`status` = \'ACTIVE\''];
            $params = [];

            if ($players !== null && $players > 0) {
                $where[] = 'g.`min_players` <= :p1 AND g.`max_players` >= :p2';
                $params[':p1'] = $players;
                $params[':p2'] = $players;
            }

            if ($search !== null && trim($search) !== '') {
                $where[] = '(g.`name` LIKE :s1 OR g.`description` LIKE :s2)';
                $searchTerm = '%' . trim($search) . '%';
                $params[':s1'] = $searchTerm;
                $params[':s2'] = $searchTerm;
            }

            $sql = 'SELECT g.`id`, g.`name` AS `title`, g.`name`, g.`slug`, g.`description`, g.`image_url`,
                           g.`min_players`, g.`max_players`, g.`duration_minutes`, g.`status`,
                           1 AS `is_active`, g.`display_order`, g.`created_at`, g.`updated_at`,
                           MIN(gp.`price_per_person`) AS `min_price`,
                           MAX(gp.`price_per_person`) AS `max_price`
                    FROM `games` g
                    LEFT JOIN `game_prices` gp ON gp.`game_id` = g.`id` AND gp.`status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                    WHERE ' . implode(' AND ', $where) . '
                    GROUP BY g.`id`
                    ORDER BY g.`display_order` ASC, g.`name` ASC
                    LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset';

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            foreach ($params as $param => $val) {
                $stmt->bindValue($param, $val, is_int($val) ? PDO::PARAM_INT : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            }
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', max(1, $limit), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':offset', max(0, $offset), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$row) {
                $row['min_price'] = $row['min_price'] !== null ? (int) $row['min_price'] : null;
                $row['max_price'] = $row['max_price'] !== null ? (int) $row['max_price'] : null;
            }
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get active prices for a game.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getActivePrices(int $gameId): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameId): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_id`, `price_per_person` AS `base_price_per_player`, `price_per_person`,
                        `start_time` AS `time_from`, `end_time` AS `time_to`, `start_time`, `end_time`,
                        `effective_from`, `effective_until`, `status`,
                        (CASE WHEN `status` = \'ACTIVE\' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS `is_active`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `game_prices`
                 WHERE `game_id` = :game_id AND `status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                 ORDER BY `start_time` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_id', $gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create a new game.
     *
     * @param array{
     *     name?: string,
     *     title?: string,
     *     slug: string,
     *     description?: string|null,
     *     min_players?: int,
     *     max_players?: int,
     *     duration_minutes?: int,
     *     status?: string,
     *     is_active?: bool
     * } $data
     */
    public function create(array $data): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($data): int {
            $name = $data['name'] ?? $data['title'] ?? 'Kịch bản mới';
            $status = $data['status'] ?? (($data['is_active'] ?? true) ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE');

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `games` (`name`, `slug`, `description`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `duration_minutes`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:name, :slug, :description, :min_players, :max_players, :duration_minutes, :status, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':name', $name, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':slug', $data['slug'], PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':description', $data['description'] ?? null, ($data['description'] ?? null) === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':min_players', $data['min_players'] ?? 4, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':max_players', $data['max_players'] ?? 8, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':duration_minutes', $data['duration_minutes'] ?? 240, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':status', $status, PDO::PARAM_STR);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers\AdminGameRoomController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class AdminGameRoomController
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null, ?NotificationRepository $notiRepo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->notiRepo = $notiRepo ?? new NotificationRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/admin/rooms/{id}/cancel
     * Hủy phòng chơi bởi Quản trị viên và tự động kích hoạt luồng đánh giá hoàn cọc 24 giờ
     * Body: reason (string, required)
     */
    public function cancel(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $reason = trim((string) ($body['reason'] ?? $body['cancellation_reason'] ?? ''));
        $forceRefund = !empty($body['force_refund']);

        if ($reason === '') {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng nêu rõ lý do hủy phòng chơi', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        // 1. Lấy thông tin phòng
        $rStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `title`, `status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = :id LIMIT 1');
        $rStmt->bindValue(':id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $rStmt->execute();
        $room = $rStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($room === false) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi không tồn tại', 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        if (in_array($room['status'], ['CANCELLED', 'COMPLETED'], true)) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi đã ở trạng thái kết thúc (Trạng thái: ' . $room['status'] . ')', 'INVALID_ROOM_STATUS', 400);
        }

        // 2. Lấy thông tin Session nếu có để đánh giá mốc 24 giờ
        $sStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `start_at`, `status` FROM `sessions` WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id LIMIT 1');
        $sStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $sStmt->execute();
        $session = $sStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $isEligibleForRefund = true;
        $hoursUntilSession = null;
        $sessionStartAt = null;

        if ($session !== false && !empty($session['start_at'])) {
            $sessionStartAt = (string) $session['start_at'];
            $diffSeconds = strtotime($sessionStartAt) - time();
            $hoursUntilSession = round($diffSeconds / 3600, 2);

            // Quy tắc V16: Ít nhất 24 giờ trước giờ chơi mới đủ điều kiện hoàn tiền
            if ($diffSeconds < (24 * 3600)) {
                $isEligibleForRefund = false;
            }
        }

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            // 3. Cập nhật game_rooms
            $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                 SET `status` = \'CANCELLED\', `cancelled_at` = NOW(), `cancel_reason` = :reason, `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $upRoom->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->execute();

            // 4. Cập nhật sessions nếu có
            if ($session !== false) {
                $upSess = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `sessions`
                     SET `status` = \'CANCELLED\', `cancelled_at` = NOW(), `cancel_reason` = :reason, `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id'
                );
                $upSess->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $upSess->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upSess->execute();
            }

            // 5. Cập nhật room_participants
            $upParts = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `room_participants`
                 SET `status` = \'CANCELLED_BY_ADMIN\', `cancelled_at` = NOW(), `cancel_reason` = :reason, `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')'
            );
            $upParts->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $upParts->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upParts->execute();

            // 6. Xử lý hoàn tiền cho các khoản thanh toán đã nạp tiền (paid_amount > 0)
            $payStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT p.`id`, p.`room_participant_id`, p.`paid_amount`, rp.`user_id`
                 FROM `player_payments` p
                 INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                 WHERE rp.`game_room_id` = :room_id AND p.`paid_amount` > 0'
            );
            $payStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $payStmt->execute();
            $paidList = $payStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            $refundRecords = [];
            $insRef = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `refunds` (`player_payment_id`, `amount`, `reason`, `status`, `requested_at`, `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`, `review_reason`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:payment_id, :amount, :reason, :status, NOW(), :reviewed_by, NOW(), :review_reason, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $refundStatus = $isEligibleForRefund ? 'APPROVED' : 'REJECTED';
            $reviewNote = $isEligibleForRefund
                ? 'Đủ điều kiện hoàn cọc (hủy trước mốc 24h so với giờ chơi)'
                : 'Không đủ điều kiện hoàn cọc (hủy muộn hơn mốc 24h trước giờ chơi)';

            foreach ($paidList as $payment) {
                $payId = (int) $payment['id'];
                $amount = (int) $payment['paid_amount'];

                $insRef->bindValue(':payment_id', $payId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $insRef->bindValue(':amount', $amount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $insRef->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $insRef->bindValue(':status', $refundStatus, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $insRef->bindValue(':reviewed_by', (int) $auth['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $insRef->bindValue(':review_reason', $reviewNote, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $insRef->execute();

                $refundRecords[] = [
                    'refund_id' => (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId(),
                    'payment_id' => $payId,
                    'user_id' => (int) $payment['user_id'],
                    'amount' => $amount,
                    'status' => $refundStatus,
                    'note' => $reviewNote,
                ];

                // Thông báo hoàn tiền cho từng user
                $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                    'Thông báo hủy phòng & hoàn tiền',
                    "Phòng '{$room['title']}' đã bị hủy bởi quản trị viên. Kết quả hoàn tiền: {$reviewNote} (Số tiền: " . number_format($amount) . " VNĐ).",
                    'REFUND',
                    null,
                    [(int) $payment['user_id']]
                );
            }

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success([
                'game_room_id' => $roomId,
                'status' => 'CANCELLED',
                'cancel_reason' => $reason,
                'session_start_at' => $sessionStartAt,
                'hours_until_session' => $hoursUntilSession,
                'is_eligible_for_refund' => $isEligibleForRefund,
                'refunds' => $refundRecords,
            ], 'Đã hủy phòng chơi và hoàn tất quy trình đánh giá hoàn tiền');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }
}
