<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\GameCatalog\Repositories\GameRepository;
use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Repositories\GameRoomRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class GameRoomController
{
    protected GameRoomRepository $roomRepo;
    protected GameRepository $gameRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(
        ?GameRoomRepository $roomRepo = null,
        ?GameRepository $gameRepo = null,
        ?PDO $pdo = null
    ) {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->roomRepo = $roomRepo ?? new GameRoomRepository($this->pdo);
        $this->gameRepo = $gameRepo ?? new GameRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/rooms
     * Lấy danh sách các phòng đang tuyển người (FORMING)
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $gameId = $request->query('game_id') !== null ? (int) $request->query('game_id') : null;
        $status = (string) $request->query('status', 'FORMING');
        $limit = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('limit', 20)));
        $offset = max(0, (int) $request->query('offset', 0));

        $sql = 'SELECT gr.`id`, gr.`game_id`, gr.`creator_id`, gr.`title`, gr.`description`,
                       gr.`min_players`, gr.`max_players`, gr.`status`, gr.`formation_deadline_at`,
                       gr.`replacement_status`, gr.`replacement_deadline_at`,
                       g.`name` AS `game_name`, g.`image_url` AS `game_image_url`, g.`duration_minutes`,
                       u.`full_name` AS `creator_name`,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `room_participants` rp
                        WHERE rp.`game_room_id` = gr.`id`
                          AND rp.`status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')
                       ) AS `current_players`
                FROM `game_rooms` gr
                INNER JOIN `games` g ON g.`id` = gr.`game_id`
                INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = gr.`creator_id`
                WHERE 1 = 1';

        $bindings = [];

        if ($status !== 'ALL') {
            $sql .= ' AND gr.`status` = :status';
            $bindings[':status'] = [$status, PDO::PARAM_STR];
        }

        if ($gameId !== null && $gameId > 0) {
            $sql .= ' AND gr.`game_id` = :game_id';
            $bindings[':game_id'] = [$gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT];
        }

        $sql .= ' ORDER BY gr.`id` DESC LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset';

        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
        foreach ($bindings as $key => [$val, $type]) {
            $stmt->bindValue($key, $val, $type);
        }
        $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();

        $rooms = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        return Response::success([
            'rooms' => $rooms,
            'count' => count($rooms),
        ], 'Danh sách phòng chơi');
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/rooms/{id}
     * Chi tiết phòng chơi và danh sách người tham gia
     */
    public function show(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $room = $this->roomRepo->findById($roomId);

        if ($room === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy phòng chơi yêu cầu', 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        // Lấy kịch bản game
        $game = $this->gameRepo->findById((int) $room['game_id']);
        $room['game'] = $game;

        // Lấy danh sách thành viên tham gia
        $participants = $this->roomRepo->getParticipants($roomId);
        $room['participants'] = $participants;
        $room['current_players'] = count(array_filter($participants, function ($p) {
            return in_array($p['status'], ['PENDING_DEPOSIT', 'DEPOSITED', 'LEAVE_REQUESTED'], true);
        }));

        // Lấy các đề xuất lịch của phòng
        $propStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT sp.`id`, sp.`game_room_id`, sp.`proposed_by`, sp.`proposed_start_at`, sp.`proposed_end_at`,
                    sp.`status`, sp.`expires_at`, sp.`confirmed_at`, sp.`rejected_at`, sp.`created_at`,
                    u.`full_name` AS `proposer_name`
             FROM `schedule_proposals` sp
             INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = sp.`proposed_by`
             WHERE sp.`game_room_id` = :room_id
             ORDER BY sp.`id` DESC'
        );
        $propStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $propStmt->execute();
        $room['proposals'] = $propStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        return Response::success([
            'room' => $room,
        ], 'Chi tiết phòng chơi');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/rooms
     * Tạo phòng ghép người chơi mới
     */
    public function create(Request $request): Response
    {
        $userId = $this->resolveCurrentUserId($request);
        if ($userId === null) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn cần đăng nhập để tạo phòng chơi', 'UNAUTHORIZED', 401);
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $gameId = (int) ($body['game_id'] ?? 0);
        $title = trim((string) ($body['title'] ?? ''));
        $description = isset($body['description']) ? trim((string) $body['description']) : null;
        $formationDeadline = $body['formation_deadline_at'] ?? $body['formation_deadline'] ?? null;

        if ($gameId <= 0) {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng chọn kịch bản game hợp lệ', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $game = $this->gameRepo->findById($gameId);
        if ($game === null) {
            return Response::fail('Kịch bản game không tồn tại trên hệ thống', 'NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        if ($title === '') {
            $title = 'Phòng chơi ' . $game['name'] . ' #' . strtoupper(substr(uniqid(), -4));
        }

        $minPlayers = isset($body['min_players']) ? (int) $body['min_players'] : (int) $game['min_players'];
        $maxPlayers = isset($body['max_players']) ? (int) $body['max_players'] : (int) $game['max_players'];

        if ($deadlineStr = $formationDeadline) {
            $deadline = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime((string) $deadlineStr));
        } else {
            $deadline = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+3 days'));
        }

        // Lấy configuration id v1
        $cfgStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id` FROM `game_configurations` WHERE `game_id` = :game_id ORDER BY `version` DESC LIMIT 1');
        $cfgStmt->bindValue(':game_id', $gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $cfgStmt->execute();
        $cfgId = (int) ($cfgStmt->fetchColumn() ?: 1);

        $roomId = $this->roomRepo->create([
            'game_id' => $gameId,
            'game_configuration_id' => $cfgId,
            'creator_id' => $userId,
            'title' => $title,
            'description' => $description,
            'min_players' => $minPlayers,
            'max_players' => $maxPlayers,
            'status' => 'FORMING',
            'formation_deadline_at' => $deadline,
        ]);

        // Thêm creator vào làm thành viên đầu tiên
        $this->roomRepo->addParticipant($roomId, $userId);

        $createdRoom = $this->roomRepo->findById($roomId);

        return Response::success([
            'room' => $createdRoom,
        ], 'Tạo phòng chơi thành công', null, 201);
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/rooms/{id}/join
     * Tham gia vào phòng chơi
     */
    public function join(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $userId = $this->resolveCurrentUserId($request);
        if ($userId === null) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn cần đăng nhập để tham gia phòng', 'UNAUTHORIZED', 401);
        }

        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $room = $this->roomRepo->findById($roomId);
        if ($room === null) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi không tồn tại', 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $isReplacementOpen = ($room['replacement_status'] ?? '') === 'OPEN';

        if ($room['status'] !== 'FORMING' && !$isReplacementOpen) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi hiện không trong giai đoạn nhận thành viên (Trạng thái: ' . $room['status'] . ')', 'ROOM_NOT_FORMING', 400);
        }

        // Kiểm tra số lượng người hiện tại
        $participants = $this->roomRepo->getParticipants($roomId);
        $activeParticipants = array_filter($participants, function ($p) {
            return in_array($p['status'], ['PENDING_DEPOSIT', 'DEPOSITED', 'LEAVE_REQUESTED'], true);
        });

        // Kiểm tra xem user này đã ở trong phòng chưa
        foreach ($activeParticipants as $part) {
            if ((int) $part['user_id'] === $userId) {
                return Response::fail('Bạn đã có tên trong phòng chơi này rồi', 'ALREADY_JOINED', 409);
            }
        }

        // TRƯỜNG HỢP 1: Vào thay thế (Replacement Slot Takeover)
        if ($isReplacementOpen) {
            $leavePartStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `user_id` FROM `room_participants`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\'
                 ORDER BY `id` ASC LIMIT 1'
            );
            $leavePartStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $leavePartStmt->execute();
            $leavingPart = $leavePartStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($leavingPart === false) {
                return Response::fail('Hiện không còn slot tìm người thay thế trong phòng này', 'NO_REPLACEMENT_SLOT', 400);
            }

            $leavingPartId = (int) $leavingPart['id'];
            $leavingUserId = (int) $leavingPart['user_id'];

            $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
            try {
                // 1. Cho người cũ rời phòng: cập nhật status thành 'LEFT'
                $upOld = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `room_participants`
                     SET `status` = \'LEFT\', `left_at` = NOW(), `cancel_reason` = \'Đã có người chơi khác nhận thay thế slot\'
                     WHERE `id` = :id'
                );
                $upOld->bindValue(':id', $leavingPartId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upOld->execute();

                // 2. Thêm người mới vào slot thay thế
                $newPartId = $this->roomRepo->addParticipant($roomId, $userId);

                // 3. Kiểm tra xem còn ai LEAVE_REQUESTED không
                $checkLeftStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `room_participants`
                     WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\''
                );
                $checkLeftStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $checkLeftStmt->execute();
                $remainingReplacements = (int) $checkLeftStmt->fetchColumn();

                if ($remainingReplacements === 0) {
                    $closeRw = $this->pdo->prepare(
                        'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                         SET `replacement_status` = \'CLOSED\', `replacement_closed_at` = NOW()
                         WHERE `id` = :room_id'
                    );
                    $closeRw->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $closeRw->execute();
                }

                $this->pdo->commit();

                // Gửi thông báo cho người cũ
                $notiRepo = new \App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository($this->pdo);
                $notiRepo->createNotification(
                    'Đã có người thay thế slot',
                    "Yêu cầu rút phòng của bạn tại phòng '{$room['title']}' đã hoàn tất nhờ có người chơi khác thế chỗ.",
                    'ROOM',
                    null,
                    [$leavingUserId]
                );

                return Response::success([
                    'room_id' => $roomId,
                    'participant_id' => $newPartId,
                    'is_replacement' => true,
                    'replaced_participant_id' => $leavingPartId,
                    'replacement_window_closed' => $remainingReplacements === 0,
                ], 'Bạn đã tham gia thế chỗ thành công vào phòng chơi');
            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $this->pdo->rollBack();
                }
                throw $e;
            }
        }

        // TRƯỜNG HỢP 2: Vào phòng FORMING bình thường
        if (count($activeParticipants) >= (int) $room['max_players']) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi đã đủ số lượng thành viên tối đa (' . $room['max_players'] . ' người)', 'ROOM_FULL', 400);
        }

        $participantId = $this->roomRepo->addParticipant($roomId, $userId);

        return Response::success([
            'room_id' => $roomId,
            'participant_id' => $participantId,
            'current_players' => count($activeParticipants) + 1,
        ], 'Tham gia phòng chơi thành công');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/rooms/{id}/leave
     * Rời phòng chơi hoặc mở cửa sổ tìm người thay thế
     */
    public function leave(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $userId = $this->resolveCurrentUserId($request);
        if ($userId === null) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn cần đăng nhập để thực hiện thao tác này', 'UNAUTHORIZED', 401);
        }

        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $room = $this->roomRepo->findById($roomId);
        if ($room === null) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi không tồn tại', 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        // Tìm participant active của user trong room
        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `id`, `status` FROM `room_participants`
             WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `user_id` = :user_id
               AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $stmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();
        $part = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($part === false) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không phải là thành viên đang hoạt động trong phòng này', 'PARTICIPANT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $partId = (int) $part['id'];

        if (in_array($room['status'], ['CANCELLED', 'COMPLETED', 'EXPIRED'], true)) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi đã kết thúc hoặc bị hủy, không thể rời phòng', 'INVALID_ROOM_STATUS', 400);
        }

        if ($room['status'] === 'FORMING') {
            // Rời phòng trực tiếp khi phòng chưa chốt lịch
            $upStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `room_participants` SET `status` = \'LEFT\', `left_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id');
            $upStmt->bindValue(':id', $partId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upStmt->execute();

            return Response::success(null, 'Đã rời phòng chơi thành công');
        }

        // Nếu phòng đã chốt lịch/cọc: Mở cửa sổ tìm người thay thế (Replacement Window)
        $upStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `room_participants` SET `status` = \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\' WHERE `id` = :id');
        $upStmt->bindValue(':id', $partId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $upStmt->execute();

        $openRw = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'UPDATE `game_rooms`
             SET `replacement_opened_by` = :uid,
                 `replacement_reason` = \'Thành viên bận xin rút và tìm người thay thế\',
                 `replacement_status` = \'OPEN\',
                 `replacement_started_at` = NOW(),
                 `replacement_deadline_at` = DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 24 HOUR)
             WHERE `id` = :room_id'
        );
        $openRw->bindValue(':uid', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $openRw->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $openRw->execute();

        return Response::success([
            'replacement_status' => 'OPEN',
        ], 'Yêu cầu rút phòng đã được ghi nhận. Đã mở cửa sổ tuyển người thay thế trong 24 giờ.');
    }

    /**
     * Resolve current user ID from session or header.
     */
    protected function resolveCurrentUserId(Request $request): ?int
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return null;
        }

        return (int) $auth['id'];
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\GameroomManagement\Repositories\GameRoomRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameroomManagement\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class GameRoomRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find room by ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, CONCAT(\'ROOM_\', `id`) AS `room_code`, `creator_id`, `game_id`, `game_configuration_id`,
                        `title`, `description`, `min_players`, `max_players`,
                        `status`, 1 AS `is_active`,
                        `formation_deadline_at` AS `formation_deadline`, `formation_deadline_at`,
                        `description` AS `notes`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_at`,
                        `replacement_opened_by`, `replacement_status`, `replacement_deadline_at`,
                        `price_final_amount`, `price_deposit_total`, `price_remaining_total`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `game_rooms`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find room by room code (or room ID).
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findByRoomCode(string $roomCode): ?array
    {
        $id = (int) preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $roomCode);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            return null;
        }
        return $this->findById($id);
    }

    /**
     * Create a new game room.
     *
     * @param array{
     *     game_id: int,
     *     game_configuration_id?: int,
     *     creator_id: int,
     *     title: string,
     *     description?: string|null,
     *     min_players?: int,
     *     max_players?: int,
     *     status?: string,
     *     formation_deadline?: string,
     *     formation_deadline_at?: string,
     *     notes?: string|null
     * } $data
     */
    public function create(array $data): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($data): int {
            $gameId = (int) $data['game_id'];
            $configId = $data['game_configuration_id'] ?? null;
            if ($configId === null) {
                $cfgStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id` FROM `game_configurations` WHERE `game_id` = :game_id AND `is_active` = 1 ORDER BY `version` DESC LIMIT 1');
                $cfgStmt->bindValue(':game_id', $gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $cfgStmt->execute();
                $resolved = $cfgStmt->fetchColumn();
                $configId = $resolved !== false ? (int) $resolved : 1;
            }

            $deadline = $data['formation_deadline_at'] ?? $data['formation_deadline'] ?? date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+3 days'));
            $desc = $data['description'] ?? $data['notes'] ?? null;

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`,
                                           `description`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`,
                                           `formation_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:game_id, :game_configuration_id, :creator_id, :title,
                         :description, :min_players, :max_players, :status,
                         :formation_deadline_at, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':game_id', $gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_configuration_id', $configId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':creator_id', $data['creator_id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':title', $data['title'], PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':description', $desc, $desc === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':min_players', $data['min_players'] ?? 4, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':max_players', $data['max_players'] ?? 8, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':status', $data['status'] ?? 'FORMING', PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':formation_deadline_at', $deadline, PDO::PARAM_STR);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Update room status.
     */
    public function updateStatus(int $id, string $newStatus, int $expectedVersion = 0, ?string $cancelReason = null): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id, $newStatus, $cancelReason): bool {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                 SET `status` = :status,
                     `cancel_reason` = :cancel_reason,
                     `cancelled_at` = CASE WHEN :status = \'CANCELLED\' THEN NOW() ELSE `cancelled_at` END,
                     `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':status', $newStatus, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':cancel_reason', $cancelReason, $cancelReason === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);

            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->rowCount() > 0;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Add a participant to a game room.
     */
    public function addParticipant(int $gameRoomId, int $userId, string $roleInRoom = 'PLAYER', int $depositAmount = 50000): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId, $userId): int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:game_room_id, :user_id, \'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get participants for a game room.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getParticipants(int $gameRoomId): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT rp.`id`, rp.`game_room_id`, rp.`user_id`, rp.`status`,
                        rp.`joined_at`, rp.`deposit_deadline_at`, rp.`deposited_at`, rp.`left_at`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `user_name`, u.`full_name`, u.`email` AS `user_email`
                 FROM `room_participants` rp
                 INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = rp.`user_id`
                 WHERE rp.`game_room_id` = :game_room_id
                 ORDER BY rp.`joined_at` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\GameroomManagement\Services\BackgroundJobService.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\GameroomManagement\Services;

use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class BackgroundJobService
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null, ?NotificationRepository $notiRepo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->notiRepo = $notiRepo ?? new NotificationRepository($this->pdo);
    }

    /**
     * Run all background maintenance jobs.
     *
     * @return array<string, int>
     */
    public function runAll(): array
    {
        return [
            'formation_timeouts' => $this->runFormationTimeout(),
            'deposit_timeouts' => $this->runDepositTimeout(),
            'replacement_timeouts' => $this->runReplacementTimeout(),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Job 1: Quét các phòng FORMING quá hạn (formation_deadline_at < NOW())
     * và chưa đủ số người chơi tối thiểu -> Tự động CANCELLED phòng.
     */
    public function runFormationTimeout(): int
    {
        // Lấy danh sách các phòng FORMING đã quá hạn
        $sql = "SELECT gr.`id`, gr.`title`, gr.`min_players`,
                       (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `room_participants` rp
                        WHERE rp.`game_room_id` = gr.`id`
                          AND rp.`status` IN ('PENDING_DEPOSIT', 'DEPOSITED', 'LEAVE_REQUESTED')
                       ) AS `current_players`
                FROM `game_rooms` gr
                WHERE gr.`status` = 'FORMING'
                  AND gr.`formation_deadline_at` <= NOW()";

        $stmt = $this->pdo->query($sql);
        $expiredRooms = $stmt !== false ? $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) : [];

        $cancelledCount = 0;

        foreach ($expiredRooms as $room) {
            $currentPlayers = (int) $room['current_players'];
            $minPlayers = (int) $room['min_players'];
            $roomId = (int) $room['id'];

            // Nếu số người chơi hiện tại vẫn chưa đủ min_players
            if ($currentPlayers < $minPlayers) {
                $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
                try {
                    // Hủy phòng
                    $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare(
                        'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                         SET `status` = \'CANCELLED\',
                             `cancelled_at` = NOW(),
                             `cancel_reason` = \'Hết thời hạn gom phòng nhưng chưa đủ số lượng người chơi tối thiểu\',
                             `updated_at` = NOW()
                         WHERE `id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'FORMING\''
                    );
                    $upRoom->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $upRoom->execute();

                    // Cập nhật thành viên phòng
                    $upParts = $this->pdo->prepare(
                        'UPDATE `room_participants`
                         SET `status` = \'CANCELLED_UNPAID\',
                             `cancelled_at` = NOW(),
                             `cancel_reason` = \'Phòng chơi tự động giải tán do không gom đủ người\',
                             `updated_at` = NOW()
                         WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')'
                    );
                    $upParts->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $upParts->execute();

                    // Lấy danh sách user_id để gửi notification
                    $uStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `user_id` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id');
                    $uStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $uStmt->execute();
                    $userIds = $uStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);

                    $this->pdo->commit();
                    $cancelledCount++;

                    if (!empty($userIds)) {
                        $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                            'Phòng chơi đã tự động giải tán',
                            "Phòng '{$room['title']}' đã bị hủy do hết thời hạn gom phòng mà chưa đủ số lượng thành viên tối thiểu ({$minPlayers} người).",
                            'ROOM',
                            null,
                            array_map('intval', $userIds)
                        );
                    }
                } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                    if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                        $this->pdo->rollBack();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return $cancelledCount;
    }

    /**
     * Job 2: Quét các người chơi quá hạn đóng tiền cọc (deposit_deadline_at < NOW())
     * nhưng vẫn ở trạng thái PENDING_DEPOSIT -> Kick khỏi phòng và mở Replacement Window.
     */
    public function runDepositTimeout(): int
    {
        $sql = "SELECT rp.`id`, rp.`game_room_id`, rp.`user_id`, gr.`title` AS `room_title`
                FROM `room_participants` rp
                INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = rp.`game_room_id`
                WHERE rp.`status` = 'PENDING_DEPOSIT'
                  AND rp.`deposit_deadline_at` IS NOT NULL
                  AND rp.`deposit_deadline_at` <= NOW()";

        $stmt = $this->pdo->query($sql);
        $expiredParticipants = $stmt !== false ? $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) : [];

        $kickedCount = 0;

        foreach ($expiredParticipants as $part) {
            $partId = (int) $part['id'];
            $roomId = (int) $part['game_room_id'];
            $userId = (int) $part['user_id'];

            $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
            try {
                // Kick participant
                $upPart = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `room_participants`
                     SET `status` = \'CANCELLED_UNPAID\',
                         `cancelled_at` = NOW(),
                         `cancel_reason` = \'Quá hạn nộp tiền cọc\',
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `id` = :id AND `status` = \'PENDING_DEPOSIT\''
                );
                $upPart->bindValue(':id', $partId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upPart->execute();

                // Mở Replacement Window cho phòng
                $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                     SET `replacement_status` = \'OPEN\',
                         `replacement_started_at` = NOW(),
                         `replacement_deadline_at` = DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 24 HOUR),
                         `replacement_reason` = \'Có thành viên quá hạn đóng tiền cọc cần tìm người thay thế\',
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `id` = :room_id'
                );
                $upRoom->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upRoom->execute();

                $this->pdo->commit();
                $kickedCount++;

                // Bắn notification cho người bị kick
                $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                    'Hủy tư cách tham gia do quá hạn cọc',
                    "Bạn đã bị hủy khỏi phòng '{$part['room_title']}' do không hoàn tất nộp tiền cọc đúng hạn.",
                    'ROOM',
                    null,
                    [$userId]
                );
            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $this->pdo->rollBack();
                }
            }
        }

        return $kickedCount;
    }

    /**
     * Job 3: Quét các phòng đang mở Replacement Window (replacement_status = 'OPEN')
     * mà đã quá hạn 24 tiếng (replacement_deadline_at < NOW()) -> Đóng cửa sổ (EXPIRED).
     */
    public function runReplacementTimeout(): int
    {
        $sql = "SELECT gr.`id`, gr.`title`
                FROM `game_rooms` gr
                WHERE gr.`replacement_status` = 'OPEN'
                  AND gr.`replacement_deadline_at` IS NOT NULL
                  AND gr.`replacement_deadline_at` <= NOW()";

        $stmt = $this->pdo->query($sql);
        $expiredWindows = $stmt !== false ? $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) : [];

        $closedCount = 0;

        foreach ($expiredWindows as $room) {
            $roomId = (int) $room['id'];

            $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
            try {
                // Đóng replacement window
                $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                     SET `replacement_status` = \'EXPIRED\',
                         `replacement_closed_at` = NOW(),
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `id` = :room_id AND `replacement_status` = \'OPEN\''
                );
                $upRoom->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upRoom->execute();

                // Cập nhật người đang LEAVE_REQUESTED thành LEFT (bị phạt cọc không hoàn do hết hạn tìm người thay thế)
                $upParts = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `room_participants`
                     SET `status` = \'LEFT\',
                         `left_at` = NOW(),
                         `cancel_reason` = \'Hết hạn 24h tìm người thay thế mà không có người nhận slot (Không hoàn cọc)\',
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\''
                );
                $upParts->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upParts->execute();

                $this->pdo->commit();
                $closedCount++;
            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $this->pdo->rollBack();
                }
            }
        }

        return $closedCount;
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Identity\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Shared\Services\AuditLogService;

class AdminAuditLogController
{
    protected AuditLogService $auditService;

    public function __construct(?AuditLogService $auditService = null)
    {
        $this->auditService = $auditService ?? new AuditLogService();
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/admin/audit-logs
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $page = max(1, (int) $request->query('page', 1));
        $perPage = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('per_page', 20)));
        $action = $request->query('action') !== null ? (string) $request->query('action') : null;
        $entityType = $request->query('entity_type') !== null ? (string) $request->query('entity_type') : null;

        $result = $this->auditService->listLogs($page, $perPage, $action, $entityType);

        return Response::paginated($result['items'], $page, $perPage, $result['total'], 'Nhật ký kiểm toán hệ thống');
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Identity\Controllers\AdminUserController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Identity\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Identity\Repositories\UserRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class AdminUserController
{
    protected UserRepository $userRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?UserRepository $userRepo = null, ?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->userRepo = $userRepo ?? new UserRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/admin/users
     * Query: page, per_page, search, role, status
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $page = max(1, (int) $request->query('page', 1));
        $perPage = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('per_page', 20)));
        $search = $request->query('search') !== null ? (string) $request->query('search') : null;
        $roleCode = $request->query('role') !== null ? (string) $request->query('role') : null;
        $status = $request->query('status') !== null ? (string) $request->query('status') : null;

        $result = $this->userRepo->listUsers($page, $perPage, $search, $roleCode, $status);

        return Response::paginated(
            $result['items'],
            $page,
            $perPage,
            $result['total'],
            'Danh sách người dùng hệ thống'
        );
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/admin/users/{id}
     */
    public function show(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $userId = (int) $id;
        $user = $this->userRepo->findById($userId);

        if ($user === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy người dùng yêu cầu', 'USER_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        unset($user['password_hash']);

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $user,
        ], 'Chi tiết người dùng');
    }

    /**
     * PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/{id}
     * Body: full_name, phone, status (ACTIVE, INACTIVE, SUSPENDED)
     */
    public function update(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $targetUserId = (int) $id;
        $user = $this->userRepo->findById($targetUserId);

        if ($user === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy người dùng để cập nhật', 'USER_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $updateData = [];

        if (isset($body['full_name'])) {
            $name = trim((string) $body['full_name']);
            if ($name === '') {
                return Response::fail('Họ và tên không được để trống', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
            }
            $updateData['full_name'] = $name;
        }

        if (isset($body['phone'])) {
            $updateData['phone'] = trim((string) $body['phone']);
        }

        if (isset($body['status'])) {
            $newStatus = strtoupper(trim((string) $body['status']));
            if (!in_array($newStatus, ['ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED'], true)) {
                return Response::fail('Trạng thái không hợp lệ (phải là ACTIVE, INACTIVE, SUSPENDED)', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
            }

            // Chống tự khóa tài khoản Admin của chính mình
            if ((int) $auth['id'] === $targetUserId && $newStatus !== 'ACTIVE') {
                return Response::fail('Admin không thể tự khóa hoặc vô hiệu hóa tài khoản của chính mình', 'CANNOT_SUSPEND_SELF', 400);
            }

            $updateData['status'] = $newStatus;
        }

        if (!empty($updateData)) {
            $this->userRepo->update($targetUserId, $updateData);
        }

        $updated = $this->userRepo->findById($targetUserId);
        if ($updated !== null) {
            unset($updated['password_hash']);
        }

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $updated,
        ], 'Cập nhật thông tin người dùng thành công');
    }

    /**
     * PUT /api/v1/admin/users/{id}/role
     * Body: role_id OR role_code
     */
    public function assignRole(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $targetUserId = (int) $id;
        $user = $this->userRepo->findById($targetUserId);

        if ($user === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy người dùng', 'USER_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $roleTarget = $body['role_id'] ?? $body['role_code'] ?? $body['role'] ?? null;

        if ($roleTarget === null) {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng cung cấp role_id hoặc role_code cần gán', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $role = $this->userRepo->findRole($roleTarget);
        if ($role === null) {
            return Response::fail('Vai trò được chỉ định không tồn tại trên hệ thống', 'ROLE_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        // Chống tự giáng chức Admin của chính mình nếu không còn admin nào khác
        if ((int) $auth['id'] === $targetUserId && strtoupper((string) $role['code']) !== 'ADMIN') {
            return Response::fail('Admin không thể tự tước quyền Quản trị viên của chính mình', 'CANNOT_DEMOTE_SELF', 400);
        }

        $this->userRepo->assignRole($targetUserId, (int) $role['id']);

        $updated = $this->userRepo->findById($targetUserId);
        if ($updated !== null) {
            unset($updated['password_hash']);
        }

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $updated,
            'assigned_role' => $role,
        ], 'Gán vai trò cho người dùng thành công');
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\Identity\Controllers\AuthController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Identity\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Middleware\CsrfMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Identity\Repositories\UserRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;

class AuthController
{
    protected UserRepository $userRepo;

    public function __construct(?UserRepository $userRepo = null)
    {
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->userRepo = $userRepo ?? new UserRepository($pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/auth/csrf-token
     */
    public function csrfToken(Request $request): Response
    {
        $token = CsrfMiddleware::getToken();
        return Response::success(['csrf_token' => $token], 'CSRF token retrieved successfully');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/auth/register
     */
    public function register(Request $request): Response
    {
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();

        $fullName = trim((string) ($body['full_name'] ?? $body['name'] ?? ''));
        $email = trim(strtolower((string) ($body['email'] ?? '')));
        $password = (string) ($body['password'] ?? '');
        $phone = isset($body['phone']) ? trim((string) $body['phone']) : null;

        $errors = [];
        if ($fullName === '') {
            $errors['full_name'] = ['Họ và tên không được để trống'];
        }

        if ($email === '' || !filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) {
            $errors['email'] = ['Email không đúng định dạng'];
        }

        if (strlen($password) < 6) {
            $errors['password'] = ['Mật khẩu phải có ít nhất 6 ký tự'];
        }

        if (!empty($errors)) {
            return Response::fail('Dữ liệu không hợp lệ', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422, $errors);
        }

        // Check duplicate email
        $existing = $this->userRepo->findByEmail($email);
        if ($existing !== null) {
            return Response::fail('Email này đã được sử dụng trên hệ thống', 'EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS', 409);
        }

        $passwordHash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_BCRYPT);

        $userId = $this->userRepo->create([
            'full_name' => $fullName,
            'email' => $email,
            'password_hash' => $passwordHash,
            'phone' => $phone,
            'status' => 'ACTIVE',
        ]);

        $user = $this->userRepo->findById($userId);
        if ($user !== null) {
            unset($user['password_hash']);
        }

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
            @session_regenerate_id(true);
        }
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = $userId;
        $_SESSION['role_id'] = $user['role_id'] ?? 1;
        $_SESSION['role_code'] = $user['role_code'] ?? 'PLAYER';

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $user,
        ], 'Đăng ký tài khoản thành công', null, 201);
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/auth/login
     */
    public function login(Request $request): Response
    {
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();

        $email = trim(strtolower((string) ($body['email'] ?? '')));
        $password = (string) ($body['password'] ?? '');

        if ($email === '' || $password === '') {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ email và mật khẩu', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $user = $this->userRepo->findByEmail($email);
        if ($user === null) {
            return Response::fail('Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác', 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 401);
        }

        if (($user['status'] ?? '') !== 'ACTIVE') {
            return Response::fail('Tài khoản của bạn đang bị tạm khóa hoặc chưa kích hoạt', 'ACCOUNT_INACTIVE', 403);
        }

        if (!password_verify($password, (string) $user['password_hash'])) {
            return Response::fail('Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác', 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 401);
        }

        // Update last login
        $this->userRepo->update((int) $user['id'], [
            'last_login_at' => date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
        ]);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
            @session_regenerate_id(true);
        }
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = (int) $user['id'];
        $_SESSION['role_id'] = (int) ($user['role_id'] ?? 1);
        $_SESSION['role_code'] = (string) ($user['role_code'] ?? 'PLAYER');

        unset($user['password_hash']);

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $user,
        ], 'Đăng nhập thành công');
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/auth/me
     */
    public function me(Request $request): Response
    {
        $authUser = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($authUser instanceof Response) {
            return $authUser;
        }

        unset($authUser['password_hash']);

        return Response::success([
            'user' => $authUser,
        ], 'Thông tin tài khoản');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/auth/logout
     */
    public function logout(Request $request): Response
    {
        $_SESSION = [];

        if (ini_get('session.use_cookies') && !headers_sent()) {
            $params = session_get_cookie_params();
            setcookie(
                session_name(),
                '',
                time() - 42000,
                $params['path'],
                $params['domain'],
                $params['secure'],
                $params['httponly']
            );
        }

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
            @session_destroy();
        }

        return Response::success(null, 'Đăng xuất thành công');
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Identity\Repositories\UserRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Identity\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class UserRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find user by primary key ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT u.`id`, u.`role_id`, r.`code` AS `role_code`, r.`name` AS `role_name`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `name`, u.`full_name`, u.`email`, u.`password_hash`,
                        u.`phone`, u.`avatar_url`, u.`status`, u.`email_verified_at`, u.`last_login_at`,
                        u.`created_at`, u.`updated_at`
                 FROM `users` u
                 LEFT JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE u.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find user by unique email.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findByEmail(string $email): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($email): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT u.`id`, u.`role_id`, r.`code` AS `role_code`, r.`name` AS `role_name`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `name`, u.`full_name`, u.`email`, u.`password_hash`,
                        u.`phone`, u.`avatar_url`, u.`status`, u.`email_verified_at`, u.`last_login_at`,
                        u.`created_at`, u.`updated_at`
                 FROM `users` u
                 LEFT JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE u.`email` = :email
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':email', $email, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create a new user record.
     *
     * @param array{
     *     role_id?: int,
     *     name?: string,
     *     full_name?: string,
     *     email: string,
     *     password_hash: string,
     *     phone?: string|null,
     *     status?: string
     * } $data
     * @return int Inserted ID
     */
    public function create(array $data): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($data): int {
            $roleId = $data['role_id'] ?? null;
            if ($roleId === null) {
                $roleStmt = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `id` FROM `roles` WHERE `code` = 'PLAYER' OR `name` = 'PLAYER' LIMIT 1");
                $resolved = $roleStmt !== false ? $roleStmt->fetchColumn() : false;
                if ($resolved !== false && $resolved !== null) {
                    $roleId = (int) $resolved;
                } else {
                    $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `roles` (`code`, `name`, `description`) VALUES ('PLAYER', 'Player', 'Nguoi choi')");
                    $roleId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
                }
            }

            $fullName = $data['full_name'] ?? $data['name'] ?? 'User';

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `users` (`role_id`, `full_name`, `email`, `password_hash`, `phone`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:role_id, :full_name, :email, :password_hash, :phone, :status, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':role_id', $roleId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':full_name', $fullName, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':email', $data['email'], PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':password_hash', $data['password_hash'], PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':phone', $data['phone'] ?? null, ($data['phone'] ?? null) === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':status', $data['status'] ?? 'ACTIVE', PDO::PARAM_STR);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Update user by ID.
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     */
    public function update(int $id, array $data): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id, $data): bool {
            $allowedColumns = ['full_name', 'email', 'password_hash', 'phone', 'status', 'email_verified_at', 'avatar_url', 'last_login_at'];
            if (isset($data['name']) && !isset($data['full_name'])) {
                $data['full_name'] = $data['name'];
                unset($data['name']);
            }

            $setParts = [];
            $bindings = [':id' => [$id, PDO::PARAM_INT]];

            foreach ($data as $column => $value) {
                if (!in_array($column, $allowedColumns, true)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $param = ':' . $column;
                $setParts[] = "`{$column}` = {$param}";

                if ($value === null) {
                    $bindings[$param] = [null, PDO::PARAM_NULL];
                } else {
                    $bindings[$param] = [(string) $value, PDO::PARAM_STR];
                }
            }

            if (empty($setParts)) {
                return false;
            }

            $setParts[] = '`updated_at` = NOW()';
            $sql = 'UPDATE `users` SET ' . implode(', ', $setParts) . ' WHERE `id` = :id';

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            foreach ($bindings as $param => [$val, $type]) {
                $stmt->bindValue($param, $val, $type);
            }

            $stmt->execute();
            return $stmt->rowCount() > 0;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get user roles by user ID.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getRoles(int $userId): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT r.`id`, r.`code`, r.`name`, r.`description`, u.`created_at` AS `assigned_at`
                 FROM `roles` r
                 INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`role_id` = r.`id`
                 WHERE u.`id` = :user_id
                 ORDER BY r.`name` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Assign role to user.
     */
    public function assignRole(int $userId, int $roleId): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId, $roleId): bool {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `users`
                 SET `role_id` = :role_id, `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :user_id'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':role_id', $roleId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->rowCount() > 0;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Remove role from user.
     */
    public function removeRole(int $userId, int $roleId): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId, $roleId): bool {
            // Revert back to PLAYER role if target role matches
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE `users`
                 SET `role_id` = (SELECT `id` FROM `roles` WHERE `code` = 'PLAYER' OR `name` = 'PLAYER' LIMIT 1), `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :user_id AND `role_id` = :role_id"
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':role_id', $roleId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return true;
        });
    }

    /**
     * List users with pagination and filtering.
     *
     * @return array{items: list<array<string, mixed>>, total: int}
     */
    public function listUsers(
        int $page = 1,
        int $perPage = 20,
        ?string $search = null,
        ?string $roleCode = null,
        ?string $status = null
    ): array {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($page, $perPage, $search, $roleCode, $status): array {
            $where = ['1 = 1'];
            $params = [];

            if ($search !== null && trim($search) !== '') {
                $where[] = '(u.`full_name` LIKE :search OR u.`email` LIKE :search OR u.`phone` LIKE :search)';
                $params[':search'] = '%' . trim($search) . '%';
            }

            if ($roleCode !== null && trim($roleCode) !== '') {
                $where[] = 'r.`code` = :role_code';
                $params[':role_code'] = strtoupper(trim($roleCode));
            }

            if ($status !== null && trim($status) !== '') {
                $where[] = 'u.`status` = :status';
                $params[':status'] = strtoupper(trim($status));
            }

            $whereSql = implode(' AND ', $where);

            // Count total
            $countStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `users` u
                 LEFT JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE {$whereSql}"
            );
            foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
                $countStmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            }
            $countStmt->execute();
            $total = (int) $countStmt->fetchColumn();

            // Fetch items
            $offset = max(0, ($page - 1) * $perPage);
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT u.`id`, u.`role_id`, r.`code` AS `role_code`, r.`name` AS `role_name`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `name`, u.`full_name`, u.`email`,
                        u.`phone`, u.`avatar_url`, u.`status`, u.`email_verified_at`, u.`last_login_at`,
                        u.`created_at`, u.`updated_at`
                 FROM `users` u
                 LEFT JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE {$whereSql}
                 ORDER BY u.`id` ASC
                 LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset"
            );
            foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
                $stmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            }
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $perPage, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $items = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            return [
                'items' => $items,
                'total' => $total,
            ];
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find role by code or ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findRole(int|string $roleIdOrCode): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($roleIdOrCode): ?array {
            if (is_int($roleIdOrCode) || ctype_digit((string) $roleIdOrCode)) {
                $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `description` FROM `roles` WHERE `id` = :id LIMIT 1');
                $stmt->bindValue(':id', (int) $roleIdOrCode, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            } else {
                $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `description` FROM `roles` WHERE `code` = :code LIMIT 1');
                $stmt->bindValue(':code', strtoupper((string) $roleIdOrCode), PDO::PARAM_STR);
            }
            $stmt->execute();
            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Notification\Controllers\NotificationController.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Notification\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class NotificationController
{
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?NotificationRepository $notiRepo = null, ?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->notiRepo = $notiRepo ?? new NotificationRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/notifications
     * Query: limit, unread_only (0 or 1)
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $userId = (int) $auth['id'];
        $limit = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('limit', 20)));
        $unreadParam = $request->query('unread_only');
        $unreadOnly = in_array($unreadParam, ['1', 1, 'true', true], true);

        $notifications = $this->notiRepo->listByUserId($userId, $limit, $unreadOnly);

        return Response::success([
            'notifications' => $notifications,
            'count' => count($notifications),
        ], 'Danh sách thông báo');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/notifications/{id}/read
     */
    public function markAsRead(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $userId = (int) $auth['id'];
        $notiId = (int) $id;

        $updated = $this->notiRepo->markAsRead($notiId, $userId);

        if (!$updated) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy thông báo hoặc bạn không có quyền truy cập', 'NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        return Response::success(null, 'Đã đánh dấu thông báo là đã đọc');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/notifications/read-all
     */
    public function markAllAsRead(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $count = $this->notiRepo->markAllAsRead((int) $auth['id']);
        return Response::success(['updated_count' => $count], 'Đã đánh dấu tất cả thông báo là đã đọc');
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Notification\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class NotificationRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Create notification and fan out to recipients.
     *
     * @param int[] $recipientUserIds
     */
    public function createNotification(
        string $title,
        string $message,
        string $notificationType,
        ?int $senderId,
        array $recipientUserIds
    ): int {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($title, $message, $notificationType, $senderId, $recipientUserIds): int {
            $lastId = 0;
            $targets = !empty($recipientUserIds) ? $recipientUserIds : ($senderId !== null ? [$senderId] : []);

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `notifications` (`user_id`, `type`, `title`, `content`, `is_read`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:user_id, :type, :title, :content, 0, NOW())'
            );

            foreach ($targets as $uid) {
                $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $uid, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $stmt->bindValue(':type', $notificationType, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $stmt->bindValue(':title', $title, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $stmt->bindValue(':content', $message, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $stmt->execute();
                $lastId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
            }

            return $lastId;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get unread notifications for a specific user.
     *
     * @return array<int, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function getUnreadByUserId(int $userId, int $limit = 20): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId, $limit): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `user_id`, `type` AS `notification_type`, `type`, `title`,
                        `content` AS `message`, `content`, `is_read`, `read_at`, `created_at`
                 FROM `notifications`
                 WHERE `user_id` = :user_id AND `is_read` = 0
                 ORDER BY `created_at` DESC
                 LIMIT :limit'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', max(1, $limit), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Get all notifications for user (with option to filter unread only).
     *
     * @return array<int, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listByUserId(int $userId, int $limit = 50, bool $unreadOnly = false): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId, $limit, $unreadOnly): array {
            $sql = 'SELECT `id`, `user_id`, `type`, `title`, `content`, `reference_type`, `reference_id`, `is_read`, `read_at`, `created_at`
                    FROM `notifications`
                    WHERE `user_id` = :user_id';

            if ($unreadOnly) {
                $sql .= ' AND `is_read` = 0';
            }

            $sql .= ' ORDER BY `created_at` DESC LIMIT :limit';

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', max(1, min(100, $limit)), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Mark a notification as read (with user ID IDOR guard).
     */
    public function markAsRead(int $notificationId, int $userId): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($notificationId, $userId): bool {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `notifications`
                 SET `is_read` = 1, `read_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id AND `user_id` = :user_id'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $notificationId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->rowCount() > 0;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Mark all unread notifications for a user as read.
     */
    public function markAllAsRead(int $userId): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($userId): int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `notifications` SET `is_read` = 1, `read_at` = NOW() WHERE `user_id` = :user_id AND `is_read` = 0'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->rowCount();
        });
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers\AdminRefundController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class AdminRefundController
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null, ?NotificationRepository $notiRepo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->notiRepo = $notiRepo ?? new NotificationRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/admin/refunds
     * Query: page, per_page, status
     */
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $page = max(1, (int) $request->query('page', 1));
        $perPage = max(1, min(100, (int) $request->query('per_page', 20)));
        $offset = ($page - 1) * $perPage;
        $status = $request->query('status');

        $where = ['1 = 1'];
        $params = [];

        if ($status !== null && trim((string) $status) !== '' && $status !== 'ALL') {
            $where[] = 'r.`status` = :status';
            $params[':status'] = strtoupper(trim((string) $status));
        }

        $whereSql = implode(' AND ', $where);

        $cntStmt = $this->pdo->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `refunds` r WHERE {$whereSql}");
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $cntStmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        }
        $cntStmt->execute();
        $total = (int) $cntStmt->fetchColumn();

        $sql = "SELECT r.`id`, r.`player_payment_id`, r.`amount`, r.`reason`, r.`status`,
                       r.`requested_at`, r.`reviewed_by`, r.`reviewed_at`, r.`review_reason`,
                       p.`paid_amount`, rp.`user_id`, u.`full_name` AS `user_name`, u.`email` AS `user_email`,
                       gr.`id` AS `game_room_id`, gr.`title` AS `room_title`
                FROM `refunds` r
                INNER JOIN `player_payments` p ON p.`id` = r.`player_payment_id`
                INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = rp.`user_id`
                INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = rp.`game_room_id`
                WHERE {$whereSql}
                ORDER BY r.`id` DESC
                LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset";

        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $stmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        }
        $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $perPage, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();
        $items = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        return Response::paginated($items, $page, $perPage, $total, 'Danh sách yêu cầu hoàn tiền');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/admin/refunds/{id}/approve
     */
    public function approve(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $refundId = (int) $id;

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT r.`id`, r.`player_payment_id`, r.`amount`, r.`status`,
                        rp.`user_id`, gr.`title` AS `room_title`
                 FROM `refunds` r
                 INNER JOIN `player_payments` p ON p.`id` = r.`player_payment_id`
                 INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                 INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = rp.`game_room_id`
                 WHERE r.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $refundId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $refund = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($refund === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu hoàn tiền', 'REFUND_NOT_FOUND', 404);
            }

            if ($refund['status'] !== 'PENDING') {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail(
                    'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền này đã được xử lý trước đó (Trạng thái: ' . $refund['status'] . ')',
                    'INVALID_REFUND_STATUS',
                    409
                );
            }

            $upStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `refunds`
                 SET `status` = \'APPROVED\', `reviewed_by` = :reviewer, `reviewed_at` = NOW(), `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $upStmt->bindValue(':reviewer', (int) $auth['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upStmt->bindValue(':id', $refundId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upStmt->execute();

            $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền đã được duyệt',
                "Yêu cầu hoàn tiền " . number_format((int) $refund['amount']) . " VNĐ cho phòng '{$refund['room_title']}' đã được Quản trị viên phê duyệt.",
                'REFUND',
                (int) $auth['id'],
                [(int) $refund['user_id']]
            );

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success(['refund_id' => $refundId, 'status' => 'APPROVED'], 'Duyệt hoàn tiền thành công');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/admin/refunds/{id}/reject
     * Body: reason (string, required)
     */
    public function reject(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $refundId = (int) $id;
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $reason = trim((string) ($body['reason'] ?? ''));

        if ($reason === '') {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng nêu rõ lý do từ chối hoàn tiền', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT r.`id`, r.`amount`, r.`status`, rp.`user_id`, gr.`title` AS `room_title`
                 FROM `refunds` r
                 INNER JOIN `player_payments` p ON p.`id` = r.`player_payment_id`
                 INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                 INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = rp.`game_room_id`
                 WHERE r.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $refundId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $refund = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($refund === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy yêu cầu hoàn tiền', 'REFUND_NOT_FOUND', 404);
            }

            if ($refund['status'] !== 'PENDING') {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail(
                    'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền này đã được xử lý trước đó (Trạng thái: ' . $refund['status'] . ')',
                    'INVALID_REFUND_STATUS',
                    409
                );
            }

            $upStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `refunds`
                 SET `status` = \'REJECTED\', `reviewed_by` = :reviewer, `reviewed_at` = NOW(), `review_reason` = :reason, `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $upStmt->bindValue(':reviewer', (int) $auth['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upStmt->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $upStmt->bindValue(':id', $refundId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upStmt->execute();

            $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền bị từ chối',
                "Yêu cầu hoàn tiền cho phòng '{$refund['room_title']}' đã bị từ chối. Lý do: {$reason}",
                'REFUND',
                (int) $auth['id'],
                [(int) $refund['user_id']]
            );

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success(['refund_id' => $refundId, 'status' => 'REJECTED'], 'Đã từ chối hoàn tiền');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }
}
