<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Features\Pricing\Services\PricingService;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class PaymentController
{
    protected PaymentRepository $paymentRepo;
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(
        ?PaymentRepository $paymentRepo = null,
        ?NotificationRepository $notiRepo = null,
        ?PDO $pdo = null
    ) {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->paymentRepo = $paymentRepo ?? new PaymentRepository($this->pdo);
        $this->notiRepo = $notiRepo ?? new NotificationRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/rooms/{id}/payments
     * Player chỉ xem hóa đơn của chính mình; Admin/DM xem toàn bộ phòng.
     */
    public function listByRoom(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? 'PLAYER'));
        $filterUserId = in_array($userRole, ['ADMIN', 'DM'], true) ? null : (int) $auth['id'];

        $payments = $this->paymentRepo->listByRoomId($roomId, $filterUserId);

        return Response::success([
            'room_id' => $roomId,
            'payments' => $payments,
            'count' => count($payments),
        ], 'Danh sách khoản thanh toán của phòng');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/payments/{paymentId}/deposit
     */
    public function deposit(Request $request, string $paymentId): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $id = (int) $paymentId;
        $payment = $this->paymentRepo->findPaymentWithDetails($id);

        if ($payment === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy thông tin thanh toán', 'PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));

        // Chống IDOR: Chỉ chính người chơi hoặc Admin mới được thanh toán
        if ($userRole !== 'ADMIN' && (int) $payment['user_id'] !== (int) $auth['id']) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thanh toán cho người khác', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        if ($payment['status'] === 'PAID') {
            return Response::fail('Khoản thanh toán này đã được thanh toán đầy đủ', 'PAYMENT_ALREADY_COMPLETED', 400);
        }

        $idempotencyKey = $request->getHeader('x-idempotency-key')
            ?? $request->getHeader('idempotency-key')
            ?? (string) ($request->input('idempotency_key') ?? ('DEP_' . $id . '_' . bin2hex(random_bytes(8))));

        $totalAmount = (int) $payment['amount'];
        $alreadyPaid = (int) $payment['paid_amount'];
        $remaining = max(0, $totalAmount - $alreadyPaid);

        $bodyAmount = $request->input('amount');
        if ($bodyAmount !== null && (int) $bodyAmount > 0) {
            $depositAmount = min((int) $bodyAmount, $remaining);
        } else {
            // Quy chuẩn V16: Cọc cố định 50.000 VNĐ / người
            $depositAmount = min(PricingService::DEPOSIT_PER_PERSON, $remaining);
        }

        $method = (string) ($request->input('payment_method') ?? 'SIMULATED_MOMO');

        $result = $this->paymentRepo->processPayment($id, $depositAmount, $idempotencyKey, $method);

        // Tạo thông báo cho người dùng (chỉ gửi nếu giao dịch mới thành công)
        if (!empty($result['success']) && empty($result['idempotent_replay'])) {
            $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                'Thanh toán cọc thành công',
                "Bạn đã thanh toán cọc thành công số tiền " . number_format($depositAmount) . " VNĐ cho phòng " . ($payment['room_title'] ?? ''),
                'PAYMENT',
                null,
                [(int) $payment['user_id']]
            );
        }

        return Response::success($result, 'Thanh toán tiền cọc thành công');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/payments/{paymentId}/remaining
     */
    public function remaining(Request $request, string $paymentId): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $id = (int) $paymentId;
        $payment = $this->paymentRepo->findPaymentWithDetails($id);

        if ($payment === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy thông tin thanh toán', 'PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $userRole = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));

        // Chống IDOR
        if ($userRole !== 'ADMIN' && (int) $payment['user_id'] !== (int) $auth['id']) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thanh toán cho người khác', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        $totalAmount = (int) $payment['amount'];
        $alreadyPaid = (int) $payment['paid_amount'];
        $remaining = max(0, $totalAmount - $alreadyPaid);

        if ($remaining <= 0 || $payment['status'] === 'PAID') {
            return Response::fail('Khoản phí này đã được thanh toán toàn bộ', 'PAYMENT_ALREADY_COMPLETED', 400);
        }

        $idempotencyKey = $request->getHeader('x-idempotency-key')
            ?? $request->getHeader('idempotency-key')
            ?? (string) ($request->input('idempotency_key') ?? ('REM_' . $id . '_' . bin2hex(random_bytes(8))));

        $method = (string) ($request->input('payment_method') ?? 'SIMULATED_VNPAY');

        $result = $this->paymentRepo->processPayment($id, $remaining, $idempotencyKey, $method);

        if (!empty($result['success']) && empty($result['idempotent_replay'])) {
            $this->notiRepo->createNotification(
                'Thanh toán hoàn tất',
                "Bạn đã thanh toán thành công số tiền còn lại " . number_format($remaining) . " VNĐ cho phòng " . ($payment['room_title'] ?? ''),
                'PAYMENT',
                null,
                [(int) $payment['user_id']]
            );
        }

        return Response::success($result, 'Thanh toán số tiền còn lại thành công');
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class PaymentRefundRequestController
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected PaymentRepository $paymentRepo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null, ?PaymentRepository $paymentRepo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->paymentRepo = $paymentRepo ?? new PaymentRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/payments/{id}/refund-request
     */
    public function requestRefund(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $paymentId = (int) $id;
        $payment = $this->paymentRepo->findPaymentWithDetails($paymentId);

        if ($payment === null) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy khoản thanh toán', 'PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $roleCode = strtoupper((string) ($auth['role_code'] ?? ''));
        if ($roleCode !== 'ADMIN' && (int) $payment['user_id'] !== (int) $auth['id']) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn không có quyền yêu cầu hoàn tiền cho khoản thanh toán này', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        $paidAmount = (int) ($payment['paid_amount'] ?? 0);
        if ($paidAmount <= 0) {
            return Response::fail('Khoản thanh toán chưa có tiền đã nộp để hoàn lại', 'NO_PAID_AMOUNT', 400);
        }

        // Kiểm tra đã có yêu cầu hoàn tiền đang PENDING hoặc APPROVED chưa
        $dupStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `id` FROM `refunds` WHERE `player_payment_id` = :pid AND `status` IN (\'PENDING\', \'APPROVED\') LIMIT 1'
        );
        $dupStmt->bindValue(':pid', $paymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $dupStmt->execute();
        if ($dupStmt->fetchColumn() !== false) {
            return Response::fail('Đã tồn tại yêu cầu hoàn tiền đang chờ xử lý hoặc đã duyệt cho khoản này', 'DUPLICATE_REFUND_REQUEST', 409);
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $reason = trim((string) ($body['reason'] ?? 'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền từ người chơi'));
        if ($reason === '') {
            $reason = 'Yêu cầu hoàn tiền từ người chơi';
        }

        // Kiểm tra mốc 24h trước giờ chơi nếu có session
        $roomId = (int) $payment['game_room_id'];
        $sessStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `start_at` FROM `sessions` WHERE `game_room_id` = :rid AND `status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\') ORDER BY `id` DESC LIMIT 1'
        );
        $sessStmt->bindValue(':rid', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $sessStmt->execute();
        $startAt = $sessStmt->fetchColumn();

        if ($startAt !== false && $startAt !== null) {
            $diffHours = (strtotime((string) $startAt) - time()) / 3600;
            if ($diffHours < 24 && empty($body['force_refund'])) {
                return Response::fail(
                    'Chỉ có thể yêu cầu hoàn tiền trước giờ chơi ít nhất 24 giờ',
                    'REFUND_WINDOW_EXPIRED',
                    422
                );
            }
        }

        $refundId = $this->paymentRepo->createRefund(
            $paymentId,
            (int) $payment['room_participant_id'],
            $paidAmount,
            $reason,
            'FULL',
            (int) $auth['id']
        );

        return Response::success([
            'refund_id' => $refundId,
            'player_payment_id' => $paymentId,
            'amount' => $paidAmount,
            'status' => 'PENDING',
        ], 'Đã gửi yêu cầu hoàn tiền thành công', null, 201);
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class PaymentRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find payment record by ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findPaymentById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `room_participant_id`, `payment_type`, `amount` AS `amount_due`, `amount`,
                        `paid_amount` AS `amount_paid`, `paid_amount`, `status`,
                        `deadline_at` AS `deadline`, `deadline_at`, `paid_at`, `expired_at`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `player_payments`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * List payments belonging to a game room (optionally filtered by user ID).
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listByRoomId(int $roomId, ?int $userId = null): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($roomId, $userId): array {
            $sql = 'SELECT p.`id`, p.`room_participant_id`, p.`payment_type`, p.`amount`, p.`paid_amount`,
                           p.`status`, p.`deadline_at`, p.`paid_at`, p.`expired_at`, p.`created_at`, p.`updated_at`,
                           rp.`user_id`, rp.`game_room_id`, rp.`status` AS `participant_status`,
                           u.`full_name` AS `user_name`, u.`email` AS `user_email`
                    FROM `player_payments` p
                    INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                    INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = rp.`user_id`
                    WHERE rp.`game_room_id` = :room_id';

            if ($userId !== null && $userId > 0) {
                $sql .= ' AND rp.`user_id` = :user_id';
            }

            $sql .= ' ORDER BY p.`id` ASC';

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            if ($userId !== null && $userId > 0) {
                $stmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            }
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Ensure DEPOSIT payment records exist for all active participants of a room.
     */
    public function ensureDepositPaymentsForRoom(int $roomId, int $depositAmount = 50000, int $deadlineHours = 2): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($roomId, $depositAmount, $deadlineHours): int {
            $deadline = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime("+{$deadlineHours} hours"));

            $pStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id` FROM `room_participants`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id
                   AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\')'
            );
            $pStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $pStmt->execute();
            $participantIds = $pStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);

            $created = 0;
            $insStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT IGNORE INTO `player_payments`
                    (`room_participant_id`, `payment_type`, `amount`, `paid_amount`, `status`, `deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES
                    (:part_id, \'DEPOSIT\', :amount, 0, \'PENDING\', :deadline, NOW(), NOW())'
            );
            $upPartStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `room_participants`
                 SET `deposit_deadline_at` = COALESCE(`deposit_deadline_at`, :deadline), `updated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :part_id AND `status` = \'PENDING_DEPOSIT\''
            );

            foreach ($participantIds as $partId) {
                $pid = (int) $partId;
                $insStmt->bindValue(':part_id', $pid, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $insStmt->bindValue(':amount', $depositAmount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $insStmt->bindValue(':deadline', $deadline, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $insStmt->execute();
                if ($insStmt->rowCount() > 0) {
                    $created++;
                }

                $upPartStmt->bindValue(':deadline', $deadline, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $upPartStmt->bindValue(':part_id', $pid, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upPartStmt->execute();
            }

            return $created;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create player payment record.
     *
     * @param array{
     *     room_participant_id: int,
     *     session_id?: int,
     *     payment_type: string,
     *     amount_due?: int,
     *     amount?: int,
     *     paid_amount?: int,
     *     status?: string,
     *     deadline?: string|null,
     *     deadline_at?: string|null
     * } $data
     */
    public function createPayment(array $data): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($data): int {
            $amount = $data['amount'] ?? $data['amount_due'] ?? 0;
            $deadline = $data['deadline_at'] ?? $data['deadline'] ?? null;

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `player_payments` (`room_participant_id`, `payment_type`,
                                                `amount`, `paid_amount`, `status`, `deadline_at`,
                                                `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:room_participant_id, :payment_type,
                         :amount, :paid_amount, :status, :deadline_at,
                         NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':room_participant_id', $data['room_participant_id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':payment_type', $data['payment_type'], PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':amount', $amount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':paid_amount', $data['paid_amount'] ?? 0, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':status', $data['status'] ?? 'PENDING', PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':deadline_at', $deadline, $deadline === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create an idempotent payment transaction.
     */
    public function createAttempt(int $playerPaymentId, string $idempotencyKey, int $amount, string $paymentMethod = 'SIMULATED'): int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($playerPaymentId, $idempotencyKey, $amount, $paymentMethod): int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `payment_transactions` (`player_payment_id`, `idempotency_key`, `transaction_reference`,
                                                     `amount`, `status`, `provider_response`, `requested_at`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:player_payment_id, :idempotency_key, :tx_ref,
                         :amount, \'PENDING\', :provider_response, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $txRef = 'TX_' . strtoupper(bin2hex(random_bytes(8)));
            $metadata = json_encode(['method' => $paymentMethod]);

            $stmt->bindValue(':player_payment_id', $playerPaymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':idempotency_key', $idempotencyKey, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':tx_ref', $txRef, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':amount', $amount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':provider_response', $metadata, PDO::PARAM_STR);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find payment transaction by idempotency key.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findAttemptByIdempotencyKey(string $idempotencyKey): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($idempotencyKey): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `player_payment_id`, `transaction_reference`, `idempotency_key`,
                        `amount`, `status`, `provider_response`, `requested_at`, `processed_at`, `created_at`
                 FROM `payment_transactions`
                 WHERE `idempotency_key` = :idempotency_key
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':idempotency_key', $idempotencyKey, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create refund record.
     */
    public function createRefund(
        int $playerPaymentId,
        int $roomParticipantId,
        int $refundAmount,
        string $reason,
        string $refundType = 'FULL',
        ?int $processedByUserId = null
    ): int {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use (
            $playerPaymentId,
            $refundAmount,
            $reason,
            $processedByUserId
        ): int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `refunds` (`player_payment_id`, `amount`, `reason`, `status`, `requested_at`, `reviewed_by`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                 VALUES (:player_payment_id, :amount, :reason, \'PENDING\', NOW(), :reviewed_by, NOW(), NOW())'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':player_payment_id', $playerPaymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':amount', $refundAmount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':reason', $reason, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':reviewed_by', $processedByUserId, $processedByUserId === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_INT);

            $stmt->execute();

            return (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find payment with participant and room details.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findPaymentWithDetails(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT p.`id`, p.`room_participant_id`, p.`payment_type`, p.`amount`, p.`paid_amount`,
                        p.`status`, p.`deadline_at`, p.`paid_at`, p.`expired_at`, p.`created_at`, p.`updated_at`,
                        rp.`user_id`, rp.`game_room_id`, rp.`status` AS `participant_status`,
                        u.`full_name` AS `user_name`, u.`email` AS `user_email`,
                        gr.`title` AS `room_title`
                 FROM `player_payments` p
                 INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = p.`room_participant_id`
                 INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = rp.`user_id`
                 INNER JOIN `game_rooms` gr ON gr.`id` = rp.`game_room_id`
                 WHERE p.`id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Process an idempotent payment with row-level FOR UPDATE lock.
     *
     * @return array{success: bool, payment: array<string, mixed>, transaction: array<string, mixed>, idempotent_replay?: bool}
     */
    public function processPayment(int $paymentId, int $payAmount, string $idempotencyKey, string $method = 'SIMULATED'): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($paymentId, $payAmount, $idempotencyKey, $method): array {
            $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
            try {
                // 1. Check existing idempotency key inside transaction
                $existingTx = $this->findAttemptByIdempotencyKey($idempotencyKey);
                if ($existingTx !== null) {
                    $this->pdo->commit();
                    $payment = $this->findPaymentWithDetails($paymentId);
                    return [
                        'success' => $existingTx['status'] === 'SUCCESS',
                        'payment' => $payment ?? [],
                        'transaction' => $existingTx,
                        'idempotent_replay' => true,
                    ];
                }

                // 2. Lock player_payments row FOR UPDATE
                $lockStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'SELECT `id`, `room_participant_id`, `amount`, `paid_amount`, `status`
                     FROM `player_payments`
                     WHERE `id` = :id
                     FOR UPDATE'
                );
                $lockStmt->bindValue(':id', $paymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $lockStmt->execute();
                $lockedPayment = $lockStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

                if ($lockedPayment === false) {
                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Không tìm thấy thông tin khoản thanh toán');
                }

                $currentPaid = (int) $lockedPayment['paid_amount'];
                $totalAmount = (int) $lockedPayment['amount'];
                $remaining = max(0, $totalAmount - $currentPaid);

                if ($remaining <= 0 || $lockedPayment['status'] === 'PAID') {
                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Khoản thanh toán này đã được thanh toán đầy đủ');
                }

                $effectivePay = min($payAmount, $remaining);
                $newPaid = $currentPaid + $effectivePay;
                $newStatus = $newPaid >= $totalAmount ? 'PAID' : 'PARTIALLY_PAID';
                $txRef = 'TX_' . strtoupper(bin2hex(random_bytes(8)));
                $metadata = json_encode(['method' => $method, 'pay_amount' => $effectivePay]);

                // 3. Insert transaction
                $txStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'INSERT INTO `payment_transactions` (`player_payment_id`, `idempotency_key`, `transaction_reference`,
                                                         `amount`, `status`, `provider_response`, `requested_at`, `processed_at`, `created_at`)
                     VALUES (:payment_id, :idempotency_key, :tx_ref,
                             :amount, \'SUCCESS\', :provider_response, NOW(), NOW(), NOW())'
                );
                $txStmt->bindValue(':payment_id', $paymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $txStmt->bindValue(':idempotency_key', $idempotencyKey, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $txStmt->bindValue(':tx_ref', $txRef, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $txStmt->bindValue(':amount', $effectivePay, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $txStmt->bindValue(':provider_response', $metadata, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $txStmt->execute();
                $txId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

                // 4. Update payment
                $upStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `player_payments`
                     SET `paid_amount` = :paid_amount,
                         `status` = :status,
                         `paid_at` = CASE WHEN :is_paid = 1 THEN NOW() ELSE `paid_at` END,
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `id` = :id'
                );
                $upStmt->bindValue(':paid_amount', $newPaid, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upStmt->bindValue(':status', $newStatus, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                $upStmt->bindValue(':is_paid', $newStatus === 'PAID' ? 1 : 0, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upStmt->bindValue(':id', $paymentId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $upStmt->execute();

                // 5. Update participant status to DEPOSITED + deposited_at
                $partId = (int) $lockedPayment['room_participant_id'];
                $partUp = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE `room_participants`
                     SET `status` = \'DEPOSITED\',
                         `deposited_at` = COALESCE(`deposited_at`, NOW()),
                         `updated_at` = NOW()
                     WHERE `id` = :part_id AND `status` = \'PENDING_DEPOSIT\''
                );
                $partUp->bindValue(':part_id', $partId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $partUp->execute();

                $this->pdo->commit();

                $updatedPayment = $this->findPaymentWithDetails($paymentId);
                $txRecord = [
                    'id' => $txId,
                    'transaction_reference' => $txRef,
                    'amount' => $effectivePay,
                    'status' => 'SUCCESS',
                    'idempotency_key' => $idempotencyKey,
                ];

                return [
                    'success' => true,
                    'payment' => $updatedPayment ?? [],
                    'transaction' => $txRecord,
                ];
            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $this->pdo->rollBack();
                }
                throw $e;
            }
        });
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\Pricing\Controllers\PricingController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Pricing\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\Pricing\Services\PricingService;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class PricingController
{
    protected PricingService $pricingService;
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?PricingService $pricingService = null, ?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->pricingService = $pricingService ?? new PricingService($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/rooms/{id}/calculate-price
     * Body: play_time (datetime string, optional), promotion_code (string, optional)
     */
    public function calculate(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $body = $request->getBodyParams();

        $playTime = isset($body['play_time']) ? trim((string) $body['play_time']) : null;
        $promotionCode = isset($body['promotion_code']) ? trim((string) $body['promotion_code']) : null;

        try {
            $calculation = $this->pricingService->calculateAndSnapshot($roomId, $playTime, $promotionCode);
            return Response::success($calculation, 'Tính toán bảng giá và chốt snapshot thành công');
        } catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
            return Response::fail($e->getMessage(), 'NOT_FOUND', 404);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return Response::fail($e->getMessage(), 'CALCULATION_ERROR', 500);
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/rooms/{id}/price-snapshot
     */
    public function snapshot(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT gr.`id`, gr.`price_total_participants`, gr.`price_subtotal`, gr.`price_discount_amount`,
                    gr.`price_final_amount`, gr.`price_deposit_total`, gr.`price_remaining_total`,
                    gr.`price_promotion_id`, gr.`price_details`, gr.`price_calculated_at`,
                    p.`code` AS `promotion_code`, p.`name` AS `promotion_name`
             FROM `game_rooms` gr
             LEFT JOIN `promotions` p ON p.`id` = gr.`price_promotion_id`
             WHERE gr.`id` = :room_id
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $stmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();
        $room = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($room === false || empty($room['price_calculated_at'])) {
            return Response::fail('Phòng chơi chưa có snapshot giá hoặc không tồn tại', 'SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        if (!empty($room['price_details'])) {
            $room['price_details'] = json_decode((string) $room['price_details'], true);
        }

        return Response::success([
            'snapshot' => $room,
        ], 'Thông tin snapshot bảng giá phòng chơi');
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Pricing\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class PricingRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find active prices for a game.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function findPricesByGame(int $gameId): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameId): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_id`, `price_per_person` AS `base_price_per_player`, `price_per_person`,
                        `start_time` AS `time_from`, `end_time` AS `time_to`, `start_time`, `end_time`,
                        `effective_from`, `effective_until`, `status`,
                        (CASE WHEN `status` = \'ACTIVE\' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS `is_active`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `game_prices`
                 WHERE `game_id` = :game_id AND `status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                 ORDER BY `start_time` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_id', $gameId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            return $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find booking price snapshot by game room ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findSnapshotByGameRoomId(int $gameRoomId): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($gameRoomId): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id` AS `game_room_id`, `price_total_participants`, `price_subtotal`,
                        `price_discount_amount`, `price_final_amount`, `price_deposit_total`,
                        `price_remaining_total`, `price_promotion_id`, `price_details`, `price_calculated_at`
                 FROM `game_rooms`
                 WHERE `id` = :game_room_id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':game_room_id', $gameRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\Pricing\Services\PricingService.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Pricing\Services;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use InvalidArgumentException;
use PDO;

class PricingService
{
    public const DEPOSIT_PER_PERSON = 50000; // Quy chuẩn cố định 50.000 VNĐ / người của V16

    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
    }

    /**
     * Calculate and snapshot room pricing according to V16 rules.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function calculateAndSnapshot(
        int $roomId,
        ?string $playTime = null,
        ?string $promotionCode = null
    ): array {
        // 1. Lấy thông tin phòng và game
        $rStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT gr.`id`, gr.`game_id`, gr.`title`, gr.`min_players`, gr.`max_players`, gr.`status`,
                    g.`name` AS `game_name`
             FROM `game_rooms` gr
             INNER JOIN `games` g ON g.`id` = gr.`game_id`
             WHERE gr.`id` = :room_id
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $rStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $rStmt->execute();
        $room = $rStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($room === false) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Phòng chơi không tồn tại');
        }

        // 2. Lấy danh sách thành viên active
        $pStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `id`, `user_id` FROM `room_participants`
             WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id
               AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')'
        );
        $pStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $pStmt->execute();
        $participants = $pStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $totalParticipants = count($participants);
        if ($totalParticipants === 0) {
            $totalParticipants = (int) $room['min_players'];
        }

        // 3. Xác định mốc thời gian chơi
        $playDateTime = $playTime;
        if ($playDateTime === null || trim($playDateTime) === '') {
            // Thử lấy từ session hoặc confirmed proposal
            $sessStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `start_at` FROM `sessions` WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id LIMIT 1');
            $sessStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $sessStmt->execute();
            $sessStart = $sessStmt->fetchColumn();

            if ($sessStart !== false && $sessStart !== null) {
                $playDateTime = (string) $sessStart;
            } else {
                $propStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `proposed_start_at` FROM `schedule_proposals` WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'CONFIRMED\' LIMIT 1');
                $propStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $propStmt->execute();
                $propStart = $propStmt->fetchColumn();
                $playDateTime = $propStart !== false && $propStart !== null ? (string) $propStart : date('Y-m-d 18:00:00');
            }
        }

        $timeOfDay = date('H:i:s', strtotime($playDateTime));
        $dayOfWeek = (int) date('N', strtotime($playDateTime)); // 1 (Mon) -> 7 (Sun)

        // 4. Tìm bảng giá game_prices theo khung giờ (09:00 - 19:59 vs từ 20:00)
        $priceStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `price_per_person`, `start_time`, `end_time`
             FROM `game_prices`
             WHERE `game_id` = :game_id AND `status` = \'ACTIVE\'
               AND :t1 >= `start_time` AND :t2 <= `end_time`
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $priceStmt->bindValue(':game_id', (int) $room['game_id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $priceStmt->bindValue(':t1', $timeOfDay, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        $priceStmt->bindValue(':t2', $timeOfDay, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        $priceStmt->execute();
        $priceRecord = $priceStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        $basePricePerPerson = $priceRecord !== false ? (int) $priceRecord['price_per_person'] : 250000;
        $subtotal = $basePricePerPerson * $totalParticipants;

        // Tiền cọc: Cố định 50.000 VNĐ / người
        $depositPerPerson = self::DEPOSIT_PER_PERSON;
        $depositTotal = $depositPerPerson * $totalParticipants;

        // 5. Xử lý khuyến mãi
        $discountAmount = 0;
        $appliedPromotion = null;
        $promoId = null;

        if ($promotionCode !== null && trim($promotionCode) !== '') {
            $codeUpper = strtoupper(trim($promotionCode));
            $promoStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `promotion_type`, `discount_type`, `discount_value`, `rules`
                 FROM `promotions`
                 WHERE (`code` = :code OR `code` LIKE :code_like) AND `is_active` = 1
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $promoStmt->bindValue(':code', $codeUpper, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $promoStmt->bindValue(':code_like', $codeUpper . '%', PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $promoStmt->execute();
            $promo = $promoStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($promo !== false) {
                $rules = !empty($promo['rules']) ? json_decode((string) $promo['rules'], true) : [];
                $isEligible = true;

                // Kiểm tra ngày trong tuần nếu có rule
                if (!empty($rules['weekdays']) && is_array($rules['weekdays'])) {
                    if (!in_array($dayOfWeek, $rules['weekdays'], true)) {
                        $isEligible = false; // Ví dụ mã Thứ 3 mà đặt ngày khác
                    }
                }

                if ($isEligible) {
                    $promoId = (int) $promo['id'];
                    $appliedPromotion = [
                        'id' => $promoId,
                        'code' => $promo['code'],
                        'name' => $promo['name'],
                        'discount_type' => $promo['discount_type'],
                        'discount_value' => (int) $promo['discount_value'],
                    ];

                    if ($promo['discount_type'] === 'PERCENT') {
                        $percent = (int) $promo['discount_value'];
                        $discountAmount = (int) round(($subtotal * $percent) / 100);
                    } elseif ($promo['discount_type'] === 'FIXED') {
                        $perPersonDiscount = (int) ($rules['discount_per_eligible_person'] ?? $promo['discount_value']);
                        $discountAmount = $perPersonDiscount * $totalParticipants;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Không cho discount vượt quá tiền còn lại sau cọc (luôn phải cọc đủ 50k/người)
        $maxDiscountAllowed = max(0, $subtotal - $depositTotal);
        $discountAmount = min($discountAmount, $maxDiscountAllowed);

        $finalAmount = max($depositTotal, $subtotal - $discountAmount);
        $remainingTotal = max(0, $finalAmount - $depositTotal);

        $perPlayerFinal = (int) round($finalAmount / $totalParticipants);
        $perPlayerRemaining = (int) round($remainingTotal / $totalParticipants);
        $perPlayerDiscount = (int) round($discountAmount / $totalParticipants);

        $detailsJson = json_encode([
            'room_id' => $roomId,
            'game_id' => (int) $room['game_id'],
            'game_name' => $room['game_name'],
            'play_datetime' => $playDateTime,
            'time_of_day' => $timeOfDay,
            'is_peak_hours' => strtotime($timeOfDay) >= strtotime('20:00:00'),
            'base_price_per_person' => $basePricePerPerson,
            'deposit_per_person' => $depositPerPerson,
            'per_player_discount' => $perPlayerDiscount,
            'per_player_final' => $perPlayerFinal,
            'per_player_remaining' => $perPlayerRemaining,
            'applied_promotion' => $appliedPromotion,
            'calculated_at' => date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
        ]);

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            // Cập nhật snapshot vào game_rooms
            $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `game_rooms`
                 SET `price_total_participants` = :total_parts,
                     `price_subtotal` = :subtotal,
                     `price_discount_amount` = :discount,
                     `price_final_amount` = :final_amount,
                     `price_deposit_total` = :deposit_total,
                     `price_remaining_total` = :remaining_total,
                     `price_promotion_id` = :promo_id,
                     `price_details` = :details,
                     `price_calculated_at` = NOW()
                 WHERE `id` = :room_id'
            );
            $upRoom->bindValue(':total_parts', $totalParticipants, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':subtotal', $subtotal, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':discount', $discountAmount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':final_amount', $finalAmount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':deposit_total', $depositTotal, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':remaining_total', $remainingTotal, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':promo_id', $promoId, $promoId === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':details', $detailsJson, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $upRoom->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upRoom->execute();

            // Cập nhật snapshot cho từng participant
            if (!empty($participants)) {
                $snapStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                    'INSERT INTO `participant_price_snapshots`
                        (`game_room_id`, `room_participant_id`, `base_price`, `discount_amount`, `final_amount`, `deposit_amount`, `remaining_amount`, `promotion_id`, `created_at`)
                     VALUES
                        (:room_id, :part_id, :base_price, :discount, :final_amount, :deposit, :remaining, :promo_id, NOW())
                     ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                        `base_price` = VALUES(`base_price`),
                        `discount_amount` = VALUES(`discount_amount`),
                        `final_amount` = VALUES(`final_amount`),
                        `deposit_amount` = VALUES(`deposit_amount`),
                        `remaining_amount` = VALUES(`remaining_amount`),
                        `promotion_id` = VALUES(`promotion_id`)'
                );

                foreach ($participants as $part) {
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':part_id', (int) $part['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':base_price', $basePricePerPerson, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':discount', $perPlayerDiscount, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':final_amount', $perPlayerFinal, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':deposit', $depositPerPerson, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':remaining', $perPlayerRemaining, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->bindValue(':promo_id', $promoId, $promoId === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $snapStmt->execute();
                }
            }

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return [
                'game_room_id' => $roomId,
                'game_name' => $room['game_name'],
                'play_datetime' => $playDateTime,
                'total_participants' => $totalParticipants,
                'base_price_per_person' => $basePricePerPerson,
                'subtotal' => $subtotal,
                'discount_amount' => $discountAmount,
                'final_amount' => $finalAmount,
                'deposit_per_person' => $depositPerPerson,
                'deposit_total' => $depositTotal,
                'remaining_total' => $remainingTotal,
                'per_player_summary' => [
                    'base_price' => $basePricePerPerson,
                    'discount' => $perPlayerDiscount,
                    'deposit_required' => $depositPerPerson,
                    'remaining_due' => $perPlayerRemaining,
                    'total_per_player' => $perPlayerFinal,
                ],
                'applied_promotion' => $appliedPromotion,
            ];
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }
}

        ], 'Danh sách chương trình khuyến mãi');
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\Promotion\Repositories\PromotionRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\Promotion\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class PromotionRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find active promotion by unique promo code.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findByCode(string $code): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($code): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `promotion_type`, `discount_type`, `discount_value`,
                        `start_at` AS `valid_from`, `end_at` AS `valid_to`, `start_at`, `end_at`,
                        `rules`, `is_active`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `promotions`
                 WHERE `code` = :code AND `is_active` = 1
                   AND (`start_at` <= NOW())
                   AND (`end_at` >= NOW())
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':code', $code, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find promotion by ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `promotion_type`, `discount_type`, `discount_value`,
                        `start_at` AS `valid_from`, `end_at` AS `valid_to`, `start_at`, `end_at`,
                        `rules`, `is_active`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `promotions`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * List all active promotions with decoded rules.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listActive(): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function (): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `code`, `name`, `promotion_type`, `discount_type`, `discount_value`,
                        `start_at` AS `valid_from`, `end_at` AS `valid_to`, `start_at`, `end_at`,
                        `rules`, `is_active`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `promotions`
                 WHERE `is_active` = 1
                   AND (`start_at` IS NULL OR `start_at` <= NOW())
                   AND (`end_at` IS NULL OR `end_at` >= NOW())
                 ORDER BY `id` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            foreach ($rows as &$row) {
                if (!empty($row['rules']) && is_string($row['rules'])) {
                    $row['rules'] = json_decode($row['rules'], true);
                }
            }
            return $rows;
        });
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers\SchedulingController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Features\Pricing\Services\PricingService;
use App\Features\VenueScheduling\Repositories\SchedulingRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;

class SchedulingController
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected SchedulingRepository $schedRepo;
    protected PaymentRepository $paymentRepo;

    public function __construct(
        ?PDO $pdo = null,
        ?SchedulingRepository $schedRepo = null,
        ?PaymentRepository $paymentRepo = null
    ) {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->schedRepo = $schedRepo ?? new SchedulingRepository($this->pdo);
        $this->paymentRepo = $paymentRepo ?? new PaymentRepository($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/venues
     * Lấy danh sách chi nhánh và phòng chơi vật lý
     */
    public function venues(Request $request): Response
    {
        $stmt = $this->pdo->query('SELECT `id`, `name`, `address`, `phone`, `description`, `status` FROM `venues` WHERE `status` = \'ACTIVE\'');
        $venues = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        foreach ($venues as &$v) {
            $rStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `venue_id`, `name`, `capacity`, `description`, `status` FROM `physical_rooms` WHERE `venue_id` = :vid AND `status` = \'ACTIVE\'');
            $rStmt->bindValue(':vid', (int) $v['id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $rStmt->execute();
            $v['rooms'] = $rStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        }

        return Response::success([
            'venues' => $venues,
        ], 'Danh sách chi nhánh và phòng chơi vật lý');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/rooms/{id}/proposals
     * Tạo đề xuất mốc thời gian chơi
     */
    public function propose(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $userId = $this->resolveCurrentUserId($request);
        if ($userId === null) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn cần đăng nhập để đề xuất lịch chơi', 'UNAUTHORIZED', 401);
        }

        $roomId = (int) $id;
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();

        $startAt = trim((string) ($body['proposed_start_at'] ?? ''));
        $endAt = trim((string) ($body['proposed_end_at'] ?? ''));

        if ($startAt === '' || $endAt === '') {
            return Response::fail('Vui lòng chọn thời gian bắt đầu và kết thúc', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        if (strtotime($startAt) >= strtotime($endAt)) {
            return Response::fail('Thời gian bắt đầu phải trước thời gian kết thúc', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        // Kiểm tra xem user có phải là thành viên active trong phòng không
        $partStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT `id` FROM `room_participants`
             WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `user_id` = :user_id
               AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $partStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $partStmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $partStmt->execute();
        $participantId = $partStmt->fetchColumn();

        if ($participantId === false) {
            return Response::fail('Chỉ thành viên trong phòng mới có thể đề xuất lịch', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
        }

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            // Hủy đề xuất cũ đang mở (nếu có)
            $cancelStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `schedule_proposals` SET `status` = \'CANCELLED\' WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'PROPOSED\'');
            $cancelStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $cancelStmt->execute();

            $expiresAt = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+24 hours'));

            $insProp = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `schedule_proposals` (`game_room_id`, `proposed_by`, `proposed_start_at`, `proposed_end_at`, `status`, `expires_at`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:room_id, :user_id, :start_at, :end_at, \'PROPOSED\', :expires_at, NOW())'
            );
            $insProp->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $insProp->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $insProp->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $insProp->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $insProp->bindValue(':expires_at', $expiresAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $insProp->execute();

            $proposalId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

            // Tự động ghi nhận vote ACCEPTED của người đề xuất
            $insVote = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `schedule_responses` (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `room_participant_id`, `response`, `responded_at`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:room_id, :proposal_id, :part_id, \'ACCEPTED\', NOW(), NOW())'
            );
            $insVote->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $insVote->bindValue(':proposal_id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $insVote->bindValue(':part_id', (int) $participantId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $insVote->execute();

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success([
                'proposal_id' => $proposalId,
                'game_room_id' => $roomId,
                'proposed_start_at' => $startAt,
                'proposed_end_at' => $endAt,
                'status' => 'PROPOSED',
                'expires_at' => $expiresAt,
            ], 'Đã tạo đề xuất lịch chơi và mở biểu quyết cho thành viên phòng', null, 201);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/v1/proposals/{id}
     * Xem chi tiết đề xuất lịch và các phiếu bầu
     */
    public function showProposal(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $proposalId = (int) $id;
        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT sp.`id`, sp.`game_room_id`, sp.`proposed_by`, sp.`proposed_start_at`, sp.`proposed_end_at`,
                    sp.`status`, sp.`expires_at`, sp.`confirmed_at`, sp.`rejected_at`, sp.`created_at`,
                    u.`full_name` AS `proposer_name`
             FROM `schedule_proposals` sp
             INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = sp.`proposed_by`
             WHERE sp.`id` = :id
             LIMIT 1'
        );
        $stmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();
        $proposal = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        if ($proposal === false) {
            return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy đề xuất lịch chơi', 'PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        }

        $vStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            'SELECT sr.`id`, sr.`room_participant_id`, sr.`response`, sr.`responded_at`,
                    u.`full_name` AS `participant_name`
             FROM `schedule_responses` sr
             INNER JOIN `room_participants` rp ON rp.`id` = sr.`room_participant_id`
             INNER JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = rp.`user_id`
             WHERE sr.`schedule_proposal_id` = :pid'
        );
        $vStmt->bindValue(':pid', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $vStmt->execute();
        $proposal['votes'] = $vStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        return Response::success([
            'proposal' => $proposal,
        ], 'Chi tiết đề xuất lịch chơi');
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/proposals/{id}/vote
     * Thành viên biểu quyết đề xuất lịch (bọc transaction, tìm phòng vật lý & DM động, sinh hóa đơn cọc 50k)
     */
    public function vote(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $userId = $this->resolveCurrentUserId($request);
        if ($userId === null) {
            return Response::fail('Bạn cần đăng nhập để biểu quyết', 'UNAUTHORIZED', 401);
        }

        $proposalId = (int) $id;
        $body = $request->getBodyParams();
        $responseVote = strtoupper(trim((string) ($body['response'] ?? 'ACCEPTED')));

        if (!in_array($responseVote, ['ACCEPTED', 'REJECTED'], true)) {
            return Response::fail('Lựa chọn biểu quyết không hợp lệ (phải là ACCEPTED hoặc REJECTED)', 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 422);
        }

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            // Khóa proposal FOR UPDATE
            $pStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `status`, `proposed_start_at`, `proposed_end_at`, `expires_at`
                 FROM `schedule_proposals`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE'
            );
            $pStmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $pStmt->execute();
            $proposal = $pStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($proposal === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Đề xuất lịch chơi không tồn tại', 'PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND', 404);
            }

            if ($proposal['status'] !== 'PROPOSED') {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Đề xuất lịch này đã kết thúc hoặc bị hủy (Trạng thái: ' . $proposal['status'] . ')', 'PROPOSAL_CLOSED', 400);
            }

            // Kiểm tra hạn biểu quyết
            if (!empty($proposal['expires_at']) && strtotime((string) $proposal['expires_at']) < time()) {
                $expStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `schedule_proposals` SET `status` = \'EXPIRED\' WHERE `id` = :id');
                $expStmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $expStmt->execute();
                $this->pdo->commit();
                return Response::fail('Đề xuất lịch chơi này đã hết hạn biểu quyết', 'PROPOSAL_EXPIRED', 400);
            }

            $roomId = (int) $proposal['game_room_id'];

            // Khóa game_room FOR UPDATE
            $roomLock = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = :room_id FOR UPDATE');
            $roomLock->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $roomLock->execute();

            // Lấy participant của user trong phòng
            $partStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id` FROM `room_participants`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `user_id` = :user_id
                   AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $partStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $partStmt->bindValue(':user_id', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $partStmt->execute();
            $participantId = $partStmt->fetchColumn();

            if ($participantId === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Bạn không phải là thành viên hợp lệ trong phòng để biểu quyết', 'FORBIDDEN', 403);
            }

            $participantId = (int) $participantId;

            // Ghi nhận hoặc cập nhật vote
            $voteStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `schedule_responses` (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `room_participant_id`, `response`, `responded_at`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:room_id, :proposal_id, :part_id, :response, NOW(), NOW())
                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE `response` = VALUES(`response`), `responded_at` = NOW()'
            );
            $voteStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $voteStmt->bindValue(':proposal_id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $voteStmt->bindValue(':part_id', $participantId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $voteStmt->bindValue(':response', $responseVote, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $voteStmt->execute();

            // Nếu REJECTED -> bác bỏ proposal ngay
            if ($responseVote === 'REJECTED') {
                $rejStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `schedule_proposals` SET `status` = \'REJECTED\', `rejected_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id');
                $rejStmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $rejStmt->execute();
                $this->pdo->commit();

                return Response::success([
                    'proposal_status' => 'REJECTED',
                ], 'Bạn đã từ chối mốc thời gian này. Đề xuất lịch đã được đánh dấu REJECTED để thành viên đề xuất mốc khác.');
            }

            // Đếm số active participants và số phiếu ACCEPTED
            $allPartsStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id` FROM `room_participants`
                 WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id
                   AND `status` IN (\'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', \'DEPOSITED\', \'LEAVE_REQUESTED\')'
            );
            $allPartsStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $allPartsStmt->execute();
            $activeParticipantIds = $allPartsStmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);

            $acceptStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `schedule_responses`
                 WHERE `schedule_proposal_id` = :pid AND `response` = \'ACCEPTED\''
            );
            $acceptStmt->bindValue(':pid', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $acceptStmt->execute();
            $acceptedCount = (int) $acceptStmt->fetchColumn();

            $sessionCreated = false;
            $sessionId = null;
            $assignedPhysicalRoomId = null;
            $assignedVenueId = null;

            // Nếu 100% thành viên ACCEPTED -> Tìm phòng trống + DM rảnh + Chốt session + Sinh hóa đơn cọc
            if ($acceptedCount >= count($activeParticipantIds) && count($activeParticipantIds) > 0) {
                $startAt = (string) $proposal['proposed_start_at'];
                $endAt = (string) $proposal['proposed_end_at'];

                // Kiểm tra đã có session cho phòng này chưa
                $sessCheck = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `venue_id`, `physical_room_id` FROM `sessions` WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id LIMIT 1');
                $sessCheck->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                $sessCheck->execute();
                $existingSess = $sessCheck->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

                if ($existingSess === false) {
                    // Tìm phòng vật lý trống đủ sức chứa (có đệm 30 phút dọn phòng)
                    $availableRoom = $this->schedRepo->findAvailablePhysicalRoom(
                        count($activeParticipantIds),
                        $startAt,
                        $endAt,
                        30
                    );

                    if ($availableRoom === null) {
                        $this->pdo->rollBack();
                        return Response::fail(
                            'Không còn phòng vật lý trống đủ sức chứa trong khung giờ này. Vui lòng đề xuất khung giờ khác.',
                            'PHYSICAL_ROOM_CONFLICT',
                            409
                        );
                    }

                    $assignedVenueId = (int) $availableRoom['venue_id'];
                    $assignedPhysicalRoomId = (int) $availableRoom['id'];

                    // Lấy hoặc phân công DM rảnh động
                    $dmStmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                        'SELECT `id` FROM `dm_assignments`
                         WHERE `game_room_id` = :room_id AND `status` IN (\'PENDING\', \'ACCEPTED\')
                         ORDER BY `id` DESC LIMIT 1'
                    );
                    $dmStmt->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $dmStmt->execute();
                    $dmAssignId = $dmStmt->fetchColumn();

                    if ($dmAssignId === false) {
                        $dmUserId = $this->schedRepo->findAvailableDmUserId($startAt, $endAt, 30);
                        if ($dmUserId === null) {
                            $this->pdo->rollBack();
                            return Response::fail('Hiện chưa có Quản trò (DM) khả dụng trong hệ thống', 'DM_UNAVAILABLE', 409);
                        }

                        $insDm = $this->pdo->prepare(
                            'INSERT INTO `dm_assignments` (`game_room_id`, `dm_user_id`, `assigned_by`, `status`, `assigned_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                             VALUES (:room_id, :dm_user_id, :assigned_by, \'PENDING\', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())'
                        );
                        $insDm->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                        $insDm->bindValue(':dm_user_id', $dmUserId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                        $insDm->bindValue(':assigned_by', $userId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                        $insDm->execute();
                        $dmAssignId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
                    } else {
                        $dmAssignId = (int) $dmAssignId;
                    }

                    // Đánh dấu proposal CONFIRMED
                    $confStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `schedule_proposals` SET `status` = \'CONFIRMED\', `confirmed_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id');
                    $confStmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $confStmt->execute();

                    // Tạo Session chính thức
                    $insSess = $this->pdo->prepare(
                        'INSERT INTO `sessions` (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`, `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`)
                         VALUES (:room_id, :proposal_id, :dm_id, :venue_id, :physical_room_id, :start_at, :end_at, \'SCHEDULED\', NOW(), NOW())'
                    );
                    $insSess->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':proposal_id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':dm_id', $dmAssignId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':venue_id', $assignedVenueId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':physical_room_id', $assignedPhysicalRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                    $insSess->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
                    $insSess->execute();
                    $sessionId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();
                    $sessionCreated = true;

                    // Cập nhật trạng thái phòng -> SCHEDULED
                    $upRoom = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `game_rooms` SET `status` = \'SCHEDULED\', `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :room_id AND `status` = \'FORMING\'');
                    $upRoom->bindValue(':room_id', $roomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $upRoom->execute();

                    // Tự động tạo hóa đơn cọc 50.000đ cho toàn bộ thành viên active
                    $this->paymentRepo->ensureDepositPaymentsForRoom($roomId, PricingService::DEPOSIT_PER_PERSON, 2);
                } else {
                    $confStmt = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `schedule_proposals` SET `status` = \'CONFIRMED\', `confirmed_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id');
                    $confStmt->bindValue(':id', $proposalId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
                    $confStmt->execute();
                    $sessionId = (int) $existingSess['id'];
                    $assignedVenueId = (int) $existingSess['venue_id'];
                    $assignedPhysicalRoomId = (int) $existingSess['physical_room_id'];
                }
            }

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success([
                'proposal_id' => $proposalId,
                'accepted_votes' => $acceptedCount,
                'total_required' => count($activeParticipantIds),
                'proposal_confirmed' => $sessionCreated,
                'session_id' => $sessionId,
                'venue_id' => $assignedVenueId,
                'physical_room_id' => $assignedPhysicalRoomId,
            ], 'Đã ghi nhận biểu quyết của bạn');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Resolve current user ID from session or header.
     */
    protected function resolveCurrentUserId(Request $request): ?int
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return null;
        }

        return (int) $auth['id'];
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers\SessionLifecycleController.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers;

use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use App\Shared\Services\AuditLogService;
use PDO;

class SessionLifecycleController
{
    protected PDO $pdo;
    protected AuditLogService $auditService;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null, ?AuditLogService $auditService = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->auditService = $auditService ?? new AuditLogService($this->pdo);

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE && !headers_sent()) {
            @session_start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/start
     */
    public function start(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN', 'DM']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $sessionId = (int) $id;

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `status` FROM `sessions` WHERE `id` = :id LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE');
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $sessionId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $session = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($session === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy phiên chơi', 'SESSION_NOT_FOUND', 404);
            }

            if ($session['status'] !== 'SCHEDULED') {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail(
                    'Chỉ có thể bắt đầu phiên chơi đang ở trạng thái SCHEDULED (Hiện tại: ' . $session['status'] . ')',
                    'INVALID_SESSION_STATUS',
                    409
                );
            }

            $upd = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `sessions` SET `status` = \'IN_PROGRESS\', `started_at` = NOW(), `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $upd->bindValue(':id', $sessionId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upd->execute();

            $updRoom = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `game_rooms` SET `status` = \'IN_PROGRESS\', `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :rid');
            $updRoom->bindValue(':rid', (int) $session['game_room_id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $updRoom->execute();

            $this->auditService->log((int) $auth['id'], 'START_SESSION', 'session', $sessionId, ['game_room_id' => (int) $session['game_room_id']]);

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success([
                'session_id' => $sessionId,
                'status' => 'IN_PROGRESS',
            ], 'Đã bắt đầu phiên chơi');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/complete
     */
    public function complete(Request $request, string $id): Response
    {
        $auth = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($request, ['ADMIN', 'DM']);
        if ($auth instanceof Response) {
            return $auth;
        }

        $sessionId = (int) $id;

        $this->pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare('SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `status` FROM `sessions` WHERE `id` = :id LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE');
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $sessionId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();
            $session = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

            if ($session === false) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail('Không tìm thấy phiên chơi', 'SESSION_NOT_FOUND', 404);
            }

            if ($session['status'] !== 'IN_PROGRESS') {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
                return Response::fail(
                    'Chỉ có thể hoàn tất phiên chơi đang diễn ra IN_PROGRESS (Hiện tại: ' . $session['status'] . ')',
                    'INVALID_SESSION_STATUS',
                    409
                );
            }

            $upd = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE `sessions` SET `status` = \'COMPLETED\', `completed_at` = NOW(), `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :id'
            );
            $upd->bindValue(':id', $sessionId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $upd->execute();

            $updRoom = $this->pdo->prepare('UPDATE `game_rooms` SET `status` = \'COMPLETED\', `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `id` = :rid');
            $updRoom->bindValue(':rid', (int) $session['game_room_id'], PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $updRoom->execute();

            $this->auditService->log((int) $auth['id'], 'COMPLETE_SESSION', 'session', $sessionId, ['game_room_id' => (int) $session['game_room_id']]);

            $this->pdo->commit();

            return Response::success([
                'session_id' => $sessionId,
                'status' => 'COMPLETED',
            ], 'Đã hoàn tất phiên chơi');
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($this->pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $this->pdo->rollBack();
            }
            throw $e;
        }
    }
}
