FILE: backend\app\Features\VenueScheduling\Repositories\SchedulingRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Features\VenueScheduling\Repositories;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository;
use PDO;

class SchedulingRepository extends BaseRepository
{
    /**
     * Find session by ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findSessionById(int $id): ?array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($id): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`,
                        `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, CONCAT(\'SESS_\', `id`) AS `session_code`,
                        DATE(`start_at`) AS `scheduled_date`, TIME(`start_at`) AS `start_time`, TIME(`end_at`) AS `end_time`,
                        `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `started_at` AS `actual_start_time`,
                        `completed_at` AS `actual_end_time`, `cancel_reason` AS `cancellation_reason`,
                        `created_at`, `updated_at`
                 FROM `sessions`
                 WHERE `id` = :id
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':id', $id, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find session by session code or ID.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findSessionByCode(string $sessionCode): ?array
    {
        $id = (int) preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $sessionCode);
        if ($id <= 0) {
            return null;
        }
        return $this->findSessionById($id);
    }

    /**
     * List physical rooms by capacity.
     *
     * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public function listAvailablePhysicalRooms(int $minCapacity = 1): array
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($minCapacity): array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT `id`, `venue_id`, CONCAT(\'ROOM_\', `id`) AS `code`, `name`, `capacity`, `status`, `description`
                 FROM `physical_rooms`
                 WHERE `status` = \'ACTIVE\' AND `capacity` >= :min_capacity
                 ORDER BY `capacity` ASC, `name` ASC'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':min_capacity', max(1, $minCapacity), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            /** @var list<array<string, mixed>> $rows */
            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $rows;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Check if a physical room has conflicting reservations.
     */
    public function hasPhysicalRoomConflict(int $physicalRoomId, string $date, string $startTime, string $endTime): bool
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($physicalRoomId, $date, $startTime, $endTime): bool {
            $startAt = $date . ' ' . $startTime;
            $endAt = $date . ' ' . $endTime;

            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT COUNT(*) AS conflict_count
                 FROM `sessions`
                 WHERE `physical_room_id` = :physical_room_id
                   AND `status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\')
                   AND `start_at` < :end_at
                   AND `end_at` > :start_at'
            );

            $stmt->bindValue(':physical_room_id', $physicalRoomId, PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return !empty($result['conflict_count']) && (int) $result['conflict_count'] > 0;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find an active physical room with sufficient capacity and no conflicting session (including cleanup buffer).
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>|null
     */
    public function findAvailablePhysicalRoom(
        int $minCapacity,
        string $startAt,
        string $endAt,
        int $bufferMinutes = 30
    ): ?array {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($minCapacity, $startAt, $endAt, $bufferMinutes): ?array {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT pr.`id`, pr.`venue_id`, pr.`name`, pr.`capacity`
                 FROM `physical_rooms` pr
                 INNER JOIN `venues` v ON v.`id` = pr.`venue_id` AND v.`status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                 WHERE pr.`status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                   AND pr.`capacity` >= :min_capacity
                   AND NOT EXISTS (
                       SELECT 1 FROM `sessions` s
                       WHERE s.`physical_room_id` = pr.`id`
                         AND s.`status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\')
                         AND s.`start_at` < DATE_ADD(:end_at, INTERVAL :buf1 MINUTE)
                         AND s.`end_at` > DATE_SUB(:start_at, INTERVAL :buf2 MINUTE)
                   )
                 ORDER BY pr.`capacity` ASC, pr.`id` ASC
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':min_capacity', max(1, $minCapacity), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':buf1', max(0, $bufferMinutes), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':buf2', max(0, $bufferMinutes), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $result = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
            return $result !== false ? $result : null;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Find an active DM user who does not have an overlapping session.
     */
    public function findAvailableDmUserId(string $startAt, string $endAt, int $bufferMinutes = 30): ?int
    {
        return $this->executeQuery(function () use ($startAt, $endAt, $bufferMinutes): ?int {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'SELECT u.`id`
                 FROM `users` u
                 INNER JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE r.`code` = \'DM\'
                   AND u.`status` = \'ACTIVE\'
                   AND NOT EXISTS (
                       SELECT 1
                       FROM `sessions` s
                       INNER JOIN `dm_assignments` da ON da.`id` = s.`dm_assignment_id`
                       WHERE da.`dm_user_id` = u.`id`
                         AND s.`status` IN (\'SCHEDULED\', \'IN_PROGRESS\')
                         AND s.`start_at` < DATE_ADD(:end_at, INTERVAL :buf1 MINUTE)
                         AND s.`end_at` > DATE_SUB(:start_at, INTERVAL :buf2 MINUTE)
                   )
                 ORDER BY u.`id` ASC
                 LIMIT 1'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':end_at', $endAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':start_at', $startAt, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':buf1', max(0, $bufferMinutes), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':buf2', max(0, $bufferMinutes), PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->execute();

            $id = $stmt->fetchColumn();
            if ($id !== false && $id !== null) {
                return (int) $id;
            }

            // Fallback: bất kỳ DM ACTIVE nào nếu tất cả đều bận (để Admin điều phối lại)
            $fallback = $this->pdo->query(
                'SELECT u.`id` FROM `users` u INNER JOIN `roles` r ON r.`id` = u.`role_id`
                 WHERE r.`code` = \'DM\' AND u.`status` = \'ACTIVE\' ORDER BY u.`id` ASC LIMIT 1'
            );
            $fallbackId = $fallback !== false ? $fallback->fetchColumn() : false;
            return $fallbackId !== false && $fallbackId !== null ? (int) $fallbackId : null;
        });
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Config\Config.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Config;

class Config
{
    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    private static array $repository = [];
    private static bool $loaded = false;

    /**
     * Load environment configuration from .env file and environment variables.
     */
    public static function load(?string $basePath = null): void
    {
        if (self::$loaded && $basePath === null) {
            return;
        }

        $basePath = $basePath ?? dirname(__DIR__, 3);
        $envFile = rtrim($basePath, '/\\') . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR . '.env';

        if (file_exists($envFile)) {
            $lines = file($envFile, FILE_IGNORE_NEW_LINES | FILE_SKIP_EMPTY_LINES);
            if ($lines !== false) {
                foreach ($lines as $line) {
                    $line = trim($line);
                    if ($line === '' || str_starts_with($line, '#')) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (str_contains($line, '=')) {
                        [$name, $value] = explode('=', $line, 2);
                        $name = trim($name);
                        $value = trim($value);

                        // Strip outer quotes if present
                        if ((str_starts_with($value, '"') && str_ends_with($value, '"')) ||
                            (str_starts_with($value, "'") && str_ends_with($value, "'"))) {
                            $value = substr($value, 1, -1);
                        }

                        if (array_key_exists($name, $_ENV)) {
                            self::$repository[$name] = $_ENV[$name];
                        } else {
                            self::$repository[$name] = $value;
                            $_ENV[$name] = $value;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        self::$loaded = true;
    }

    /**
     * Get an environment variable with a fallback default.
     */
    public static function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        if (!self::$loaded) {
            self::load();
        }

        if (array_key_exists($key, self::$repository)) {
            return self::castValue(self::$repository[$key]);
        }

        $envVal = getenv($key);
        if ($envVal !== false) {
            return self::castValue($envVal);
        }

        if (isset($_ENV[$key])) {
            return self::castValue($_ENV[$key]);
        }

        return $default;
    }

    /**
     * Set a configuration value at runtime (useful for testing).
     */
    public static function set(string $key, mixed $value): void
    {
        if (!self::$loaded) {
            self::load();
        }
        self::$repository[$key] = $value;
    }

    /**
     * Clear all stored configuration (useful for testing).
     */
    public static function reset(): void
    {
        self::$repository = [];
        self::$loaded = false;
    }

    private static function castValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (!is_string($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        return match (strtolower($value)) {
            'true', '(true)' => true,
            'false', '(false)' => false,
            'null', '(null)' => null,
            'empty', '(empty)' => '',
            default => $value,
        };
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\BaseRepository.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database;

use PDO;
use PDOException;
use Throwable;

/**
 * Base Repository providing unified PDO execution, error translation,
 * and security-hardened statement execution.
 */
abstract class BaseRepository
{
    public function __construct(
        protected readonly PDO $pdo
    ) {
    }

    /**
     * Get the underlying PDO connection.
     */
    public function getPdo(): PDO
    {
        return $this->pdo;
    }

    /**
     * Executes a callback within repository try-catch block, translating
     * PDOException to DatabaseException with sanitized error messages.
     *
     * @template T
     * @param callable(): T $operation
     * @return T
     * @throws DatabaseException
     */
    protected function executeQuery(callable $operation): mixed
    {
        try {
            return $operation();
        } catch (PDOException $e) {
            throw new DatabaseException(
                message: 'Database query execution failed: ' . $e->getMessage(),
                code: (int) ($e->getCode() !== 0 && is_numeric($e->getCode()) ? $e->getCode() : 0),
                previous: $e
            );
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            if ($e instanceof DatabaseException) {
                throw $e;
            }
            throw new DatabaseException(
                message: 'Repository operation failed: ' . $e->getMessage(),
                code: (int) ($e->getCode() !== 0 && is_numeric($e->getCode()) ? $e->getCode() : 0),
                previous: $e
            );
        }
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\ConnectionFactory.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database;

use App\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\Exceptions\InvalidConfigurationException;
use PDO;
use PDOException;

/**
 * Factory responsible for creating and configuring secure PDO database connections.
 *
 * Supports both dependency injection (instantiable service) and static creation.
 */
class ConnectionFactory
{
    /**
     * Mandatory secure PDO connection options that cannot be overridden.
     *
     * @var array<int, mixed>
     */
    public const MANDATORY_OPTIONS = [
        PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
        PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
        PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false,
        PDO::ATTR_PERSISTENT => false,
    ];

    /**
     * Default secure PDO connection options.
     *
     * @var array<int, mixed>
     */
    public const DEFAULT_OPTIONS = self::MANDATORY_OPTIONS;

    private DatabaseConfig $config;

    /**
     * @param DatabaseConfig|null $config Validated DatabaseConfig. Falls back to environment config if omitted.
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException If environment configuration is invalid.
     */
    public function __construct(?DatabaseConfig $config = null)
    {
        $this->config = $config ?? DatabaseConfig::fromEnvironment();
    }

    /**
     * Get the active DatabaseConfig associated with this factory instance.
     */
    public function getConfig(): DatabaseConfig
    {
        return $this->config;
    }

    /**
     * Create a new PDO instance using the configured or provided DatabaseConfig.
     *
     * @param DatabaseConfig|array<string, mixed>|null $overrideConfig Optional override configuration.
     * @param array<int, mixed> $customOptions Optional additional PDO attributes (cannot override mandatory security attributes).
     * @throws DatabaseException If configuration is invalid or database connection fails.
     */
    public function createConnection(
        DatabaseConfig|array|null $overrideConfig = null,
        array $customOptions = []
    ): PDO {
        $resolvedConfig = match (true) {
            $overrideConfig instanceof DatabaseConfig => $overrideConfig,
            is_array($overrideConfig) && !empty($overrideConfig) => DatabaseConfig::fromArray(
                array_merge($this->config->toArray(), $overrideConfig)
            ),
            default => $this->config,
        };

        return self::establishConnection($resolvedConfig, $customOptions);
    }

    /**
     * Static factory method for backward compatibility with existing procedural callers.
     *
     * @param DatabaseConfig|array<string, mixed> $overrideConfig
     * @param array<int, mixed> $customOptions
     * @throws DatabaseException
     */
    public static function create(
        DatabaseConfig|array $overrideConfig = [],
        array $customOptions = []
    ): PDO {
        if ($overrideConfig instanceof DatabaseConfig) {
            return self::establishConnection($overrideConfig, $customOptions);
        }

        $factory = new self();
        return $factory->createConnection($overrideConfig, $customOptions);
    }

    /**
     * Internal connection establishment logic.
     *
     * @param array<int, mixed> $customOptions
     * @throws DatabaseException
     */
    private static function establishConnection(DatabaseConfig $dbConfig, array $customOptions = []): PDO
    {
        // 1. Start with custom options if any
        $options = $customOptions;

        // 2. Enforce MySQL charset and collation init command (cannot be disabled or bypassed by custom options)
        if ($dbConfig->getDriver() === 'mysql' && defined('PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND')) {
            $requiredInitCommand = sprintf(
                "SET NAMES '%s' COLLATE '%s'",
                $dbConfig->getCharset(),
                $dbConfig->getCollation()
            );

            if (isset($customOptions[PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND])) {
                $userInit = trim((string) $customOptions[PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND]);
                if ($userInit !== '' && !str_contains($userInit, "SET NAMES '{$dbConfig->getCharset()}'")) {
                    $options[PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND] = $requiredInitCommand . '; ' . $userInit;
                } else {
                    $options[PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND] = $userInit !== '' ? $userInit : $requiredInitCommand;
                }
            } else {
                $options[PDO::MYSQL_ATTR_INIT_COMMAND] = $requiredInitCommand;
            }
        }

        // 3. Strictly enforce mandatory security attributes over any custom options
        foreach (self::MANDATORY_OPTIONS as $attribute => $mandatoryValue) {
            $options[$attribute] = $mandatoryValue;
        }

        try {
            return new PDO(
                $dbConfig->getDsn(),
                $dbConfig->getUsername(),
                $dbConfig->getPassword(),
                $options
            );
        } catch (PDOException $e) {
            $sanitizedMsg = ExceptionHandler::sanitize($e->getMessage());

            $errorInfo = sprintf(
                'Failed to connect to database [%s] on host [%s:%d]: %s',
                $dbConfig->getDatabase(),
                $dbConfig->getHost(),
                $dbConfig->getPort(),
                $sanitizedMsg
            );

            // Wrap in DatabaseException WITHOUT chaining the original PDOException as previous,
            // preventing plain-text credentials in PDO constructor arguments from leaking via getTrace() / exception chaining.
            throw new DatabaseException($errorInfo, (int) $e->getCode());
        }
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConfig.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database;

use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\Exceptions\InvalidConfigurationException;

/**
 * Immutable value object holding validated database connection configuration.
 */
final readonly class DatabaseConfig
{
    public const DEFAULT_DRIVER = 'mysql';
    public const DEFAULT_HOST = '127.0.0.1';
    public const DEFAULT_PORT = 3306;
    public const DEFAULT_CHARSET = 'utf8mb4';
    public const DEFAULT_COLLATION = 'utf8mb4_unicode_ci';

    /**
     * Allowlist of supported MySQL charsets and their compatible collations.
     *
     * @var array<string, array{default_collation: string, collations: list<string>}>
     */
    public const SUPPORTED_CHARSETS = [
        'utf8mb4' => [
            'default_collation' => 'utf8mb4_unicode_ci',
            'collations' => [
                'utf8mb4_unicode_ci',
                'utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci',
                'utf8mb4_general_ci',
                'utf8mb4_bin',
            ],
        ],
        'utf8' => [
            'default_collation' => 'utf8_unicode_ci',
            'collations' => [
                'utf8_unicode_ci',
                'utf8_general_ci',
                'utf8_bin',
            ],
        ],
        'latin1' => [
            'default_collation' => 'latin1_swedish_ci',
            'collations' => [
                'latin1_swedish_ci',
                'latin1_general_ci',
                'latin1_bin',
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private string $driver;
    private string $host;
    private int $port;
    private string $database;
    private string $username;
    private string $password;
    private string $charset;
    private string $collation;

    public function __construct(
        string $driver,
        string $host,
        int $port,
        string $database,
        string $username,
        string $password,
        string $charset = self::DEFAULT_CHARSET,
        ?string $collation = null
    ) {
        $trimmedDriver = strtolower(trim($driver));
        $trimmedHost = trim($host);
        $trimmedDatabase = trim($database);
        $trimmedUsername = trim($username);
        $normalizedCharset = strtolower(trim($charset));

        // 1. Validate driver
        if ($trimmedDriver !== 'mysql') {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidDriver($driver);
        }

        // 2. Validate host (prohibit control chars, delimiters, whitespace; validate IPv4, IPv6, RFC hostname)
        self::validateHost($trimmedHost);

        // 3. Validate port (1..65535)
        if ($port < 1 || $port > 65535) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidPort($port);
        }

        // 4. Validate database name (MySQL identifier 1..64 chars, no delimiters/control chars)
        self::validateDatabaseName($trimmedDatabase);

        // 5. Validate username
        if ($trimmedUsername === '') {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::missingRequired('DB_USERNAME');
        }
        if (preg_match('/[\x00-\x1F\x7F]/', $trimmedUsername)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidFormat('DB_USERNAME', 'cannot contain control characters');
        }

        // 6. Validate charset against allowlist
        if (!array_key_exists($normalizedCharset, self::SUPPORTED_CHARSETS)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::unsupportedCharset(
                $charset,
                array_keys(self::SUPPORTED_CHARSETS)
            );
        }

        // 7. Resolve and validate collation compatibility
        $charsetMeta = self::SUPPORTED_CHARSETS[$normalizedCharset];
        $effectiveCollation = ($collation === null || trim($collation) === '')
            ? $charsetMeta['default_collation']
            : strtolower(trim($collation));

        if (!in_array($effectiveCollation, $charsetMeta['collations'], true)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::incompatibleCollation(
                $collation ?? $effectiveCollation,
                $normalizedCharset,
                $charsetMeta['collations']
            );
        }

        $this->driver = $trimmedDriver;
        $this->host = $trimmedHost;
        $this->port = $port;
        $this->database = $trimmedDatabase;
        $this->username = $trimmedUsername;
        // Password must NEVER be trimmed or modified
        $this->password = $password;
        $this->charset = $normalizedCharset;
        $this->collation = $effectiveCollation;
    }

    /**
     * Create DatabaseConfig from environment variables via central Config loader.
     *
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException
     */
    public static function fromEnvironment(): self
    {
        $rawDriver = Config::get('DB_CONNECTION', self::DEFAULT_DRIVER);
        if (!is_string($rawDriver)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_CONNECTION', 'string', gettype($rawDriver));
        }

        $rawHost = Config::get('DB_HOST', self::DEFAULT_HOST);
        if (!is_string($rawHost)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_HOST', 'string', gettype($rawHost));
        }

        $rawPort = Config::get('DB_PORT', self::DEFAULT_PORT);
        $port = self::parseAndValidatePort($rawPort);

        $rawDatabase = Config::get('DB_DATABASE', '');
        if (!is_string($rawDatabase)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_DATABASE', 'string', gettype($rawDatabase));
        }

        $rawUsername = Config::get('DB_USERNAME', '');
        if (!is_string($rawUsername)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_USERNAME', 'string', gettype($rawUsername));
        }

        $rawPassword = Config::get('DB_PASSWORD', '');
        if (!is_string($rawPassword)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_PASSWORD', 'string', gettype($rawPassword));
        }

        $rawCharset = Config::get('DB_CHARSET', self::DEFAULT_CHARSET);
        if (!is_string($rawCharset)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_CHARSET', 'string', gettype($rawCharset));
        }

        $rawCollation = Config::get('DB_COLLATION');
        if ($rawCollation !== null && !is_string($rawCollation)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_COLLATION', 'string', gettype($rawCollation));
        }

        return new self(
            driver: $rawDriver,
            host: $rawHost,
            port: $port,
            database: $rawDatabase,
            username: $rawUsername,
            password: $rawPassword,
            charset: $rawCharset,
            collation: $rawCollation
        );
    }

    /**
     * Create DatabaseConfig from an explicit associative array with environment fallbacks.
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException
     */
    public static function fromArray(array $data): self
    {
        $driver = self::resolveString($data, 'driver', 'DB_CONNECTION', (string) Config::get('DB_CONNECTION', self::DEFAULT_DRIVER));
        $host = self::resolveString($data, 'host', 'DB_HOST', (string) Config::get('DB_HOST', self::DEFAULT_HOST));

        $rawPort = self::resolveValue($data, 'port', 'DB_PORT', Config::get('DB_PORT', self::DEFAULT_PORT));
        $port = self::parseAndValidatePort($rawPort);

        $database = self::resolveString($data, 'database', 'DB_DATABASE', (string) Config::get('DB_DATABASE', ''));
        $username = self::resolveString($data, 'username', 'DB_USERNAME', (string) Config::get('DB_USERNAME', ''));
        $password = self::resolvePassword($data, (string) Config::get('DB_PASSWORD', ''));

        $charset = self::resolveString($data, 'charset', 'DB_CHARSET', (string) Config::get('DB_CHARSET', self::DEFAULT_CHARSET));

        $rawCollation = self::resolveOptionalString($data, 'collation', 'DB_COLLATION', Config::get('DB_COLLATION'));

        return new self(
            driver: $driver,
            host: $host,
            port: $port,
            database: $database,
            username: $username,
            password: $password,
            charset: $charset,
            collation: $rawCollation
        );
    }

    /**
     * Parse and strictly validate port values.
     * Only accepts true integers or clean numeric digit strings within 1..65535.
     *
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException
     */
    public static function parseAndValidatePort(mixed $port): int
    {
        if (is_int($port)) {
            $intPort = $port;
        } elseif (is_string($port) && preg_match('/^[0-9]+$/', $port) === 1) {
            $intPort = (int) $port;
        } else {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidPort($port);
        }

        if ($intPort < 1 || $intPort > 65535) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidPort($intPort);
        }

        return $intPort;
    }

    /**
     * Validate host against DSN injection, control characters, and verify IP or hostname format.
     *
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException
     */
    private static function validateHost(string $host): void
    {
        if ($host === '') {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::missingRequired('DB_HOST');
        }

        // Prohibit null bytes, control characters, whitespace, and DSN delimiters
        if (preg_match('/[\x00-\x1F\x7F\s;=]/', $host)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidHost($host, 'contains invalid or control characters');
        }

        if (strlen($host) > 255) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidHost($host, 'exceeds maximum length of 255 characters');
        }

        // 1. IPv4 check
        if (filter_var($host, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV4) !== false) {
            return;
        }

        // 2. IPv6 check (supports [::1] and ::1)
        $unbracketed = (str_starts_with($host, '[') && str_ends_with($host, ']'))
            ? substr($host, 1, -1)
            : $host;

        if (filter_var($unbracketed, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV6) !== false) {
            return;
        }

        // 3. RFC 1123 / RFC 952 Hostname check
        if (preg_match('/^(?!-)[a-zA-Z0-9-]{1,63}(?<!-)(\.(?!-)[a-zA-Z0-9-]{1,63}(?<!-))*$/', $host)) {
            return;
        }

        throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidHost($host, 'must be a valid hostname, IPv4, or IPv6 address');
    }

    /**
     * Validate database name against DSN injection and MySQL identifier rules.
     *
     * @throws InvalidConfigurationException
     */
    private static function validateDatabaseName(string $database): void
    {
        if ($database === '') {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::missingRequired('DB_DATABASE');
        }

        // Prohibit null bytes, control characters, whitespace, DSN delimiters, quotes, and path separators
        if (preg_match('/[\x00-\x1F\x7F\s;=\/\\\.]/', $database)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidDatabase($database, 'contains invalid or control characters');
        }

        if (strlen($database) > 64) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidDatabase($database, 'exceeds maximum length of 64 characters');
        }

        if (!preg_match('/^[a-zA-Z0-9_$-]{1,64}$/', $database)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidDatabase($database, 'contains disallowed characters for database name');
        }
    }

    private static function resolveValue(array $data, string $key, string $envKey, mixed $fallback): mixed
    {
        if (array_key_exists($key, $data)) {
            return $data[$key];
        }
        if (array_key_exists($envKey, $data)) {
            return $data[$envKey];
        }
        return $fallback;
    }

    private static function resolveString(array $data, string $key, string $envKey, ?string $fallback): string
    {
        $hasKey = array_key_exists($key, $data);
        $hasEnvKey = array_key_exists($envKey, $data);

        if (!$hasKey && !$hasEnvKey) {
            if ($fallback === null) {
                throw InvalidConfigurationException::missingRequired($envKey);
            }
            return $fallback;
        }

        $activeKey = $hasKey ? $key : $envKey;
        $val = $data[$activeKey];

        if (!is_string($val)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType($envKey, 'string', gettype($val));
        }

        return $val;
    }

    private static function resolveOptionalString(array $data, string $key, string $envKey, mixed $fallback): ?string
    {
        $hasKey = array_key_exists($key, $data);
        $hasEnvKey = array_key_exists($envKey, $data);

        if (!$hasKey && !$hasEnvKey) {
            if ($fallback === null) {
                return null;
            }
            if (is_string($fallback)) {
                return $fallback;
            }
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType($envKey, 'string', gettype($fallback));
        }

        $activeKey = $hasKey ? $key : $envKey;
        $val = $data[$activeKey];

        if ($val === null) {
            return null;
        }

        if (!is_string($val)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType($envKey, 'string', gettype($val));
        }

        return $val;
    }

    private static function resolvePassword(array $data, string $fallback): string
    {
        $hasKey = array_key_exists('password', $data);
        $hasEnvKey = array_key_exists('DB_PASSWORD', $data);

        if (!$hasKey && !$hasEnvKey) {
            return $fallback;
        }

        $activeKey = $hasKey ? 'password' : 'DB_PASSWORD';
        $val = $data[$activeKey];

        if (!is_string($val)) {
            throw InvalidConfigurationException::invalidType('DB_PASSWORD', 'string', gettype($val));
        }

        // Retain raw password without any trimming or mutation
        return $val;
    }

    public function getDriver(): string
    {
        return $this->driver;
    }

    public function getHost(): string
    {
        return $this->host;
    }

    public function getPort(): int
    {
        return $this->port;
    }

    public function getDatabase(): string
    {
        return $this->database;
    }

    public function getUsername(): string
    {
        return $this->username;
    }

    public function getPassword(): string
    {
        return $this->password;
    }

    public function getCharset(): string
    {
        return $this->charset;
    }

    public function getCollation(): string
    {
        return $this->collation;
    }

    /**
     * Build standard PDO DSN string using validated parameters.
     */
    public function getDsn(): string
    {
        return sprintf(
            '%s:host=%s;port=%d;dbname=%s;charset=%s',
            $this->driver,
            $this->host,
            $this->port,
            $this->database,
            $this->charset
        );
    }

    /**
     * Convert configuration to associative array for connection drivers.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        return [
            'driver' => $this->driver,
            'host' => $this->host,
            'port' => $this->port,
            'database' => $this->database,
            'username' => $this->username,
            'password' => $this->password,
            'charset' => $this->charset,
            'collation' => $this->collation,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Export safe configuration array with masked password for logging/debug output.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toSafeArray(): array
    {
        $safe = $this->toArray();
        $safe['password'] = '***';
        return $safe;
    }

    /**
     * Prevent leaking plain-text database credentials in var_dump, print_r, or stack trace inspectors.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function __debugInfo(): array
    {
        return $this->toSafeArray();
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database;

use PDO;
use Throwable;

/**
 * Database connection holder and provider.
 *
 * Exposes database connections according to project conventions,
 * supporting both dependency injection (instantiable provider)
 * and legacy static access (getInstance).
 */
class DatabaseConnection
{
    private static ?PDO $instance = null;
    private static ?ConnectionFactory $defaultFactory = null;

    private ?PDO $connection = null;
    private ConnectionFactory $factory;

    /**
     * Create an instance-based database connection provider for DI containers.
     *
     * @param ConnectionFactory|null $factory Factory to create connection when needed.
     * @param PDO|null $connection Existing connection (e.g. for testing/mocking).
     */
    public function __construct(
        ?ConnectionFactory $factory = null,
        ?PDO $connection = null
    ) {
        $this->factory = $factory ?? self::getDefaultFactory();
        $this->connection = $connection;
    }

    /**
     * Get or initialize the connection for this provider instance.
     *
     * @throws DatabaseException
     */
    public function getConnection(): PDO
    {
        if ($this->connection === null) {
            $this->connection = $this->factory->createConnection();
        }

        return $this->connection;
    }

    /**
     * Check connectivity on this provider instance.
     */
    public function ping(): bool
    {
        return self::checkConnectivity($this->getConnection());
    }

    /**
     * Get or initialize the shared PDO connection (static singleton for backward compatibility).
     *
     * Uses the configured ConnectionFactory (from setFactory or default).
     *
     * @param DatabaseConfig|array<string, mixed> $config
     * @throws DatabaseException
     */
    public static function getInstance(DatabaseConfig|array $config = []): PDO
    {
        if (self::$instance === null || !empty($config)) {
            $factory = self::getDefaultFactory();
            self::$instance = $factory->createConnection(!empty($config) ? $config : null);
        }

        return self::$instance;
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom PDO instance for static access (useful for mocks or test SQLite).
     */
    public static function setInstance(?PDO $pdo): void
    {
        self::$instance = $pdo;
    }

    /**
     * Configure or reset the default ConnectionFactory for static/default instances.
     * Clears current static instance so future getInstance() calls use the new factory.
     */
    public static function setFactory(?ConnectionFactory $factory): void
    {
        self::$defaultFactory = $factory;
        self::$instance = null;
    }

    /**
     * Get the default ConnectionFactory instance.
     */
    public static function getDefaultFactory(): ConnectionFactory
    {
        if (self::$defaultFactory === null) {
            self::$defaultFactory = new ConnectionFactory();
        }

        return self::$defaultFactory;
    }

    /**
     * Reset all static state (both singleton instance and default factory).
     */
    public static function reset(): void
    {
        self::$instance = null;
        self::$defaultFactory = null;
    }

    /**
     * Verify database connectivity by running a lightweight query.
     */
    public static function checkConnectivity(?PDO $pdo = null): bool
    {
        try {
            $connection = $pdo ?? self::getInstance();
            $statement = $connection->query('SELECT 1');
            return $statement !== false && (int) $statement->fetchColumn() === 1;
        } catch (Throwable) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Execute a callable within a transactional boundary.
     *
     * Delegates to TransactionManager to provide safe nested savepoint transactions
     * and unified transaction lifecycle management.
     *
     * @template T
     * @param callable(PDO): T $callback
     * @return T
     * @throws Throwable
     */
    public static function transaction(callable $callback, ?PDO $pdo = null): mixed
    {
        $connection = $pdo ?? self::getInstance();
        $manager = new TransactionManager($connection);

        return $manager->transaction($callback);
    }
}

use App\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler;
use RuntimeException;
use Throwable;

class DatabaseException extends RuntimeException
{
    public function __construct(string $message = 'Database error occurred', int $code = 0, ?Throwable $previous = null)
    {
        $sanitizedMessage = ExceptionHandler::sanitize($message);
        parent::__construct($sanitizedMessage, $code, $previous);
    }

============================================================
FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\TransactionManager.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\Exceptions\TransactionException;
use PDO;
use Throwable;
use WeakMap;

/**
 * Manages database transaction boundaries with nested savepoint support.
 *
 * Tracks transaction depth per PDO instance using WeakMap so that
 * different connections maintain independent transaction states without
 * memory leaks or state collision.
 */
class TransactionManager
{
    private PDO|DatabaseConnection $connectionSource;

    /**
     * @var WeakMap<PDO, int>|null
     */
    private static ?WeakMap $depthRegistry = null;

    /**
     * @param PDO|DatabaseConnection $connection
     */
    public function __construct(PDO|DatabaseConnection $connection)
    {
        $this->connectionSource = $connection;
        self::initRegistry();
    }

    /**
     * Execute a callable within a managed transaction boundary.
     *
     * Supports nested transactions via SAVEPOINTs:
     * - Outermost level uses beginTransaction / commit / rollBack.
     * - Nested levels use SAVEPOINT, RELEASE SAVEPOINT, and ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT.
     *
     * @template T
     * @param callable(PDO): T $callback
     * @return T
     * @throws Throwable
     */
    public function transaction(callable $callback): mixed
    {
        $pdo = $this->getPdo();

        $this->beginTransactionLevel($pdo);

        try {
            $result = $callback($pdo);
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            $this->rollBackLevel($pdo);
            throw $e;
        }

        try {
            $this->commitLevel($pdo);
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            $this->rollBackLevel($pdo);
            throw $e;
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * Get the current active transaction nesting depth for this connection.
     */
    public function getTransactionDepth(): int
    {
        $pdo = $this->getPdo();
        return self::$depthRegistry[$pdo] ?? 0;
    }

    /**
     * Resolve the underlying PDO instance.
     */
    public function getPdo(): PDO
    {
        if ($this->connectionSource instanceof DatabaseConnection) {
            return $this->connectionSource->getConnection();
        }

        return $this->connectionSource;
    }

    /**
     * Initialize the shared connection depth registry.
     */
    private static function initRegistry(): void
    {
        if (self::$depthRegistry === null) {
            self::$depthRegistry = new WeakMap();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set the current depth for a PDO instance.
     */
    private function setDepth(PDO $pdo, int $depth): void
    {
        self::initRegistry();
        if ($depth <= 0) {
            unset(self::$depthRegistry[$pdo]);
        } else {
            self::$depthRegistry[$pdo] = $depth;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current depth for a PDO instance.
     */
    private function getDepth(PDO $pdo): int
    {
        self::initRegistry();
        return self::$depthRegistry[$pdo] ?? 0;
    }

    /**
     * Begin a transaction or create an internal savepoint.
     *
     * @throws TransactionException
     */
    private function beginTransactionLevel(PDO $pdo): void
    {
        $depth = $this->getDepth($pdo);

        if ($depth === 0) {
            try {
                $pdo->beginTransaction();
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                // If beginTransaction fails, depth remains 0
                throw new TransactionException('Failed to start database transaction', 0, $e);
            }
        } else {
            $savepointName = $this->getSavepointName($depth);
            try {
                $pdo->exec("SAVEPOINT {$savepointName}");
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                // If creating savepoint fails, depth remains unchanged
                throw new TransactionException(
                    "Failed to create savepoint at depth {$depth}",
                    0,
                    $e
                );
            }
        }

        $this->setDepth($pdo, $depth + 1);
    }

    /**
     * Commit the current transaction level or release its savepoint.
     *
     * Note: Depth is decremented ONLY when commit/release succeeds.
     * If an error occurs, depth remains at the current level so rollBackLevel()
     * can roll back this exact level.
     *
     * @throws TransactionException
     */
    private function commitLevel(PDO $pdo): void
    {
        $currentDepth = $this->getDepth($pdo);
        if ($currentDepth <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        if ($currentDepth === 1) {
            try {
                $pdo->commit();
                $this->setDepth($pdo, 0);
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                throw new TransactionException('Failed to commit database transaction', 0, $e);
            }
        } else {
            // Nested level: release the savepoint that was created for this level
            $savepointLevel = $currentDepth - 1;
            $savepointName = $this->getSavepointName($savepointLevel);

            try {
                $pdo->exec("RELEASE SAVEPOINT {$savepointName}");
                $this->setDepth($pdo, $currentDepth - 1);
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                throw new TransactionException(
                    "Failed to release savepoint at depth {$savepointLevel}",
                    0,
                    $e
                );
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Rollback the current transaction level to its savepoint or abort outermost transaction.
     *
     * Rollback is performed using the current level's savepoint, and depth is decremented.
     * Secondary rollback failures are suppressed so the primary exception is preserved.
     */
    private function rollBackLevel(PDO $pdo): void
    {
        $currentDepth = $this->getDepth($pdo);
        if ($currentDepth <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        if ($currentDepth === 1) {
            try {
                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $pdo->rollBack();
                }
            } catch (Throwable) {
                // Secondary rollback failure must not overwrite primary exception
            } finally {
                $this->setDepth($pdo, 0);
            }
        } else {
            $savepointLevel = $currentDepth - 1;
            $savepointName = $this->getSavepointName($savepointLevel);

            try {
                $pdo->exec("ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT {$savepointName}");
            } catch (Throwable) {
                // Secondary rollback failure must not overwrite primary exception
            } finally {
                $this->setDepth($pdo, $currentDepth - 1);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Generate an internal, safe savepoint identifier based on depth.
     */
    private function getSavepointName(int $depth): string
    {
        return 'sp_depth_' . $depth;
    }
}

FILE: backend\app\Infrastructure\Database\Exceptions\InvalidConfigurationException.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Infrastructure\Database\Exceptions;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseException;

class InvalidConfigurationException extends DatabaseException
{
    public static function missingRequired(string $field): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: [%s] is required and cannot be empty.', $field));
    }

    public static function invalidPort(mixed $port): self
    {
        $displayPort = is_scalar($port) ? (string) $port : gettype($port);
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: Invalid DB_PORT [%s]. Must be an integer between 1 and 65535.', $displayPort));
    }

    public static function invalidType(string $field, string $expectedType, string $actualType): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: [%s] must be of type %s, %s given.', $field, $expectedType, $actualType));
    }

    public static function invalidHost(string $host, string $reason): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: Invalid DB_HOST [%s] - %s.', $host, $reason));
    }

    public static function invalidDatabase(string $database, string $reason): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: Invalid DB_DATABASE [%s] - %s.', $database, $reason));
    }

    public static function invalidCharset(string $charset): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: Invalid or unsupported DB_CHARSET [%s].', $charset));
    }

    /**
     * @param list<string> $supported
     */
    public static function unsupportedCharset(string $charset, array $supported): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf(
            'Database configuration error: Unsupported DB_CHARSET [%s]. Supported charsets: %s.',
            $charset,
            implode(', ', $supported)
        ));
    }

    /**
     * @param list<string> $supported
     */
    public static function incompatibleCollation(string $collation, string $charset, array $supported): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf(
            'Database configuration error: Incompatible collation [%s] for charset [%s]. Supported collations: %s.',
            $collation,
            $charset,
            implode(', ', $supported)
        ));
    }

    public static function invalidDriver(string $driver): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: Unsupported database driver [%s]. Only "mysql" is supported.', $driver));
    }

    public static function invalidFormat(string $field, string $reason): self
    {
        return new self(sprintf('Database configuration error: [%s] %s.', $field, $reason));
    }
}

class TransactionException extends DatabaseException
{
    public function __construct(
        string $message = 'Database transaction failed',
        int $code = 0,
        ?Throwable $previous = null
    ) {
        parent::__construct($message, $code, $previous);
    }

FILE: backend\app\Shared\Services\AuditLogService.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Shared\Services;

use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;
use Throwable;

class AuditLogService
{
    protected PDO $pdo;

    public function __construct(?PDO $pdo = null)
    {
        $this->pdo = $pdo ?? DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
    }

    /**
     * Record an audit event safely (never breaks primary business flow).
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $metadata
     */
    public function log(
        ?int $actorId,
        string $action,
        string $entityType,
        ?int $entityId = null,
        array $metadata = [],
        ?string $ipAddress = null
    ): void {
        try {
            $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO `audit_logs` (`actor_id`, `action`, `entity_type`, `entity_id`, `metadata`, `ip_address`, `created_at`)
                 VALUES (:actor_id, :action, :entity_type, :entity_id, :metadata, :ip_address, NOW())'
            );
            $stmt->bindValue(':actor_id', $actorId, $actorId === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':action', $action, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':entity_type', $entityType, PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->bindValue(':entity_id', $entityId, $entityId === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_INT);
            $stmt->bindValue(':metadata', !empty($metadata) ? json_encode($metadata, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) : null, !empty($metadata) ? PDO::PARAM_STR : PDO::PARAM_NULL);
            $ip = $ipAddress ?? ($_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? null);
            $stmt->bindValue(':ip_address', $ip, $ip === null ? PDO::PARAM_NULL : PDO::PARAM_STR);
            $stmt->execute();
        } catch (Throwable) {
            // Do not fail business request if audit logging fails
        }
    }

    /**
     * List audit logs with pagination and optional filters.
     *
     * @return array{items: list<array<string, mixed>>, total: int}
     */
    public function listLogs(int $page = 1, int $perPage = 20, ?string $action = null, ?string $entityType = null): array
    {
        $where = ['1 = 1'];
        $params = [];

        if ($action !== null && trim($action) !== '') {
            $where[] = 'al.`action` = :action';
            $params[':action'] = trim($action);
        }

        if ($entityType !== null && trim($entityType) !== '') {
            $where[] = 'al.`entity_type` = :entity_type';
            $params[':entity_type'] = trim($entityType);
        }

        $whereSql = implode(' AND ', $where);

        $cntStmt = $this->pdo->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `audit_logs` al WHERE {$whereSql}");
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $cntStmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        }
        $cntStmt->execute();
        $total = (int) $cntStmt->fetchColumn();

        $offset = max(0, ($page - 1) * $perPage);
        $stmt = $this->pdo->prepare(
            "SELECT al.`id`, al.`actor_id`, u.`full_name` AS `actor_name`, u.`email` AS `actor_email`,
                    al.`action`, al.`entity_type`, al.`entity_id`, al.`metadata`, al.`ip_address`, al.`created_at`
             FROM `audit_logs` al
             LEFT JOIN `users` u ON u.`id` = al.`actor_id`
             WHERE {$whereSql}
             ORDER BY al.`id` DESC
             LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset"
        );
        foreach ($params as $k => $v) {
            $stmt->bindValue($k, $v, PDO::PARAM_STR);
        }
        $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $perPage, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $offset, PDO::PARAM_INT);
        $stmt->execute();

        $items = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
        foreach ($items as &$row) {
            if (!empty($row['metadata']) && is_string($row['metadata'])) {
                $row['metadata'] = json_decode($row['metadata'], true);
            }
        }

        return ['items' => $items, 'total' => $total];
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\bin\cron.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

require dirname(__DIR__) . '/vendor/autoload.php';

use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Services\BackgroundJobService;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;

Config::load(dirname(__DIR__));

$pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
$service = new BackgroundJobService($pdo);

// Parse CLI options
$options = getopt('', ['job:']);
$targetJob = strtolower((string) ($options['job'] ?? 'all'));

echo "=====================================================\n";
echo " KỊCH BẢN SÁT - BACKGROUND CRON RUNNER (V16)\n";
echo " Time: " . date('Y-m-d H:i:s') . "\n";
echo " Target Job: " . strtoupper($targetJob) . "\n";
echo "=====================================================\n\n";

switch ($targetJob) {
    case 'formation-timeout':
        $count = $service->runFormationTimeout();
        echo "[SUCCESS] Processed {$count} expired forming rooms.\n";
        break;

    case 'deposit-timeout':
        $count = $service->runDepositTimeout();
        echo "[SUCCESS] Processed {$count} deposit timeout participants.\n";
        break;

    case 'replacement-timeout':
        $count = $service->runReplacementTimeout();
        echo "[SUCCESS] Processed {$count} expired replacement windows.\n";
        break;

    case 'all':
    default:
        $stats = $service->runAll();
        echo "[SUMMARY] Background jobs completed successfully:\n";
        echo "  - Formation Timeouts: {$stats['formation_timeouts']} room(s) cancelled.\n";
        echo "  - Deposit Timeouts: {$stats['deposit_timeouts']} participant(s) kicked.\n";
        echo "  - Replacement Timeouts: {$stats['replacement_timeouts']} window(s) expired.\n";
        break;
}

echo "\n[DONE] Finished maintenance cycle.\n";

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

$basePath = dirname(__DIR__);

// Load Composer autoloader
$autoloader = $basePath . '/vendor/autoload.php';
if (file_exists($autoloader)) {
    require_once $autoloader;
}

// Initialize environment configuration
App\Infrastructure\Config\Config::load($basePath);
date_default_timezone_set((string) App\Infrastructure\Config\Config::get('APP_TIMEZONE', 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh'));

// Instantiate application
$app = new App\Core\Application($basePath);

// Register global middleware
$app->addMiddleware(new App\Core\Middleware\CorsMiddleware());
$app->addMiddleware(new App\Core\Middleware\CsrfMiddleware());
$app->addMiddleware(new App\Core\Middleware\RateLimitMiddleware());

// Register routes (use require so multiple instantiations in testing register routes)
$router = $app->getRouter();
$routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
if (file_exists($routesFile)) {
    require $routesFile;
}

return $app;
