FILE: backend\database\kich_ban_sat_v3_clean.sql
============================================================
-- =============================================================
-- KỊCH BẢN SÁT - HỆ THỐNG CƠ SỞ DỮ LIỆU PHIÊN BẢN V3
-- Hệ quản trị CSDL mục tiêu: MySQL 8.0.16+ / MySQL 8.4 LTS
-- Bộ mã ký tự: utf8mb4 (utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci)
-- Múi giờ mặc định: +07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh)
--
-- TỔNG QUAN KIẾN TRÚC:
--   - 19 Bảng nghiệp vụ phân bổ vào 9 Domain rõ ràng
--   - 02 Bảng hạ tầng kỹ thuật (idempotency_records, outbox_events)
--   - Đã tối ưu hóa: nhúng Replacement Window & Price Snapshot vào game_rooms
--   - Đã hoàn thiện toàn bộ CHECK constraints, Generated Columns an toàn
-- =============================================================

CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS `kich_ban_sat_v3`
  CHARACTER SET utf8mb4
  COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

USE `kich_ban_sat_v3`;

SET NAMES utf8mb4;
SET time_zone = '+07:00';
SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 1: IDENTITY & QUẢN TRỊ TÀI KHOẢN
-- =============================================================

-- 1. roles: Bảng vai trò người dùng (ADMIN, DM, PLAYER)
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `roles`;
CREATE TABLE `roles` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `code` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    `name` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `description` VARCHAR(255) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_roles_code` UNIQUE (`code`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 2. users: Bảng tài khoản người dùng
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `users`;
CREATE TABLE `users` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `role_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `full_name` VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    `email` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    `password_hash` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    `phone` VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    `avatar_url` VARCHAR(500) NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    `email_verified_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `last_login_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_users_email` UNIQUE (`email`),
    KEY `idx_users_role_status` (`role_id`, `status`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_users_role`
        FOREIGN KEY (`role_id`) REFERENCES `roles`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_users_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 2: GAME CATALOG & CẤU HÌNH KỊCH BẢN
-- =============================================================

-- 3. games: Danh mục kịch bản Án Mạng / TRPG
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `games`;
CREATE TABLE `games` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `name` VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    `slug` VARCHAR(220) NOT NULL,
    `description` TEXT NULL,
    `image_url` VARCHAR(500) NULL,
    `min_players` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `max_players` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `duration_minutes` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    `display_order` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_games_slug` UNIQUE (`slug`),

    CONSTRAINT `chk_games_player_range`
        CHECK (`min_players` > 0 AND `max_players` >= `min_players`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_games_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE', 'DRAFT'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 4. game_configurations: Cấu hình đạo cụ, phiên bản kịch bản theo version
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `game_configurations`;
CREATE TABLE `game_configurations` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `version` INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `configuration` JSON NOT NULL,
    `description` TEXT NULL,
    `is_active` TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    `created_by` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_game_configuration_version` UNIQUE (`game_id`, `version`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_game_configuration_game_id_id` UNIQUE (`game_id`, `id`),
    KEY `idx_game_configuration_active` (`game_id`, `is_active`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_configuration_game`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_id`) REFERENCES `games`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_configuration_creator`
        FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_configuration_active`
        CHECK (`is_active` IN (0, 1))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 3: GAMEROOM, THÀNH VIÊN & CỬA SỔ THAY THẾ
-- =============================================================

-- 5. game_rooms: Phòng ghép người chơi (tích hợp Replacement Window & Snapshot Giá)
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `game_rooms`;
CREATE TABLE `game_rooms` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `game_configuration_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `creator_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,

    `title` VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    `description` TEXT NULL,
    `min_players` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `max_players` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FORMING',
    `formation_deadline_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `cancel_reason` TEXT NULL,
    `cancelled_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,

    -- Cửa sổ tìm người thay thế (Replacement Window 1:0..1)
    `replacement_opened_by` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `replacement_reason` TEXT NULL,
    `replacement_started_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `replacement_deadline_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `replacement_status` VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    `replacement_closed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,

    -- Ảnh chụp giá phòng khi chốt lịch (Booking Price Snapshot 1:0..1)
    `price_total_participants` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_subtotal` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_discount_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `price_final_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_deposit_total` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_remaining_total` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_promotion_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `price_details` JSON NULL,
    `price_calculated_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,

    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_game_room_game_id_id` UNIQUE (`game_id`, `id`),

    KEY `idx_game_rooms_status_created` (`status`, `created_at`),
    KEY `idx_game_rooms_game_status` (`game_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_game_rooms_creator_status` (`creator_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_game_rooms_replacement_deadline` (`replacement_status`, `replacement_deadline_at`),
    KEY `idx_game_rooms_price_promotion` (`price_promotion_id`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_rooms_game`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_id`) REFERENCES `games`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_rooms_configuration`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`)
        REFERENCES `game_configurations`(`game_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_rooms_creator`
        FOREIGN KEY (`creator_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_rooms_replacement_opener`
        FOREIGN KEY (`replacement_opened_by`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_rooms_player_range`
        CHECK (`min_players` > 0 AND `max_players` >= `min_players`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_rooms_replacement_status`
        CHECK (
            `replacement_status` IS NULL
            OR `replacement_status` IN ('OPEN', 'CLOSED', 'EXPIRED')
        ),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_rooms_replacement_time`
        CHECK (
            `replacement_started_at` IS NULL
            OR `replacement_deadline_at` IS NULL
            OR `replacement_deadline_at` > `replacement_started_at`
        ),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_rooms_price`
        CHECK (
            `price_subtotal` IS NULL
            OR (
                `price_discount_amount` <= `price_subtotal`
                AND `price_final_amount` IS NOT NULL
                AND `price_deposit_total` IS NOT NULL
                AND `price_remaining_total` IS NOT NULL
                AND `price_details` IS NOT NULL
                AND `price_total_participants` IS NOT NULL
                AND `price_final_amount` = `price_subtotal` - `price_discount_amount`
                AND `price_deposit_total` + `price_remaining_total` = `price_final_amount`
                AND `price_total_participants` > 0
            )
        )
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 6. room_participants: Thành viên tham gia phòng chơi
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `room_participants`;
CREATE TABLE `room_participants` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING_DEPOSIT',

    -- Cột sinh học: NULL khi đã rời phòng/bị hủy, mang user_id khi đang chiếm slot
    `active_user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
            CASE
                WHEN `status` IN ('PENDING_DEPOSIT', 'DEPOSITED', 'LEAVE_REQUESTED')
                THEN `user_id`
                ELSE NULL
            END
        ) STORED,

    `joined_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `deposit_deadline_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `deposited_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `left_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `cancelled_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `cancel_reason` TEXT NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_room_active_user` UNIQUE (`game_room_id`, `active_user_id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_room_participant_room_id` UNIQUE (`game_room_id`, `id`),

    KEY `idx_participants_room_status` (`game_room_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_participants_user_created` (`user_id`, `created_at`),
    KEY `idx_participants_deposit_deadline` (`status`, `deposit_deadline_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_participants_game_room`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`) REFERENCES `game_rooms`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_participants_user`
        FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_participant_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN (
            'PENDING_DEPOSIT', 'DEPOSITED', 'LEAVE_REQUESTED',
            'LEFT', 'CANCELLED_UNPAID', 'CANCELLED_BY_ADMIN'
        ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 4: DM ASSIGNMENT (GÁN QUẢN TRÒ)
-- =============================================================

-- 7. dm_assignments: Phân công Quản trò (Host / DM) cho phòng chơi
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `dm_assignments`;
CREATE TABLE `dm_assignments` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `dm_user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `assigned_by` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `assigned_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `responded_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `revoked_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `reason` TEXT NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_dm_assignment_room_id` UNIQUE (`game_room_id`, `id`),
    KEY `idx_dm_assignments_dm_status` (`dm_user_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_dm_assignments_room_status` (`game_room_id`, `status`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_dm_assignment_room`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`) REFERENCES `game_rooms`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_dm_assignment_user`
        FOREIGN KEY (`dm_user_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_dm_assignment_assigner`
        FOREIGN KEY (`assigned_by`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_dm_assignment_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('PENDING', 'ACCEPTED', 'REJECTED', 'REVOKED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 5: VENUE & PHYSICAL ROOM (CƠ SỞ VẬT CHẤT & PHÒNG THỰC TẾ)
-- =============================================================

-- 8. venues: Địa điểm, chi nhánh cửa hàng Kịch Bản Sát
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `venues`;
CREATE TABLE `venues` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `name` VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    `address` VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    `phone` VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    `description` TEXT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_venues_status` (`status`),

    CONSTRAINT `chk_venues_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 9. physical_rooms: Phòng chơi thực tế ngoài đời tại chi nhánh
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `physical_rooms`;
CREATE TABLE `physical_rooms` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `venue_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `name` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `capacity` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `description` TEXT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_physical_room_venue_name` UNIQUE (`venue_id`, `name`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_physical_room_venue_id` UNIQUE (`venue_id`, `id`),
    KEY `idx_physical_rooms_venue_status` (`venue_id`, `status`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_physical_rooms_venue`
        FOREIGN KEY (`venue_id`) REFERENCES `venues`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_physical_room_capacity` CHECK (`capacity` > 0),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_physical_room_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 6: SCHEDULING & SESSION (ĐỀ XUẤT LỊCH & BUỔI CHƠI)
-- =============================================================

-- 10. schedule_proposals: Đề xuất lịch chơi do thành viên khởi tạo
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `schedule_proposals`;
CREATE TABLE `schedule_proposals` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `proposed_by` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `proposed_start_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `proposed_end_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PROPOSED',

    -- Đảm bảo mỗi GameRoom chỉ có tối đa 1 đề xuất đang mở bình chọn cùng lúc
    `active_game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
            CASE WHEN `status` = 'PROPOSED' THEN `game_room_id` ELSE NULL END
        ) STORED,

    `expires_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `confirmed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `rejected_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_active_schedule_proposal` UNIQUE (`active_game_room_id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_schedule_proposal_room_id` UNIQUE (`game_room_id`, `id`),
    KEY `idx_schedule_proposals_room_status` (`game_room_id`, `status`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_schedule_proposal_room`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`) REFERENCES `game_rooms`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_schedule_proposal_user`
        FOREIGN KEY (`proposed_by`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_schedule_proposal_time`
        CHECK (`proposed_start_at` < `proposed_end_at`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_schedule_proposal_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('PROPOSED', 'CONFIRMED', 'REJECTED', 'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 11. schedule_responses: Biểu quyết của từng thành viên với đề xuất lịch
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `schedule_responses`;
CREATE TABLE `schedule_responses` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `schedule_proposal_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `room_participant_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `response` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `responded_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_schedule_response_participant`
        UNIQUE (`schedule_proposal_id`, `room_participant_id`),
    KEY `idx_schedule_responses_room_response` (`game_room_id`, `response`),

    -- Composite FK chống vote chéo phòng
    CONSTRAINT `fk_schedule_response_proposal`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`)
        REFERENCES `schedule_proposals`(`game_room_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_schedule_response_participant`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`, `room_participant_id`)
        REFERENCES `room_participants`(`game_room_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_schedule_response`
        CHECK (`response` IN ('ACCEPTED', 'REJECTED', 'PENDING'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 12. sessions: Buổi chơi thực tế đã chốt lịch, phòng và DM
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `sessions`;
CREATE TABLE `sessions` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `schedule_proposal_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `dm_assignment_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `venue_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `physical_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `start_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `end_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SCHEDULED',
    `started_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `completed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `cancelled_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `cancel_reason` TEXT NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_session_game_room` UNIQUE (`game_room_id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_session_schedule_proposal` UNIQUE (`schedule_proposal_id`),

    KEY `idx_sessions_room_status` (`game_room_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_sessions_physical_time` (`physical_room_id`, `status`, `start_at`, `end_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_sessions_proposal`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`)
        REFERENCES `schedule_proposals`(`game_room_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_sessions_dm_assignment`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`, `dm_assignment_id`)
        REFERENCES `dm_assignments`(`game_room_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_sessions_physical_room`
        FOREIGN KEY (`venue_id`, `physical_room_id`)
        REFERENCES `physical_rooms`(`venue_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_sessions_time` CHECK (`start_at` < `end_at`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_sessions_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('SCHEDULED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 7: PRICING & PROMOTION (GIÁ & KHUYẾN MÃI)
-- =============================================================

-- 13. game_prices: Bảng giá kịch bản theo khung giờ và khoảng ngày hiệu lực
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `game_prices`;
CREATE TABLE `game_prices` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `price_per_person` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `start_time` TIME NOT NULL,
    `end_time` TIME NOT NULL,
    `effective_from` DATE NOT NULL,
    `effective_until` DATE NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_game_prices_lookup`
        (`game_id`, `status`, `effective_from`, `effective_until`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_game_prices_game`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_id`) REFERENCES `games`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_prices_amount` CHECK (`price_per_person` > 0),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_prices_time` CHECK (`start_time` < `end_time`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_prices_period`
        CHECK (`effective_until` IS NULL OR `effective_until` >= `effective_from`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_game_prices_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 14. promotions: Chương trình khuyến mãi, voucher giảm giá
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `promotions`;
CREATE TABLE `promotions` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `code` VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    `name` VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    `promotion_type` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    `discount_type` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    `discount_value` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `start_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `end_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,
    `rules` JSON NULL,
    `is_active` TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_promotions_code` UNIQUE (`code`),
    KEY `idx_promotions_active_period` (`is_active`, `start_at`, `end_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `chk_promotions_period` CHECK (`end_at` > `start_at`),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_promotions_discount_type`
        CHECK (`discount_type` IN ('PERCENT', 'FIXED')),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_promotions_discount_value` CHECK (`discount_value` > 0),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_promotions_active` CHECK (`is_active` IN (0, 1)),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_promotions_percent`
        CHECK (`discount_type` <> 'PERCENT' OR `discount_value` <= 100)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- Liên kết khóa ngoại giữa game_rooms và promotions
ALTER TABLE `game_rooms`
    ADD CONSTRAINT `fk_game_rooms_price_promotion`
    FOREIGN KEY (`price_promotion_id`) REFERENCES `promotions`(`id`)
    ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT;

-- 15. participant_price_snapshots: Chi tiết snapshot giá cho từng thành viên
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `participant_price_snapshots`;
CREATE TABLE `participant_price_snapshots` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `game_room_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `room_participant_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `base_price` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `discount_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `final_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `deposit_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `remaining_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `promotion_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_participant_price_snapshot` UNIQUE (`room_participant_id`),
    KEY `idx_participant_snapshot_room` (`game_room_id`),
    KEY `idx_participant_snapshot_promotion` (`promotion_id`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_participant_snapshot_room_participant`
        FOREIGN KEY (`game_room_id`, `room_participant_id`)
        REFERENCES `room_participants`(`game_room_id`, `id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_participant_snapshot_promotion`
        FOREIGN KEY (`promotion_id`) REFERENCES `promotions`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_participant_snapshot_amounts`
        CHECK (
            `discount_amount` <= `base_price`
            AND `final_amount` = `base_price` - `discount_amount`
            AND `deposit_amount` + `remaining_amount` = `final_amount`
        )
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 8: PAYMENT & REFUND (THANH TOÁN & HOÀN TIỀN)
-- =============================================================

-- 16. player_payments: Theo dõi công nợ của người chơi (Cọc & Tất toán)
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `player_payments`;
CREATE TABLE `player_payments` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `room_participant_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `payment_type` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    `amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `paid_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `deadline_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `paid_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `expired_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_player_payment_type` UNIQUE (`room_participant_id`, `payment_type`),
    KEY `idx_player_payments_status_deadline` (`status`, `deadline_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_player_payments_participant`
        FOREIGN KEY (`room_participant_id`) REFERENCES `room_participants`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_player_payment_type`
        CHECK (`payment_type` IN ('DEPOSIT', 'REMAINING')),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_player_payment_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN (
            'PENDING', 'PROCESSING', 'PAID', 'PARTIALLY_PAID',
            'FAILED', 'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED'
        )),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_player_payment_amount`
        CHECK (`amount` > 0 AND `paid_amount` <= `amount`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 17. payment_transactions: Lịch sử giao dịch thanh toán (có Idempotency Key)
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `payment_transactions`;
CREATE TABLE `payment_transactions` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `player_payment_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `idempotency_key` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `transaction_reference` VARCHAR(150) NULL,
    `amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `provider_response` JSON NULL,
    `requested_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `processed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_payment_transaction_idempotency` UNIQUE (`idempotency_key`),
    KEY `idx_payment_transactions_payment_created` (`player_payment_id`, `created_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_payment_transactions_payment`
        FOREIGN KEY (`player_payment_id`) REFERENCES `player_payments`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_payment_transaction_amount` CHECK (`amount` > 0),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_payment_transaction_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('PENDING', 'SUCCESS', 'FAILED', 'CANCELLED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 18. refunds: Yêu cầu và lịch sử hoàn tiền
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `refunds`;
CREATE TABLE `refunds` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `player_payment_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `reason` TEXT NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `requested_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `reviewed_by` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `reviewed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `completed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `review_reason` TEXT NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `updated_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3)
        ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_refunds_payment_status` (`player_payment_id`, `status`),
    KEY `idx_refunds_reviewer` (`reviewed_by`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_refunds_payment`
        FOREIGN KEY (`player_payment_id`) REFERENCES `player_payments`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT `fk_refunds_reviewer`
        FOREIGN KEY (`reviewed_by`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_refunds_amount` CHECK (`amount` > 0),
    CONSTRAINT `chk_refunds_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN (
            'PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED',
            'PROCESSING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED'
        ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- DOMAIN 9: NOTIFICATIONS (THÔNG BÁO)
-- =============================================================

-- 19. notifications: Thông báo gửi tới người dùng
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `notifications`;
CREATE TABLE `notifications` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `type` VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    `title` VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    `content` TEXT NOT NULL,
    `reference_type` VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    `reference_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `is_read` TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `read_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_notifications_user_read_created` (`user_id`, `is_read`, `created_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_notifications_user`
        FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_notifications_is_read` CHECK (`is_read` IN (0, 1))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- =============================================================
-- INFRASTRUCTURE: IDEMPOTENCY & OUTBOX (HẠ TẦNG HỆ THỐNG)
-- =============================================================

-- 20. idempotency_records: Chống gọi trùng lặp API quan trọng
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `idempotency_records`;
CREATE TABLE `idempotency_records` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `idempotency_key` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `request_hash` CHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PROCESSING',
    `response_code` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `response_body` JSON NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `expires_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    CONSTRAINT `uq_idempotency_user_key` UNIQUE (`user_id`, `idempotency_key`),
    KEY `idx_idempotency_expiry` (`status`, `expires_at`),

    CONSTRAINT `fk_idempotency_user`
        FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`id`)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE RESTRICT,

    CONSTRAINT `chk_idempotency_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('PROCESSING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

-- 21. outbox_events: Đảm bảo giao phát sự kiện an toàn (Transactional Outbox Pattern)
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `outbox_events`;
CREATE TABLE `outbox_events` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `aggregate_type` VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    `aggregate_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    `event_type` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `payload` JSON NOT NULL,
    `status` VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    `attempts` SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    `available_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    `processed_at` DATETIME(3) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),

    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_outbox_dispatch` (`status`, `available_at`),
    KEY `idx_outbox_aggregate` (`aggregate_type`, `aggregate_id`),

    CONSTRAINT `chk_outbox_status`
        CHECK (`status` IN ('PENDING', 'PROCESSING', 'PROCESSED', 'FAILED'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;

FILE: backend\database\upgrade-stage3.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

require dirname(__DIR__) . '/vendor/autoload.php';

use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;

Config::load(dirname(__DIR__));
$pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

// 1. Tạo bảng audit_logs
$pdo->exec("
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `audit_logs` (
    `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    `actor_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `action` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    `entity_type` VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    `entity_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    `metadata` JSON NULL,
    `ip_address` VARCHAR(45) NULL,
    `created_at` DATETIME(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),
    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    KEY `idx_audit_actor_created` (`actor_id`, `created_at`),
    KEY `idx_audit_entity` (`entity_type`, `entity_id`),
    KEY `idx_audit_action` (`action`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
");
echo "[OK] Table audit_logs ready.\n";

// 2. Thêm cột student_verified vào bảng users nếu chưa có
$colCheck = $pdo->query("
    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
    WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
      AND TABLE_NAME = 'users'
      AND COLUMN_NAME = 'student_verified'
");
if ((int) $colCheck->fetchColumn() === 0) {
    $pdo->exec("ALTER TABLE `users` ADD COLUMN `student_verified` TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER `status`");
    echo "[OK] Added column users.student_verified.\n";
} else {
    echo "[OK] Column users.student_verified already exists.\n";
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\database\verify-connection.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * CLI script to verify database connectivity safely without exposing passwords.
 * Usage: php database/verify-connection.php
 */

$basePath = dirname(__DIR__);
require_once $basePath . '/vendor/autoload.php';

use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseException;

Config::load($basePath);

$host = Config::get('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1');
$port = Config::get('DB_PORT', 3306);
$database = Config::get('DB_DATABASE', 'booking_v16');
$username = Config::get('DB_USERNAME', 'root');

echo "============================================\n";
echo "  Kịch Bản Sát V16 - DB Connectivity Check  \n";
echo "============================================\n";
echo "Target Host: {$host}:{$port}\n";
echo "Database:    {$database}\n";
echo "User:        {$username}\n";
echo "Status:      Connecting...\n";

try {
    $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
    $connected = DatabaseConnection::checkConnectivity($pdo);

    if ($connected) {
        $serverVersion = $pdo->query('SELECT VERSION()')->fetchColumn();
        echo "RESULT:      SUCCESS\n";
        echo "Server:      MySQL {$serverVersion}\n";
        echo "PDO Mode:    ERRMODE_EXCEPTION enabled\n";
        echo "Emulation:   PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES disabled\n";
        exit(0);
    } else {
        echo "RESULT:      FAILED (ping returned false)\n";
        exit(1);
    }
} catch (DatabaseException $e) {
    echo "RESULT:      FAILED\n";
    echo "Error:       " . $e->getMessage() . "\n";
    exit(1);
} catch (Throwable $e) {
    echo "RESULT:      ERROR\n";
    echo "Error:       " . $e->getMessage() . "\n";
    exit(1);
}

            CONSTRAINT `chk_player_payments_amounts` CHECK (`amount_due` >= 0 AND `amount_paid` >= 0)
        ) ENGINE = InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8mb4 COLLATE = utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

        -- 19. payment_transactions: Accounting ledger entries for confirmed payments
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `payment_transactions` (
            `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
            `player_payment_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            `transaction_reference` VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
            `idempotency_key` VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
            `payment_method` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SIMULATED',
            `amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            `currency` VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'VND',
            `status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SUCCESS',
            `transacted_at` TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            `metadata` JSON NULL,
            `created_at` TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
            UNIQUE KEY `uq_payment_transactions_reference` (`transaction_reference`),
            UNIQUE KEY `uq_payment_transactions_idempotency` (`idempotency_key`),
            INDEX `idx_payment_transactions_payment` (`player_payment_id`),
            CONSTRAINT `fk_payment_transactions_payment` FOREIGN KEY (`player_payment_id`) REFERENCES `player_payments` (`id`) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
            CONSTRAINT `chk_payment_transactions_amount` CHECK (`amount` > 0)
        ) ENGINE = InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8mb4 COLLATE = utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

        -- 20. refunds: Refund requests and ledger
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `refunds` (
            `id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
            `player_payment_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            `room_participant_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            `processed_by_user_id` BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
            `idempotency_key` VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
            `reason` TEXT NOT NULL,
            `refund_type` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FULL',
            `refund_amount` BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            `status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
            `processed_at` TIMESTAMP NULL,
            `created_at` TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            `updated_at` TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
            UNIQUE KEY `uq_refunds_idempotency` (`idempotency_key`),
            INDEX `idx_refunds_payment_status` (`player_payment_id`, `status`),
            INDEX `idx_refunds_participant` (`room_participant_id`),
            INDEX `idx_refunds_processor` (`processed_by_user_id`),
            CONSTRAINT `fk_refunds_payment` FOREIGN KEY (`player_payment_id`) REFERENCES `player_payments` (`id`) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
            CONSTRAINT `fk_refunds_participant` FOREIGN KEY (`room_participant_id`) REFERENCES `room_participants` (`id`) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
            CONSTRAINT `fk_refunds_processor` FOREIGN KEY (`processed_by_user_id`) REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
            CONSTRAINT `chk_refunds_amount` CHECK (`refund_amount` > 0)
        ) ENGINE = InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8mb4 COLLATE = utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

        SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;
        SQL);
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        $this->execute(<<<SQL
        SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0;
        DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `refunds`;
        DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `payment_transactions`;
        DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `player_payments`;
        SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;
        SQL);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Phinx\Seed\AbstractSeed;

class ReferenceDataSeeder extends AbstractSeed
{
    /**
     * Run Method.
     *
     * Idempotent seeding of essential reference venue and physical rooms.
     */
    public function run(): void
    {
        // 1. Primary Venue
        $venueCode = 'VENUE_01';
        $existingVenue = $this->fetchRow(
            sprintf("SELECT id FROM `venues` WHERE `code` = '%s' LIMIT 1", $venueCode)
        );

        if (!$existingVenue) {
            $this->table('venues')->insert([
                [
                    'code' => $venueCode,
                    'name' => 'Kịch Bản Sát Station - Chi nhánh Trung Tâm',
                    'address' => '123 Nguyễn Huệ, P. Bến Nghé, Quận 1, TP. Hồ Chí Minh',
                    'phone' => '0901234567',
                    'opening_hours' => '09:00 - 23:00',
                    'is_active' => 1,
                ]
            ])->saveData();
            $venueId = (int) $this->getAdapter()->getConnection()->lastInsertId();
        } else {
            $venueId = (int) $existingVenue['id'];
        }

        // 2. Physical Rooms at Store Venue
        $rooms = [
            [
                'venue_id' => $venueId,
                'code' => 'ROOM_01',
                'name' => 'Phòng Án Mạng 01',
                'capacity' => 8,
                'status' => 'AVAILABLE',
                'description' => 'Phòng chơi tiêu chuẩn phong cách cổ điển Châu Âu',
            ],
            [
                'venue_id' => $venueId,
                'code' => 'ROOM_02',
                'name' => 'Phòng Án Mạng 02',
                'capacity' => 8,
                'status' => 'AVAILABLE',
                'description' => 'Phòng chơi phong cách Á Đông truyền thống',
            ],
            [
                'venue_id' => $venueId,
                'code' => 'ROOM_VIP',
                'name' => 'Phòng VIP Hoàng Gia',
                'capacity' => 10,
                'status' => 'AVAILABLE',
                'description' => 'Phòng lớn chuyên dụng kịch bản đông người',
            ],
        ];

        foreach ($rooms as $room) {
            $existing = $this->fetchRow(
                sprintf("SELECT id FROM `physical_rooms` WHERE `code` = '%s' LIMIT 1", $room['code'])
            );

            if (!$existing) {
                $this->table('physical_rooms')->insert([$room])->saveData();
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: backend\database\seeders\RoleSeeder.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Phinx\Seed\AbstractSeed;

class RoleSeeder extends AbstractSeed
{
    /**
     * Run Method.
     *
     * Idempotent seeding of essential system roles.
     */
    public function run(): void
    {
        $roles = [
            [
                'name' => 'ADMIN',
                'label' => 'Quản trị viên',
                'description' => 'Toàn quyền quản trị và điều phối hệ thống',
            ],
            [
                'name' => 'DM',
                'label' => 'Quản trò (Dungeon Master)',
                'description' => 'Quản lý phiên chơi, kịch bản và dẫn truyện',
            ],
            [
                'name' => 'PLAYER',
                'label' => 'Người chơi',
                'description' => 'Người dùng tham gia ghép đội, đặt phòng và chơi game',
            ],
        ];

        foreach ($roles as $role) {
            $existing = $this->fetchRow(
                sprintf("SELECT id FROM `roles` WHERE `name` = '%s' LIMIT 1", $role['name'])
            );

            if (!$existing) {
                $this->table('roles')->insert([$role])->saveData();
            } else {
                $this->execute(sprintf(
                    "UPDATE `roles` SET `label` = '%s', `description` = '%s', `updated_at` = NOW() WHERE `name` = '%s'",
                    addslashes($role['label']),
                    addslashes($role['description']),
                    $role['name']
                ));
            }
        }
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\public\index.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * Kịch Bản Sát V16 - Front Controller & Same-Origin Static Asset Server
 */

$requestUri = $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/';
$path = parse_url($requestUri, PHP_URL_PATH) ?? '/';

// Serve frontend static files if request is not an API call
if (!str_starts_with($path, '/api')) {
    $projectRoot = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
    $frontendDir = $projectRoot . '/frontend';

    if ($path === '/' || $path === '/index.html') {
        header('Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8');
        readfile($frontendDir . '/index.html');
        exit;
    }

    $filePath = realpath($frontendDir . $path);
    $realFrontendDir = realpath($frontendDir);

    // Path traversal check
    if ($filePath && $realFrontendDir && str_starts_with($filePath, $realFrontendDir) && is_file($filePath)) {
        $ext = strtolower(pathinfo($filePath, PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
        $mimeTypes = [
            'js' => 'application/javascript',
            'mjs' => 'application/javascript',
            'css' => 'text/css',
            'html' => 'text/html',
            'json' => 'application/json',
            'png' => 'image/png',
            'jpg' => 'image/jpeg',
            'svg' => 'image/svg+xml',
            'ico' => 'image/x-icon',
        ];
        $contentType = $mimeTypes[$ext] ?? 'text/plain';
        header("Content-Type: {$contentType}; charset=utf-8");
        readfile($filePath);
        exit;
    }
}

// Delegate API requests to application router
$app = require_once __DIR__ . '/../bootstrap/app.php';

$request = App\Core\Request::capture();
$response = $app->dispatch($request);
$response->send();

FILE: backend\routes\api.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Core\Router;
use App\Features\Identity\Controllers\AuthController;
use App\Features\Identity\Controllers\AdminUserController;
use App\Features\Identity\Controllers\AdminAuditLogController;
use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers\AdminOperationsController;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers\PaymentRefundRequestController;
use App\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers\SessionLifecycleController;
use App\Features\DMManagement\Controllers\DMAssignmentController;
use App\Features\GameCatalog\Controllers\GameController;
use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers\GameRoomController;
use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Controllers\AdminGameRoomController;
use App\Features\VenueScheduling\Controllers\SchedulingController;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers\PaymentController;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Controllers\AdminRefundController;
use App\Features\Notification\Controllers\NotificationController;
use App\Features\Pricing\Controllers\PricingController;
use App\Features\Promotion\Controllers\PromotionController;

/** @var Router $router */

$registerApiRoutes = function (string $prefix) use ($router): void {
    // Health check endpoint
    $router->get($prefix . '/health', function (Request $request): Response {
        return Response::success([
            'status' => 'ok',
            'application' => 'Kich Ban Sat',
            'version' => '16',
        ], 'API Service Operational');
    });

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 1: AUTH & IDENTITY API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/auth/csrf-token', [AuthController::class, 'csrfToken']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/auth/register', [AuthController::class, 'register']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/auth/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/auth/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/auth/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);

    // Admin User & Role Management
    $router->get($prefix . '/admin/users', [AdminUserController::class, 'index']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/admin/users/{id}', [AdminUserController::class, 'show']);
    $router->patch($prefix . '/admin/users/{id}', [AdminUserController::class, 'update']);
    $router->put($prefix . '/admin/users/{id}/role', [AdminUserController::class, 'assignRole']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/admin/audit-logs', [AdminAuditLogController::class, 'index']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/admin/rooms/{id}/assign-dm', [AdminOperationsController::class, 'assignDm']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/dm/assignments/{id}/revoke', [AdminOperationsController::class, 'revokeDmAssignment']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/admin/rooms/{id}/assign-venue', [AdminOperationsController::class, 'assignVenue']);
    $router->put($prefix . '/admin/users/{id}/student-verification', [AdminOperationsController::class, 'updateStudentVerification']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/sessions/{id}/start', [SessionLifecycleController::class, 'start']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/sessions/{id}/complete', [SessionLifecycleController::class, 'complete']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/payments/{id}/refund-request', [PaymentRefundRequestController::class, 'requestRefund']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 2: GAME CATALOG & PROMOTIONS API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/games', [GameController::class, 'index']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/games/{id}', [GameController::class, 'show']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/games/{id}/prices', [GameController::class, 'prices']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/promotions', [PromotionController::class, 'index']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 3: GAMEROOM MANAGEMENT & PRICING SNAPSHOT API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/rooms', [GameRoomController::class, 'index']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/rooms', [GameRoomController::class, 'create']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/rooms/{id}', [GameRoomController::class, 'show']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/join', [GameRoomController::class, 'join']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/leave', [GameRoomController::class, 'leave']);

    // Pricing calculation & snapshot
    $router->post($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/calculate-price', [PricingController::class, 'calculate']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/price-snapshot', [PricingController::class, 'snapshot']);

    // Admin GameRoom Cancel with 24-hour Cutoff
    $router->post($prefix . '/admin/rooms/{id}/cancel', [AdminGameRoomController::class, 'cancel']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 4 & 5: SCHEDULING, VOTING & VENUES API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/venues', [SchedulingController::class, 'venues']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/proposals', [SchedulingController::class, 'propose']);
    $router->get($prefix . '/proposals/{id}', [SchedulingController::class, 'showProposal']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/proposals/{id}/vote', [SchedulingController::class, 'vote']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 6: DM ASSIGNMENT API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/dm/assignments', [DMAssignmentController::class, 'index']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/dm/assignments/{id}/accept', [DMAssignmentController::class, 'accept']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/dm/assignments/{id}/reject', [DMAssignmentController::class, 'reject']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 7: PAYMENT & REFUND API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/rooms/{id}/payments', [PaymentController::class, 'listByRoom']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/payments/{paymentId}/deposit', [PaymentController::class, 'deposit']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/payments/{paymentId}/remaining', [PaymentController::class, 'remaining']);

    // Admin Refunds Management
    $router->get($prefix . '/admin/refunds', [AdminRefundController::class, 'index']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/admin/refunds/{id}/approve', [AdminRefundController::class, 'approve']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/admin/refunds/{id}/reject', [AdminRefundController::class, 'reject']);

    // =============================================================
    // DOMAIN 8: NOTIFICATION API
    // =============================================================
    $router->get($prefix . '/notifications', [NotificationController::class, 'index']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/notifications/{id}/read', [NotificationController::class, 'markAsRead']);
    $router->post($prefix . '/notifications/read-all', [NotificationController::class, 'markAllAsRead']);
};

// Register for both standard V16 prefix (/api/v1) and legacy fallback (/api)
$registerApiRoutes('/api/v1');
$registerApiRoutes('/api');

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\AdminRoomCancelRefundApiTest.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class AdminRoomCancelRefundApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;
    protected PaymentRepository $paymentRepo;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->paymentRepo = new PaymentRepository($pdo);

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testNonAdminCannotCancelRoom(): void
    {
        $req = new Request('POST', '/api/v1/admin/rooms/1/cancel', [], ['reason' => 'Bận đột xuất'], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 3']);
        $res = $this->app->dispatch($req);
        $this->assertSame(403, $res->getStatusCode());
    }

    public function testCancelRoomRequiresReason(): void
    {
        $req = new Request('POST', '/api/v1/admin/rooms/1/cancel', [], ['reason' => '   '], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $res = $this->app->dispatch($req);
        $this->assertSame(422, $res->getStatusCode());
    }

    public function testCancelRoomEligibleForRefundWhenMoreThan24Hours(): void
    {
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

        // 1. Tạo phòng test
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`, `formation_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 1, 3, 'Phòng test hoàn cọc trước 24h', 6, 8, 'SCHEDULED', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 3 DAY), NOW(), NOW())");
        $roomId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        // 2. Tạo participant và payment cho User 3 (đã nạp 50k)
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 3, 'DEPOSITED', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $partId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $payId = $this->paymentRepo->createPayment([
            'room_participant_id' => $partId,
            'payment_type' => 'DEPOSIT',
            'amount' => 50000,
            'paid_amount' => 50000,
            'status' => 'PAID',
        ]);

        // 3. Tạo Proposal và Session bắt đầu sau 48 tiếng (> 24h)
        $startAt = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+48 hours'));
        $endAt = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+52 hours'));

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `schedule_proposals` (`game_room_id`, `proposed_by`, `proposed_start_at`, `proposed_end_at`, `status`, `expires_at`, `created_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 3, '{$startAt}', '{$endAt}', 'CONFIRMED', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 24 HOUR), NOW())");
        $propId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `dm_assignments` (`game_room_id`, `dm_user_id`, `assigned_by`, `status`, `assigned_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 2, 1, 'ACCEPTED', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $dmAssignId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `sessions` (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`, `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, {$propId}, {$dmAssignId}, 1, 1, '{$startAt}', '{$endAt}', 'SCHEDULED', NOW(), NOW())");

        // 4. Admin hủy phòng
        $cancelReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/admin/rooms/{$roomId}/cancel",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'reason' => 'Bảo trì chi nhánh đột xuất',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $cancelRes = $this->app->dispatch($cancelReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $cancelRes->getStatusCode());

        $cancelData = json_decode($cancelRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($cancelData['success']);
        $this->assertTrue($cancelData['data']['is_eligible_for_refund']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($cancelData['data']['refunds']);
        $this->assertSame('APPROVED', $cancelData['data']['refunds'][0]['status']);
    }

    public function testCancelRoomNotEligibleForRefundWhenLessThan24Hours(): void
    {
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

        // 1. Tạo phòng test
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`, `formation_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 1, 3, 'Phòng test không hoàn cọc dưới 24h', 6, 8, 'SCHEDULED', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 3 DAY), NOW(), NOW())");
        $roomId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        // 2. Tạo participant và payment cho User 3 (đã nạp 50k)
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 3, 'DEPOSITED', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $partId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $payId = $this->paymentRepo->createPayment([
            'room_participant_id' => $partId,
            'payment_type' => 'DEPOSIT',
            'amount' => 50000,
            'paid_amount' => 50000,
            'status' => 'PAID',
        ]);

        // 3. Tạo Proposal và Session bắt đầu sau 6 tiếng (< 24h)
        $startAt = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+6 hours'));
        $endAt = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+10 hours'));

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `schedule_proposals` (`game_room_id`, `proposed_by`, `proposed_start_at`, `proposed_end_at`, `status`, `expires_at`, `created_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 3, '{$startAt}', '{$endAt}', 'CONFIRMED', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 24 HOUR), NOW())");
        $propId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `dm_assignments` (`game_room_id`, `dm_user_id`, `assigned_by`, `status`, `assigned_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 2, 1, 'ACCEPTED', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $dmAssignId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `sessions` (`game_room_id`, `schedule_proposal_id`, `dm_assignment_id`, `venue_id`, `physical_room_id`, `start_at`, `end_at`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, {$propId}, {$dmAssignId}, 1, 1, '{$startAt}', '{$endAt}', 'SCHEDULED', NOW(), NOW())");

        // 4. Admin hủy phòng
        $cancelReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/admin/rooms/{$roomId}/cancel",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'reason' => 'Hủy do sự cố thời tiết cận giờ chơi',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $cancelRes = $this->app->dispatch($cancelReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $cancelRes->getStatusCode());

        $cancelData = json_decode($cancelRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($cancelData['success']);
        // Vi phạm 24h: không đủ điều kiện
        $this->assertFalse($cancelData['data']['is_eligible_for_refund']);
        $this->assertSame('REJECTED', $cancelData['data']['refunds'][0]['status']);
    }

    public function testAdminCanListAndReviewRefunds(): void
    {
        // 1. List refunds
        $listReq = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/admin/refunds', ['page' => 1, 'per_page' => 5], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $listRes = $this->app->dispatch($listReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $listRes->getStatusCode());

        $listData = json_decode($listRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($listData['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($listData['data']);

        $paymentId = (int) $listData['data'][0]['player_payment_id'];
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

        // 2. Tạo 1 yêu cầu PENDING -> Admin reject thành công (200), sau đó cố approve đè bị chặn (409)
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `refunds` (`player_payment_id`, `amount`, `reason`, `status`, `requested_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$paymentId}, 50000, 'Yêu cầu hoàn cọc test reject', 'PENDING', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $rejectTargetId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $rejReq = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/admin/refunds/{$rejectTargetId}/reject", [], ['reason' => 'Từ chối giải ngân'], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $rejRes = $this->app->dispatch($rejReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $rejRes->getStatusCode());

        $reApproveReq = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/admin/refunds/{$rejectTargetId}/approve", [], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $reApproveRes = $this->app->dispatch($reApproveReq);
        $this->assertSame(409, $reApproveRes->getStatusCode());

        // 3. Tạo 1 yêu cầu PENDING khác -> Admin approve thành công (200), sau đó cố reject đè bị chặn (409)
        $pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `refunds` (`player_payment_id`, `amount`, `reason`, `status`, `requested_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$paymentId}, 50000, 'Yêu cầu hoàn cọc test approve', 'PENDING', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $approveTargetId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();

        $appReq = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/admin/refunds/{$approveTargetId}/approve", [], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $appRes = $this->app->dispatch($appReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $appRes->getStatusCode());

        $reRejectReq = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/admin/refunds/{$approveTargetId}/reject", [], ['reason' => 'Từ chối sau khi đã duyệt'], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $reRejectRes = $this->app->dispatch($reRejectReq);
        $this->assertSame(409, $reRejectRes->getStatusCode());
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class AdminUserApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testNonAdminCannotListUsers(): void
    {
        // 1. Unauthenticated request
        $req1 = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/admin/users');
        $res1 = $this->app->dispatch($req1);
        $this->assertSame(401, $res1->getStatusCode());

        // 2. Authenticated as PLAYER (User 3)
        $req2 = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $res2 = $this->app->dispatch($req2);
        $this->assertSame(403, $res2->getStatusCode());
        $data2 = json_decode($res2->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data2['success']);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $data2['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testAdminCanListUsersWithPaginationAndFilters(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users',
            queryParams: [
                'page' => 1,
                'per_page' => 5,
            ],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1', // Admin
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertIsArray($data['data']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']);
        $this->assertNotNull($data['meta']['pagination']);
        $this->assertSame(1, $data['meta']['pagination']['page']);
        $this->assertSame(5, $data['meta']['pagination']['per_page']);
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(4, $data['meta']['pagination']['total']);
    }

    public function testAdminCanGetUserDetail(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/1',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $data['data']['user']['id']);
        $this->assertSame('ADMIN', $data['data']['user']['role_code']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('password_hash', $data['data']['user']);
    }

    public function testAdminCanUpdateUser(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'PATCH',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/3',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'phone' => '0987654321',
                'status' => 'ACTIVE',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('0987654321', $data['data']['user']['phone']);
    }

    public function testAdminCannotSuspendSelf(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'PATCH',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/1',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'status' => 'SUSPENDED',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(400, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('CANNOT_SUSPEND_SELF', $data['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testAdminCanAssignRoleToUser(): void
    {
        // Gán role DM cho User 3 (Nguyễn Xuân Hiếu)
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'PUT',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/3/role',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'role_code' => 'DM',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('DM', $data['data']['user']['role_code']);

        // Khôi phục lại role PLAYER cho User 3
        $revert = new Request(
            method: 'PUT',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/3/role',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'role_code' => 'PLAYER',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );
        $this->app->dispatch($revert);
    }

    public function testAdminCannotDemoteSelf(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'PUT',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/1/role',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'role_code' => 'PLAYER',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(400, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('CANNOT_DEMOTE_SELF', $data['error']['code']);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\ApplicationTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Exceptions\BadRequestException;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class ApplicationTest extends TestCase
{
    private Application $app;
    private array $capturedLogs = [];

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        Config::reset();
        Config::set('APP_DEBUG', false);
        $this->capturedLogs = [];
        \App\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler::setLogger(function (string $message) {
            $this->capturedLogs[] = $message;
        });
        $this->app = require __DIR__ . '/../../bootstrap/app.php';
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        \App\Core\Exceptions\ExceptionHandler::setLogger(null);
        Config::reset();
        parent::tearDown();
    }

    public function testHealthEndpointReturnsSuccessJson(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/health');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($body['success']);
        $this->assertSame('ok', $body['data']['status']);
        $this->assertSame('Kich Ban Sat', $body['data']['application']);
        $this->assertSame('16', $body['data']['version']);
    }

    public function testUnknownRouteReturns404Json(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/unknown-endpoint');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertFalse($body['success']);
        $this->assertSame('NOT_FOUND', $body['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testUnsupportedMethodReturns405WithAllowHeader(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('POST', '/api/health');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(405, $response->getStatusCode());
        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertArrayHasKey('Allow', $headers);
        $this->assertSame('GET', $headers['Allow']);

        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($body['success']);
        $this->assertSame('METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED', $body['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testExceptionHandlerHandlesCustomBadRequest(): void
    {
        $this->app->getRouter()->post('/api/test-bad-request', function () {
            throw new BadRequestException('Invalid payload provided', ['field' => 'email is required']);
        });

        $request = new Request('POST', '/api/test-bad-request');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(400, $response->getStatusCode());
        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertFalse($body['success']);
        $this->assertSame('BAD_REQUEST', $body['error']['code']);
        $this->assertSame('Invalid payload provided', $body['error']['message']);
        $this->assertSame(['field' => 'email is required'], $body['error']['details']);
    }

    public function testUncaughtExceptionInProductionHidesDetails(): void
    {
        Config::set('APP_DEBUG', false);

        $this->app->getRouter()->get('/api/server-error', function () {
            throw new \RuntimeException('Database password secret revealed');
        });

        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/server-error');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertFalse($body['success']);
        $this->assertSame('INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR', $body['error']['code']);
        $this->assertSame('Internal Server Error', $body['error']['message']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('details', $body['error']);
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('password', $response->getContent());

        // Verify captured log output is sanitized and did not leak secret
        $this->assertNotEmpty($this->capturedLogs);
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('Database password secret revealed', $this->capturedLogs[0]);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\AuthApiTest.php
