<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class AuthApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);

        // Clear session between tests
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanRegisterNewUser(): void
    {
        $uniqueEmail = 'player_' . uniqid() . '@test.vn';
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/register',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'full_name' => 'Nguyễn Văn Test',
                'email' => $uniqueEmail,
                'password' => 'password123',
                'phone' => '0912345678',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(201, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('Nguyễn Văn Test', $data['data']['user']['full_name']);
        $this->assertSame($uniqueEmail, $data['data']['user']['email']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('password_hash', $data['data']['user']);
    }

    public function testCannotRegisterWithDuplicateEmail(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/register',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'full_name' => 'Trùng Email',
                'email' => 'admin@kichbansat.vn',
                'password' => 'password123',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(409, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
    }

    public function testCanLoginWithRegisteredUser(): void
    {
        // First register
        $email = 'login_user_' . uniqid() . '@test.vn';
        $regRequest = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/register',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'full_name' => 'Login Tester',
                'email' => $email,
                'password' => 'mysecret123',
            ]
        );
        $this->app->dispatch($regRequest);

        // Clear session to simulate fresh login
        $_SESSION = [];

        // Now login
        $loginRequest = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/login',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'email' => $email,
                'password' => 'mysecret123',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($loginRequest);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame($email, $data['data']['user']['email']);
        $this->assertSame('Login Tester', $data['data']['user']['full_name']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('password_hash', $data['data']['user']);
    }

    public function testCannotLoginWithWrongPassword(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/login',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'email' => 'admin@kichbansat.vn',
                'password' => 'definitely_wrong_password',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(401, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
    }

    public function testCanGetProfileViaBearerHeader(): void
    {
        // Register a dedicated user
        $email = 'bearer_user_' . uniqid() . '@test.vn';
        $regRequest = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/register',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'full_name' => 'Bearer User',
                'email' => $email,
                'password' => 'pass123456',
            ]
        );
        $regResponse = $this->app->dispatch($regRequest);
        $regData = json_decode($regResponse->getContent(), true);
        $userId = $regData['data']['user']['id'];

        // Clear session
        $_SESSION = [];

        $request = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/auth/me',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => "Bearer {$userId}",
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('Bearer User', $data['data']['user']['full_name']);
        $this->assertSame($email, $data['data']['user']['email']);
    }

    public function testCanLogout(): void
    {
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 1;

        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/auth/logout'
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('user_id', $_SESSION);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\tests\Feature\BackgroundJobTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Services\BackgroundJobService;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class BackgroundJobTest extends TestCase
{
    protected BackgroundJobService $jobService;
    protected \PDO $pdo;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->jobService = new BackgroundJobService($this->pdo);
    }

    public function testFormationTimeoutCancelsUnderpopulatedRoom(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo phòng đã quá hạn gom người và chưa đủ min_players (6 người)
        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`, `formation_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 1, 3, 'Phòng test formation timeout', 6, 8, 'FORMING', DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 2 HOUR), NOW(), NOW())");
        $roomId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 3, 'PENDING_DEPOSIT', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");

        // 2. Chạy job
        $cancelled = $this->jobService->runFormationTimeout();
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(1, $cancelled);

        // 3. Kiểm tra DB: phòng phải là 'CANCELLED', participant là 'CANCELLED_UNPAID'
        $roomStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = {$roomId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('CANCELLED', $roomStatus);

        $partStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = {$roomId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('CANCELLED_UNPAID', $partStatus);
    }

    public function testDepositTimeoutKicksUnpaidParticipantAndOpensReplacement(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo phòng và participant đã quá hạn nộp tiền cọc
        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`, `formation_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 1, 3, 'Phòng test deposit timeout', 6, 8, 'FORMING', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 3 DAY), NOW(), NOW())");
        $roomId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `deposit_deadline_at`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 4, 'PENDING_DEPOSIT', DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 1 HOUR), NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");
        $partId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

        // 2. Chạy job
        $kicked = $this->jobService->runDepositTimeout();
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(1, $kicked);

        // 3. Participant bị chuyển thành 'CANCELLED_UNPAID'
        $partStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `id` = {$partId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('CANCELLED_UNPAID', $partStatus);

        // 4. Phòng mở replacement_status = 'OPEN'
        $rwStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `replacement_status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = {$roomId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('OPEN', $rwStatus);
    }

    public function testReplacementTimeoutClosesExpiredWindow(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo phòng có Replacement Window đã quá hạn 24 tiếng
        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `game_rooms` (`game_id`, `game_configuration_id`, `creator_id`, `title`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `status`, `formation_deadline_at`, `replacement_status`, `replacement_deadline_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 1, 3, 'Phòng test replacement window timeout', 6, 8, 'CONFIRMED', DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL 3 DAY), 'OPEN', DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 2 HOUR), NOW(), NOW())");
        $roomId = (int) $this->pdo->lastInsertId();

        $this->pdo->exec("INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES ({$roomId}, 4, 'LEAVE_REQUESTED', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())");

        // 2. Chạy job
        $closed = $this->jobService->runReplacementTimeout();
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(1, $closed);

        // 3. Phòng chuyển thành 'EXPIRED', participant thành 'LEFT'
        $rwStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `replacement_status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = {$roomId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('EXPIRED', $rwStatus);

        $partStatus = $this->pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = {$roomId} AND `user_id` = 4")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('LEFT', $partStatus);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\DMAssignmentApiTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Features\DMManagement\Repositories\DMRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class DMAssignmentApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;
    protected DMRepository $dmRepo;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->dmRepo = new DMRepository($pdo);

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testPlayerCannotAccessDMAssignments(): void
    {
        // User 3 is PLAYER
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/dm/assignments',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(403, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $data['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testDMCanListTheirAssignments(): void
    {
        // User 2 is DM (Host Hoàng Nam)
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/dm/assignments',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 2',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertIsArray($data['data']['assignments']);
    }

    public function testDMCanAcceptPendingAssignmentAndPreventsIDOR(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo assignment PENDING cho DM User 2
        $assignmentId = $this->dmRepo->assignToRoom(1, 2, 'Cần DM có kinh nghiệm kịch bản kinh dị', 1);

        // 2. Thử accept bằng tài khoản khác không phải DM này và không phải Admin (giả sử tạo tạm DM giả hoặc user khác)
        // Set user 3 thành role DM tạm để test IDOR giữa 2 DM
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $pdo->exec("UPDATE `users` SET `role_id` = (SELECT `id` FROM `roles` WHERE `code` = 'DM' LIMIT 1) WHERE `id` = 4");

        $idorReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/dm/assignments/{$assignmentId}/accept",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4', // DM khác
            ]
        );
        $idorRes = $this->app->dispatch($idorReq);
        $this->assertSame(403, $idorRes->getStatusCode());
        $idorData = json_decode($idorRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($idorData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $idorData['error']['code']);

        // 3. Đúng DM được phân công (User 2) accept
        $acceptReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/dm/assignments/{$assignmentId}/accept",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 2',
            ]
        );
        $acceptRes = $this->app->dispatch($acceptReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $acceptRes->getStatusCode());
        $acceptData = json_decode($acceptRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($acceptData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('ACCEPTED', $acceptData['data']['assignment']['status']);

        // 4. Accept lại lần nữa phải báo lỗi
        $repeatRes = $this->app->dispatch($acceptReq);
        $this->assertSame(400, $repeatRes->getStatusCode());

        // Revert user 4
        $pdo->exec("UPDATE `users` SET `role_id` = (SELECT `id` FROM `roles` WHERE `code` = 'PLAYER' LIMIT 1) WHERE `id` = 4");
    }

    public function testDMCanRejectPendingAssignmentWithReason(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo assignment PENDING cho DM User 2
        $assignmentId = $this->dmRepo->assignToRoom(1, 2, 'Phân công ca tối', 1);

        // 2. Từ chối nhưng thiếu lý do -> 422
        $emptyReasonReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/dm/assignments/{$assignmentId}/reject",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'reason' => '   ',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 2',
            ]
        );
        $emptyRes = $this->app->dispatch($emptyReasonReq);
        $this->assertSame(422, $emptyRes->getStatusCode());

        // 3. Từ chối có lý do hợp lệ -> 200
        $rejectReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/dm/assignments/{$assignmentId}/reject",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'reason' => 'Tôi bận lịch cá nhân đột xuất vào khung giờ này',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 2',
            ]
        );
        $rejectRes = $this->app->dispatch($rejectReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $rejectRes->getStatusCode());

        $rejectData = json_decode($rejectRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($rejectData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('REJECTED', $rejectData['data']['assignment']['status']);
        $this->assertSame('Tôi bận lịch cá nhân đột xuất vào khung giờ này', $rejectData['data']['assignment']['reason']);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class GameApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanListActiveGames(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['games']);
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(1, count($data['data']['games']));
    }

    public function testCanFilterGamesByPlayerCount(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games', ['players' => 6]);
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        foreach ($data['data']['games'] as $g) {
            $this->assertLessThanOrEqual(6, (int) $g['min_players']);
            $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(6, (int) $g['max_players']);
        }
    }

    public function testCanSearchGamesByName(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games', ['search' => 'Tuyết']);
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['games']);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Tuyết', $data['data']['games'][0]['name']);
        $this->assertNotNull($data['data']['games'][0]['min_price']);
    }

    public function testCanGetGameDetailById(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games/1');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('Đêm Đoạt Mệnh Tại Trang Viên', $data['data']['game']['name']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['game']['prices']);
    }

    public function testCanGetGameDetailBySlug(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games/dem-doat-menh-tai-trang-vien');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $data['data']['game']['id']);
    }

    public function testCanGetGamePrices(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games/1/prices');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $data['data']['game_id']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['prices']);
    }

    public function testNonExistentGameReturns404(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/games/999999');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class GameRoomApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanListRooms(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/rooms');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['rooms']);
    }

    public function testCanGetRoomDetail(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/rooms/1');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $data['data']['room']['id']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['room']['participants']);
    }

    public function testCanCreateRoomWhenLoggedIn(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'game_id' => 1,
                'title' => 'Phòng test tích hợp API V3',
                'description' => 'Mô tả phòng test',
                'min_players' => 6,
                'max_players' => 8,
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 1', // Admin ID
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(201, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('Phòng test tích hợp API V3', $data['data']['room']['title']);
        $this->assertSame('FORMING', $data['data']['room']['status']);
    }

    public function testCannotCreateRoomWhenNotLoggedIn(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'game_id' => 1,
                'title' => 'Phòng vô danh',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(401, $response->getStatusCode());
    }

    public function testCanJoinAndLeaveRoom(): void
    {
        // 1. User 4 (Trần Minh Anh) join Room 1
        $joinReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms/1/join',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4',
            ]
        );

        $joinRes = $this->app->dispatch($joinReq);
        // Có thể là 200 hoặc 409 nếu trước đó đã join
        if ($joinRes->getStatusCode() === 200) {
            $joinData = json_decode($joinRes->getContent(), true);
            $this->assertTrue($joinData['success']);
        } else {
            $this->assertSame(409, $joinRes->getStatusCode());
        }

        // 2. User 4 cố tình join lại -> Báo lỗi 409
        $dupReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms/1/join',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4',
            ]
        );
        $dupRes = $this->app->dispatch($dupReq);
        $this->assertSame(409, $dupRes->getStatusCode());

        // 3. User 4 rời phòng -> Trả về 200
        $leaveReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms/1/leave',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4',
            ]
        );
        $leaveRes = $this->app->dispatch($leaveReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $leaveRes->getStatusCode());
        $leaveData = json_decode($leaveRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($leaveData['success']);
    }

    public function testReplacementSlotTakeoverWhenWindowIsOpen(): void
    {
        $pdo = \App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

        // 1. Tạo phòng mới với User 3
        $createReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/rooms',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'game_id' => 1,
                'title' => 'Phòng test Replacement Takeover',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $createRes = $this->app->dispatch($createReq);
        $this->assertSame(201, $createRes->getStatusCode());
        $createData = json_decode($createRes->getContent(), true);
        $roomId = (int) $createData['data']['room']['id'];

        // 2. User 4 join vào phòng
        $join4 = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/rooms/{$roomId}/join", [], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 4']);
        $res4 = $this->app->dispatch($join4);
        $this->assertSame(200, $res4->getStatusCode());

        // 3. Giả lập phòng đã chốt lịch (status = CONFIRMED)
        $pdo->exec("UPDATE `game_rooms` SET `status` = 'CONFIRMED' WHERE `id` = {$roomId}");

        // 4. User 4 xin rút phòng -> Mở Replacement Window (OPEN)
        $leave4 = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/rooms/{$roomId}/leave", [], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 4']);
        $leaveRes4 = $this->app->dispatch($leave4);
        $this->assertSame(200, $leaveRes4->getStatusCode());
        $leaveData4 = json_decode($leaveRes4->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($leaveData4['success']);
        $this->assertSame('OPEN', $leaveData4['data']['replacement_status']);

        // 5. User 1 (Admin) tham gia thế chỗ slot của User 4
        $takeoverReq = new Request('POST', "/api/v1/rooms/{$roomId}/join", [], [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']);
        $takeoverRes = $this->app->dispatch($takeoverReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $takeoverRes->getStatusCode());

        $takeoverData = json_decode($takeoverRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($takeoverData['success']);
        $this->assertTrue($takeoverData['data']['is_replacement']);
        $this->assertTrue($takeoverData['data']['replacement_window_closed']);

        // 6. Kiểm tra trạng thái trong DB:
        // User 4 phải là 'LEFT'
        $u4Status = $pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = {$roomId} AND `user_id` = 4")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('LEFT', $u4Status);

        // User 1 phải là 'PENDING_DEPOSIT'
        $u1Status = $pdo->query("SELECT `status` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = {$roomId} AND `user_id` = 1")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('PENDING_DEPOSIT', $u1Status);

        // game_rooms.replacement_status phải là 'CLOSED'
        $rwStatus = $pdo->query("SELECT `replacement_status` FROM `game_rooms` WHERE `id` = {$roomId}")->fetchColumn();
        $this->assertSame('CLOSED', $rwStatus);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\NotificationApiTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class NotificationApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;
    protected NotificationRepository $notiRepo;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->notiRepo = new NotificationRepository($pdo);

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanListNotificationsAndMarkAsReadWithIDORGuard(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo 2 thông báo cho User 3
        $n1 = $this->notiRepo->createNotification('Phòng chơi mới', 'Phòng #1 đang mở tuyển thành viên', 'ROOM', null, [3]);
        $n2 = $this->notiRepo->createNotification('Lịch chơi', 'Đã có đề xuất lịch mới trong phòng bạn tham gia', 'SCHEDULE', null, [3]);

        // Tạo 1 thông báo cho User 4
        $n4 = $this->notiRepo->createNotification('Riêng tư User 4', 'Thông báo bí mật', 'SYSTEM', null, [4]);

        // 2. User 3 lấy danh sách thông báo
        $listReq = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/notifications',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $listRes = $this->app->dispatch($listReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $listRes->getStatusCode());

        $listData = json_decode($listRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($listData['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($listData['data']['notifications']);

        // Xác nhận thông báo của User 4 không xuất hiện trong danh sách của User 3
        foreach ($listData['data']['notifications'] as $item) {
            $this->assertSame(3, (int) $item['user_id']);
            $this->assertNotSame($n4, (int) $item['id']);
        }

        // 3. Chống IDOR: User 3 cố tình đánh dấu đã đọc thông báo của User 4 -> 404
        $idorReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/notifications/{$n4}/read",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $idorRes = $this->app->dispatch($idorReq);
        $this->assertSame(404, $idorRes->getStatusCode());

        // 4. User 3 đánh dấu đã đọc thông báo n1 của chính mình -> 200
        $readReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/notifications/{$n1}/read",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $readRes = $this->app->dispatch($readReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $readRes->getStatusCode());
        $readData = json_decode($readRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($readData['success']);

        // 5. User 3 filter unread_only=1 -> n1 không xuất hiện
        $unreadReq = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/notifications',
            queryParams: [
                'unread_only' => 1,
            ],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $unreadRes = $this->app->dispatch($unreadReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $unreadRes->getStatusCode());
        $unreadData = json_decode($unreadRes->getContent(), true);

        $unreadIds = array_column($unreadData['data']['notifications'], 'id');
        $this->assertNotContains($n1, $unreadIds);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: backend\tests\Feature\PaymentApiTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class PaymentApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;
    protected PaymentRepository $paymentRepo;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $this->paymentRepo = new PaymentRepository($pdo);

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanProcessDepositAndRemainingPaymentWithIdempotencyAndIDORGuard(): void
    {
        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();

        // 1. Tìm hoặc tạo room participant cho User 3 trong Room 1
        $pStmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT `id` FROM `room_participants` WHERE `game_room_id` = 1 AND `user_id` = 3 LIMIT 1');
        $pStmt->execute();
        $participantId = $pStmt->fetchColumn();

        if ($participantId === false) {
            $insPart = $pdo->prepare('INSERT INTO `room_participants` (`game_room_id`, `user_id`, `status`, `joined_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) VALUES (1, 3, \'PENDING_DEPOSIT\', NOW(), NOW(), NOW())');
            $insPart->execute();
            $participantId = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();
        } else {
            $participantId = (int) $participantId;
            $pdo->exec("UPDATE `room_participants` SET `status` = 'PENDING_DEPOSIT' WHERE `id` = {$participantId}");
        }

        // Dọn sạch payment cũ của participant này nếu có để đảm bảo test độc lập
        $pdo->exec("DELETE FROM `payment_transactions` WHERE `player_payment_id` IN (SELECT `id` FROM `player_payments` WHERE `room_participant_id` = {$participantId})");
        $pdo->exec("DELETE FROM `player_payments` WHERE `room_participant_id` = {$participantId}");

        // 2. Tạo payment record trị giá 300,000 VNĐ
        $paymentId = $this->paymentRepo->createPayment([
            'room_participant_id' => $participantId,
            'payment_type' => 'DEPOSIT',
            'amount' => 300000,
            'paid_amount' => 0,
            'status' => 'PENDING',
        ]);

        // 3. Test IDOR: User 4 thử thanh toán cho User 3 -> 403 Forbidden
        $idorReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/payments/{$paymentId}/deposit",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'amount' => 150000,
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4', // User 4 (không phải chủ payment)
            ]
        );
        $idorRes = $this->app->dispatch($idorReq);
        $this->assertSame(403, $idorRes->getStatusCode());
        $idorData = json_decode($idorRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($idorData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $idorData['error']['code']);

        // 4. User 3 thanh toán tiền cọc 150,000 VNĐ kèm Idempotency-Key
        $idempKey = 'DEP_TEST_' . uniqid();
        $depReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/payments/{$paymentId}/deposit",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'amount' => 150000,
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
                'X-Idempotency-Key' => $idempKey,
            ]
        );
        $depRes = $this->app->dispatch($depReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $depRes->getStatusCode());
        $depData = json_decode($depRes->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($depData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('PARTIALLY_PAID', $depData['data']['payment']['status']);
        $this->assertSame(150000, (int) $depData['data']['payment']['paid_amount']);
        $this->assertSame('SUCCESS', $depData['data']['transaction']['status']);

        // 5. Thử gửi lại đúng request với Idempotency-Key cũ -> Trả về kết quả idempotent cũ
        $repeatRes = $this->app->dispatch($depReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $repeatRes->getStatusCode());
        $repeatData = json_decode($repeatRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($repeatData['success']);
        $this->assertSame($depData['data']['transaction']['id'], $repeatData['data']['transaction']['id']);

        // 6. User 3 thanh toán nốt số tiền còn lại (150,000 VNĐ)
        $remReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/payments/{$paymentId}/remaining",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $remRes = $this->app->dispatch($remReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $remRes->getStatusCode());
        $remData = json_decode($remRes->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($remData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('PAID', $remData['data']['payment']['status']);
        $this->assertSame(300000, (int) $remData['data']['payment']['paid_amount']);

        // 7. Cố tình thanh toán tiếp khi đã thanh toán đủ -> Báo lỗi 400
        $extraRes = $this->app->dispatch($remReq);
        $this->assertSame(400, $extraRes->getStatusCode());
        $extraData = json_decode($extraRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($extraData['success']);
        $this->assertSame('PAYMENT_ALREADY_COMPLETED', $extraData['error']['code']);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\PricingCalculationApiTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class PricingCalculationApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testUnauthenticatedUserCannotCalculatePrice(): void
    {
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/rooms/1/calculate-price',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'play_time' => '2026-11-25 14:00:00',
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(401, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('UNAUTHORIZED', $data['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testCanCalculateRoomPriceWithFixedDepositAndTimeSlot(): void
    {
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 3;

        // Tính giá cho Room 1 vào khung giờ ban ngày (14:00)
        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/rooms/1/calculate-price',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'play_time' => '2026-11-25 14:00:00', // Thứ 4, 14h chiều
            ]
        );

        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $calc = $data['data'];

        // Cọc phải đúng 50.000 VNĐ / người
        $this->assertSame(50000, $calc['deposit_per_person']);
        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, $calc['total_participants']);
        $this->assertSame(50000 * $calc['total_participants'], $calc['deposit_total']);
        $this->assertSame($calc['subtotal'] - $calc['discount_amount'], $calc['final_amount']);
        $this->assertSame($calc['final_amount'] - $calc['deposit_total'], $calc['remaining_total']);
    }

    public function testTuesdayPromotionOnlyAppliesOnTuesday(): void
    {
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 3;

        // 1. Ngày Thứ Ba: 2026-11-24
        $tueReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/rooms/1/calculate-price',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'play_time' => '2026-11-24 15:00:00', // Thứ Ba
                'promotion_code' => 'TUESDAY_30',
            ]
        );
        $tueRes = $this->app->dispatch($tueReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $tueRes->getStatusCode());
        $tueData = json_decode($tueRes->getContent(), true)['data'];

        // Phải được giảm giá > 0 và có applied_promotion
        $this->assertNotNull($tueData['applied_promotion']);
        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, $tueData['discount_amount']);

        // 2. Ngày Thứ Tư: 2026-11-25 (không áp dụng được TUESDAY_30)
        $wedReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/rooms/1/calculate-price',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'play_time' => '2026-11-25 15:00:00', // Thứ Tư
                'promotion_code' => 'TUESDAY_30',
            ]
        );
        $wedRes = $this->app->dispatch($wedReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $wedRes->getStatusCode());
        $wedData = json_decode($wedRes->getContent(), true)['data'];

        // Không được giảm giá
        $this->assertNull($wedData['applied_promotion']);
        $this->assertSame(0, $wedData['discount_amount']);
    }

    public function testCanGetPriceSnapshotAfterCalculation(): void
    {
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 3;

        // Gọi calculate trước
        $calcReq = new Request('POST', '/api/v1/rooms/1/calculate-price', [], ['play_time' => '2026-11-24 15:00:00']);
        $this->app->dispatch($calcReq);

        // Lấy snapshot
        $snapReq = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/rooms/1/price-snapshot');
        $snapRes = $this->app->dispatch($snapReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $snapRes->getStatusCode());

        $snapData = json_decode($snapRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($snapData['success']);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $snapData['data']['snapshot']['id']);
        $this->assertNotNull($snapData['data']['snapshot']['price_calculated_at']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($snapData['data']['snapshot']['price_details']);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\SchedulingApiTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class SchedulingApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanListVenuesAndPhysicalRooms(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/venues');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['venues']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['venues'][0]['rooms']);
    }

    public function testCanProposeAndVoteSchedule(): void
    {
        // 1. Tạo phòng mới với User 3 (Nguyễn Xuân Hiếu)
        $createReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/rooms',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'game_id' => 1,
                'title' => 'Phòng test biểu quyết lịch',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $createRes = $this->app->dispatch($createReq);
        $this->assertSame(201, $createRes->getStatusCode());
        $createData = json_decode($createRes->getContent(), true);
        $roomId = (int) $createData['data']['room']['id'];

        // 2. User 4 (Trần Minh Anh) tham gia phòng
        $joinReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/rooms/{$roomId}/join",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4',
            ]
        );
        $joinRes = $this->app->dispatch($joinReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $joinRes->getStatusCode());

        // 3. User 3 tạo đề xuất lịch
        $start = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+30 days 10:00:00'));
        $end = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', strtotime('+30 days 12:00:00'));

        $propReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/rooms/{$roomId}/proposals",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'proposed_start_at' => $start,
                'proposed_end_at' => $end,
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 3',
            ]
        );
        $propRes = $this->app->dispatch($propReq);
        $this->assertSame(201, $propRes->getStatusCode());
        $propData = json_decode($propRes->getContent(), true);
        $proposalId = (int) $propData['data']['proposal_id'];

        // 4. Xem chi tiết đề xuất lịch
        $showReq = new Request('GET', "/api/proposals/{$proposalId}");
        $showRes = $this->app->dispatch($showReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $showRes->getStatusCode());
        $showData = json_decode($showRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertSame('PROPOSED', $showData['data']['proposal']['status']);
        $this->assertCount(1, $showData['data']['proposal']['votes']); // User 3 tự động vote ACCEPTED

        // 5. User 4 vote ACCEPTED -> Đủ 2/2 phiếu -> Tự động chốt Session!
        $voteReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/proposals/{$proposalId}/vote",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [
                'response' => 'ACCEPTED',
            ],
            headers: [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer 4',
            ]
        );
        $voteRes = $this->app->dispatch($voteReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $voteRes->getStatusCode());
        $voteData = json_decode($voteRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($voteData['success']);
        $this->assertTrue($voteData['data']['proposal_confirmed']);
        $this->assertNotNull($voteData['data']['session_id']);
    }
}

FILE: backend\tests\Feature\SecurityMiddlewareTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Middleware\AuthMiddleware;
use App\Core\Middleware\CsrfMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class SecurityMiddlewareTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testCanRetrieveCsrfToken(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/auth/csrf-token');
        $response = $this->app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['data']['csrf_token']);
        $this->assertSame($_SESSION['_csrf_token'], $data['data']['csrf_token']);
    }

    public function testCsrfMiddlewareBlocksStateChangeWithoutValidTokenInProduction(): void
    {
        // Add CsrfMiddleware to pipeline
        $app = new Application(dirname(__DIR__, 2));
        $app->addMiddleware(new CsrfMiddleware());

        $app->getRouter()->post('/api/v1/test-mutation', function (Request $req) {
            return Response::success(['ok' => true]);
        });

        // Simulating non-testing request without header/token
        Config::set('APP_ENV', 'production');

        $request = new Request('POST', '/api/v1/test-mutation');
        $response = $app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(403, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertFalse($data['success']);
        $this->assertSame('CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID', $data['error']['code']);

        // Reset config
        Config::set('APP_ENV', 'testing');
    }

    public function testCsrfMiddlewareAllowsValidToken(): void
    {
        $app = new Application(dirname(__DIR__, 2));
        $app->addMiddleware(new CsrfMiddleware());

        $app->getRouter()->post('/api/v1/test-mutation', function (Request $req) {
            return Response::success(['ok' => true]);
        });

        Config::set('APP_ENV', 'production');
        $validToken = CsrfMiddleware::getToken();

        $request = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/test-mutation',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: [
                'X-CSRF-Token' => $validToken,
            ]
        );
        $response = $app->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $data = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($data['success']);

        Config::set('APP_ENV', 'testing');
    }

    public function testAuthMiddlewareRejectsUnauthorizedAccess(): void
    {
        $req = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/protected');
        $res = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($req);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Response::class, $res);
        $this->assertSame(401, $res->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($res->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertSame('UNAUTHORIZED', $data['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testAuthMiddlewareRejectsForbiddenRole(): void
    {
        // User 3 is PLAYER
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 3;

        $req = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/admin-only');
        $res = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($req, ['ADMIN']);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Response::class, $res);
        $this->assertSame(403, $res->getStatusCode());

        $data = json_decode($res->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $data['error']['code']);
    }

    public function testAuthMiddlewareAllowsAuthorizedRole(): void
    {
        // User 1 is ADMIN
        $_SESSION['user_id'] = 1;

        $req = new Request('GET', '/api/v1/admin-only');
        $res = AuthMiddleware::authenticate($req, ['ADMIN']);

        $this->assertIsArray($res);
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $res['id']);
        $this->assertSame('ADMIN', strtoupper((string) $res['role_code']));
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Core\Application;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class Stage3ApiTest extends TestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);
        $this->app = new Application($basePath);
        $_SESSION = [];

        $routesFile = $basePath . '/routes/api.php';
        $router = $this->app->getRouter();
        require $routesFile;
    }

    public function testAdminAssignAndRevokeDmAndListAuditLogs(): void
    {
        $assignReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/rooms/1/assign-dm',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: ['dm_user_id' => 2],
            headers: ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']
        );
        $assignRes = $this->app->dispatch($assignReq);
        $this->assertSame(201, $assignRes->getStatusCode());
        $assignData = json_decode($assignRes->getContent(), true);
        $assignmentId = (int) $assignData['data']['assignment_id'];

        $revokeReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: "/api/v1/dm/assignments/{$assignmentId}/revoke",
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: ['reason' => 'Đổi ca phân công cho DM khác'],
            headers: ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']
        );
        $revokeRes = $this->app->dispatch($revokeReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $revokeRes->getStatusCode());

        $auditReq = new Request(
            method: 'GET',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/audit-logs',
            queryParams: ['page' => 1, 'per_page' => 10],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']
        );
        $auditRes = $this->app->dispatch($auditReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $auditRes->getStatusCode());
        $auditData = json_decode($auditRes->getContent(), true);
        $this->assertTrue($auditData['success']);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($auditData['data']);
    }

    public function testStudentVerificationAndNotificationsReadAll(): void
    {
        $verifyReq = new Request(
            method: 'PUT',
            path: '/api/v1/admin/users/3/student-verification',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: ['student_verified' => true],
            headers: ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 1']
        );
        $verifyRes = $this->app->dispatch($verifyReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $verifyRes->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertTrue(json_decode($verifyRes->getContent(), true)['data']['student_verified']);

        $pdo = DatabaseConnection::getInstance();
        $user = $pdo->query('SELECT `student_verified` FROM `users` WHERE `id` = 3')->fetch();
        $this->assertSame(1, (int) $user['student_verified']);

        $readAllReq = new Request(
            method: 'POST',
            path: '/api/v1/notifications/read-all',
            queryParams: [],
            bodyParams: [],
            headers: ['Authorization' => 'Bearer 3']
        );
        $readAllRes = $this->app->dispatch($readAllReq);
        $this->assertSame(200, $readAllRes->getStatusCode());
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Integration;

use App\Features\Booking\Repositories\BookingRepository;
use App\Features\DMManagement\Repositories\DMRepository;
use App\Features\GameCatalog\Repositories\GameRepository;
use App\Features\GameroomManagement\Repositories\GameRoomRepository;
use App\Features\Identity\Repositories\UserRepository;
use App\Features\Notification\Repositories\NotificationRepository;
use App\Features\PaymentRefund\Repositories\PaymentRepository;
use App\Features\Pricing\Repositories\PricingRepository;
use App\Features\Promotion\Repositories\PromotionRepository;
use App\Features\VenueScheduling\Repositories\SchedulingRepository;
use App\Infrastructure\Config\Config;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\ConnectionFactory;
use App\Infrastructure\Database\DatabaseConnection;
use PDO;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class RepositoryIntegrationTest extends TestCase
{
    private static PDO $pdo;

    public static function setUpBeforeClass(): void
    {
        $basePath = dirname(__DIR__, 2);
        Config::load($basePath);

        $host = Config::get('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1');
        $port = (int) Config::get('DB_PORT', 3306);
        $db = Config::get('DB_DATABASE', 'kich_ban_sat_v3');
        $user = Config::get('DB_USERNAME', 'root');
        $pass = Config::get('DB_PASSWORD', '');

        self::$pdo = new PDO("mysql:host={$host};port={$port};dbname={$db};charset=utf8mb4", $user, $pass, [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false,
        ]);

        DatabaseConnection::setInstance(self::$pdo);
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        // Clean test data after tests
        self::$pdo->exec('SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0');
        self::$pdo->exec("DELETE FROM `users` WHERE `email` LIKE '%@test-repo.local'");
        self::$pdo->exec("DELETE FROM `games` WHERE `slug` LIKE 'test-repo-%'");
        self::$pdo->exec("DELETE FROM `promotions` WHERE `code` LIKE 'TEST_PROMO_%'");
        self::$pdo->exec('SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1');
        parent::tearDown();
    }

    public function testUserRepositoryCanCreateAndFindUser(): void
    {
        $repo = new UserRepository(self::$pdo);
        $email = 'repo_user_' . uniqid() . '@test-repo.local';

        $userId = $repo->create([
            'name' => 'Test User',
            'email' => $email,
            'password_hash' => password_hash('SecretPassword123!', PASSWORD_DEFAULT),
            'phone' => '0987654321',
        ]);

        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, $userId);

        $found = $repo->findById($userId);
        $this->assertNotNull($found);
        $this->assertSame($email, $found['email']);
        $this->assertSame('Test User', $found['name']);
        $this->assertSame('ACTIVE', $found['status']);

        $byEmail = $repo->findByEmail($email);
        $this->assertNotNull($byEmail);
        $this->assertSame($userId, (int) $byEmail['id']);
    }

    public function testGameRepositoryCanFindGame(): void
    {
        $repo = new GameRepository(self::$pdo);
        $slug = 'test-repo-game-' . uniqid();

        $stmt = self::$pdo->prepare(
            'INSERT INTO `games` (`name`, `slug`, `description`, `min_players`, `max_players`, `duration_minutes`, `status`)
             VALUES (:name, :slug, :desc, 4, 8, 240, \'ACTIVE\')'
        );
        $stmt->execute([
            ':name' => 'Test Repo Game',
            ':slug' => $slug,
            ':desc' => 'Test Description',
        ]);
        $gameId = (int) self::$pdo->lastInsertId();

        $found = $repo->findById($gameId);
        $this->assertNotNull($found);
        $this->assertSame($slug, $found['slug']);

        $bySlug = $repo->findBySlug($slug);
        $this->assertNotNull($bySlug);
        $this->assertSame($gameId, (int) $bySlug['id']);

        $activeGames = $repo->listActive(50, 0);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($activeGames);
    }

    public function testPromotionRepositoryCanFindPromotion(): void
    {
        $repo = new PromotionRepository(self::$pdo);
        $code = 'TEST_PROMO_' . strtoupper(uniqid());

        $stmt = self::$pdo->prepare(
            'INSERT INTO `promotions` (`code`, `name`, `promotion_type`, `discount_type`, `discount_value`, `is_active`, `start_at`, `end_at`)
             VALUES (:code, :name, \'SPECIAL\', :type, :val, 1, NOW() - INTERVAL 1 DAY, NOW() + INTERVAL 7 DAY)'
        );
        $stmt->execute([
            ':code' => $code,
            ':name' => 'Special Test Discount',
            ':type' => 'FIXED',
            ':val' => 20000,
        ]);
        $promoId = (int) self::$pdo->lastInsertId();

        $found = $repo->findByCode($code);
        $this->assertNotNull($found);
        $this->assertSame($code, $found['code']);
        $this->assertSame(20000, (int) $found['discount_value']);

        $byId = $repo->findById($promoId);
        $this->assertNotNull($byId);
        $this->assertSame($code, $byId['code']);
    }

    public function testBookingRepositoryOverlapDetection(): void
    {
        $repo = new BookingRepository(self::$pdo);

        $isBooked = $repo->isPhysicalRoomBooked(999999, '2026-11-20', '09:00:00', '12:00:00');
        $this->assertFalse($isBooked);
    }

    public function testNotificationRepositoryCanCreateAndFetchUnread(): void
    {
        $userRepo = new UserRepository(self::$pdo);
        $userId = $userRepo->create([
            'name' => 'Notification Recipient',
            'email' => 'noti_' . uniqid() . '@test-repo.local',
            'password_hash' => password_hash('Pass123!', PASSWORD_DEFAULT),
        ]);

        $repo = new NotificationRepository(self::$pdo);
        $notiId = $repo->createNotification(
            title: 'Welcome to Kịch Bản Sát',
            message: 'Your account has been verified successfully.',
            notificationType: 'ROOM_EVENT',
            senderId: null,
            recipientUserIds: [$userId]
        );

        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, $notiId);

        $unread = $repo->getUnreadByUserId($userId);
        $this->assertNotEmpty($unread);
        $this->assertSame('Welcome to Kịch Bản Sát', $unread[0]['title']);
    }

============================================================
FILE: backend\tests\Unit\CorsMiddlewareTest.php
============================================================
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit;

use App\Core\Middleware\CorsMiddleware;
use App\Core\Request;
use App\Core\Response;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class CorsMiddlewareTest extends TestCase
{
    public function testExplicitAllowedOriginSetsCorsHeaders(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000', 'http://localhost:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/health', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://127.0.0.1:3000']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return Response::json(['status' => 'ok']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertSame('http://127.0.0.1:3000', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        $this->assertSame('true', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Credentials']);
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }

    public function testUnauthorizedOriginReceivesNoCorsAccess(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/health', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://evil-attacker.com']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return Response::json(['status' => 'ok']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', $headers);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', $headers);
        // Vary: Origin must still be present to prevent HTTP cache poisoning
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }

    public function testRequestWithoutOriginHeaderReceivesVaryOriginForCacheSafety(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/health', [], [], []); // No Origin header

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return Response::json(['status' => 'ok']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', $headers);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', $headers);
        // RFC 7234: Cache safety requires Vary: Origin even when Origin is absent
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }

    public function testMultipleAllowedOriginsVaryCorrectlyAcrossRequests(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000', 'http://localhost:3000'], true);

        // Request 1 from first allowed origin
        $req1 = new Request('GET', '/api/rooms', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://127.0.0.1:3000']);
        $res1 = $middleware->handle($req1, fn() => Response::json([]));
        $this->assertSame('http://127.0.0.1:3000', $res1->getHeaders()['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $res1->getHeaders()['Vary']);

        // Request 2 from second allowed origin
        $req2 = new Request('GET', '/api/rooms', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://localhost:3000']);
        $res2 = $middleware->handle($req2, fn() => Response::json([]));
        $this->assertSame('http://localhost:3000', $res2->getHeaders()['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $res2->getHeaders()['Vary']);

        // Request 3 from disallowed origin
        $req3 = new Request('GET', '/api/rooms', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://unauthorized.org']);
        $res3 = $middleware->handle($req3, fn() => Response::json([]));
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', $res3->getHeaders());
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $res3->getHeaders()['Vary']);
    }

    public function testWildcardOriginWithoutCredentialsReturnsLiteralAsterisk(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['*'], false);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/public', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://some-client.org']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return Response::json(['public' => true]);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        // Must return literal '*', never reflecting arbitrary incoming origin
        $this->assertSame('*', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', $headers);
    }

    public function testWildcardWithCredentialsFailsSafelyWithoutAllowingCredentials(): void
    {
        // Even if config attempts to enable credentials with wildcard '*', middleware must fail safe
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['*'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/data', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://untrusted-site.com']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return Response::json(['data' => 'test']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertSame('*', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        // Crucial security invariant: credentials must NEVER be asserted alongside wildcard
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', $headers);
    }

    public function testOptionsPreflightReturns204WithAllowedHeaders(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('OPTIONS', '/api/rooms', [], [], [
            'Origin' => 'http://127.0.0.1:3000',
            'Access-Control-Request-Method' => 'POST',
        ]);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function () {
            $this->fail('Pipeline should terminate on preflight OPTIONS');
        });

        $this->assertSame(204, $response->getStatusCode());
        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertSame('http://127.0.0.1:3000', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin']);
        $this->assertSame('true', $headers['Access-Control-Allow-Credentials']);
        $this->assertArrayHasKey('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', $headers);
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }

    public function testPreservesExistingVaryHeaderValues(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/compressed', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://127.0.0.1:3000']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return new Response('compressed content', 200, ['Vary' => 'Accept-Encoding']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertSame('Accept-Encoding, Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }

    public function testDoesNotDuplicateVaryOriginIfAlreadyPresent(): void
    {
        $middleware = new CorsMiddleware(['http://127.0.0.1:3000'], true);
        $request = new Request('GET', '/api/test', [], [], ['Origin' => 'http://127.0.0.1:3000']);

        $response = $middleware->handle($request, function (Request $req) {
            return new Response('body', 200, ['Vary' => 'Origin']);
        });

        $headers = $response->getHeaders();
        $this->assertSame('Origin', $headers['Vary']);
    }
}
